    PROCEDURE ConnectionTest@1000000000() Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      Data@1000000000 : Text[30];

    PROCEDURE IdentifyServer@1000000063();
    VAR
      URLString@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      Servername@1000000001 : Text[50];
      i@1000000002 : Integer;
      text001@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=Ihre lokalen Einstellungen haben sich ge�ndert oder Sie versuchen den Webshop von einem anderen System aus zu aktualisieren. Setzen Sie die Systemeigenschaften in der Webshop Einrichtung zur�ck oder kontaktieren Sie Ihren Systemadministrator.';
    BEGIN
      // Sicherheitsfunktion vor dem Verbinden

      IF NOT ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        URLString := CONTEXTURL;
        i := STRPOS(URLString, 'servername=');
        URLString := DELSTR(URLString,1,i+10);
        i := STRPOS(URLString,'&');
        Servername := COPYSTR(DELSTR(URLString,i),1,50);

        IF (WebshopSetup.Servername <> '') AND (WebshopSetup.Companyname <> '') THEN BEGIN
          IF (Servername <> WebshopSetup.Servername) OR (COMPANYNAME <> WebshopSetup.Companyname) THEN
            ERROR(text001);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopSetup.Servername := Servername;
          WebshopSetup.Companyname := COMPANYNAME;
          WebshopSetup.MODIFY;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE AddVar@1000000004(VAR Data@1000000000 : Text[1024];VarName@1000000001 : Text[100];VarValue@1000000002 : Variant);
    VAR
      VarValueInteger@1000000003 : Integer;
      VarValueDecimal@1000000004 : Decimal;
      VarValueText@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      VarValueDate@1000000006 : Date;
      VarValueDateTime@1000000007 : DateTime;
      VarValueTime@1000000008 : Time;
      VarValueBoolean@1000000009 : Boolean;
      TempStr@1000000010 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Variable einem zu sendenden Datensatz hinzuf�gen

      IF VarValue.ISBOOLEAN THEN BEGIN
        VarValueBoolean := VarValue;
        IF VarValueBoolean THEN
          VarValueText := '1'
        ELSE
          VarValueText := '0';
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + VarValueText + '&';
      END;

      IF VarValue.ISCODE OR VarValue.ISTEXT THEN BEGIN
        VarValueText := VarValue;
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + URLEncode(VarValueText) + '&';
      END;

      IF VarValue.ISINTEGER OR VarValue.ISOPTION THEN BEGIN
        VarValueInteger := VarValue;
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + FORMAT(VarValueInteger) + '&';
      END;

      IF VarValue.ISDECIMAL THEN BEGIN
        VarValueDecimal := VarValue;
        TempStr := CONVERTSTR(FORMAT(VarValueDecimal,0,'<Precision,2:2><Standard Format,1>'),',','.');
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + TempStr + '&';
      END;

      IF VarValue.ISDATE THEN BEGIN
        VarValueDate := VarValue;
        IF VarValueDate <> 0D THEN BEGIN
          VarValueText := FORMAT(VarValueDate,0,'<Year4>-<Month,2>-<Day,2>');
        END ELSE BEGIN
          VarValueText := 'NULL';
        END;
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + VarValueText + '&';
      END;

      IF VarValue.ISTIME THEN BEGIN
        VarValueTime := VarValue;
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + FORMAT(VarValueTime) + '&';
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE AddDTVar@1000000009(VAR Data@1000000000 : Text[1024];VarName@1000000001 : Text[100];VarValue@1000000002 : DateTime);
    VAR
      VarValueInteger@1000000003 : Integer;

    END;

    PROCEDURE SendRequest@1000000023(Action@1000000001 : Text[100];Data@1000000002 : Text[1024]) Response : Boolean;
    VAR
      Request@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      Error200@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Verbindung zum Webshop konnte nicht aufgebaut werden\Aktion: %1';
      Error300@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Keine Antwort vom Webshop erhalten\Aktion: %1';
      HTTPRequest@1000000006 : Automation "{F5078F18-C551-11D3-89B9-0000F81FE221} 6.0:{88D96A0B-F192-11D4-A65F-0040963251E5}:'Microsoft XML, v6.0'.ServerXMLHTTP60";
      ResponseText@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      InStreamResponse@1000000007 : InStream;
      OutStreamResponse@1000000014 : OutStream;
      BigTextResponse@1000000008 : BigText;
      LogFile@1000000009 : File;
      Error400@1000000013 : TextConst 'DEU=Fehlerhafte Antwort vom Webshop erhalten\Aktion: %1\Antwort: %2';
      Log001@1000000010 : TextConst 'DEU=### SendRequest: %1 - %2 ###';
      Log002@1000000011 : TextConst 'DEU="--> "';
      Log003@1000000012 : TextConst 'DEU="<-- "';
      LogResponse@1000000016 : Text[1024];
      i@1000000015 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // Datensatz senden (Antwort TRUE/FALSE)

      WebshopSetup.GET;
      IF WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        Request := WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector" + 'navconnect.php?legacy=1&action=' + Action;
        IF WebshopSetup."Log Active" THEN BEGIN
          LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
          LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
          IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
            LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File")
          ELSE
            LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File");
          LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
          LogFile.WRITE(STRSUBSTNO(Log001,Action,CURRENTDATETIME));
          IF Data <> '' THEN
            LogFile.WRITE(Log002 + COPYSTR(Data,1,1020));
          LogFile.CLOSE;
        END;
        Data := Data + 'pass=' + WebshopSetup."HTTP Password";
        CREATE(HTTPRequest);
        HTTPRequest.setTimeouts(60000,60000,60000,60000);
        HTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,FALSE);
        IF (GUIALLOWED AND ISSERVICETIER AND WebshopSetup."RTC Use Proxy Server")
           OR (GUIALLOWED AND NOT ISSERVICETIER AND WebshopSetup."Client Use Proxy Server")
           OR (NOT GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."NAS Use Proxy Server") THEN BEGIN
          HTTPRequest.setProxy(2,WebshopSetup."Name Proxy Server" + ':' + WebshopSetup."Port Proxy Server");
        END;
        HTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
        HTTPRequest.send(Data);
        IF HTTPRequest.waitForResponse(60000) THEN BEGIN
          InStreamResponse := HTTPRequest.responseStream;
          BigTextResponse.READ(InStreamResponse);
          BigTextResponse.GETSUBTEXT(ResponseText,1,1000);
          ResponseText := ConvertText(ResponseText,FALSE);
          IF WebshopSetup."Log Active" THEN BEGIN
            LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
            LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
            IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
              LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File")
            ELSE
              LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File");
            LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
            i := 1;
            REPEAT
              BigTextResponse.GETSUBTEXT(LogResponse,i,1020);
              LogFile.WRITE(Log003 + LogResponse);
              i += 1020;
            UNTIL i > BigTextResponse.LENGTH;
            LogFile.WRITE('');
            LogFile.CLOSE;
          END;
          IF ResponseText = '100' THEN BEGIN
            Response := TRUE
          END ELSE BEGIN
            Response := FALSE;
            IF ResponseText <> '' THEN BEGIN
              HTTPRequest.abort();
              ERROR(Error400,Action,ResponseText)
            END ELSE BEGIN
              HTTPRequest.abort();
              ERROR(Error300,Action);
            END;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          HTTPRequest.abort();
          ERROR(Error200,Action);
          Response := FALSE;
        END;
        HTTPRequest.abort();
        CLEAR(HTTPRequest);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendXMLRequest@1000000011(Action@1000000001 : Text[100];XMLFileName@1000000000 : Text[1024]) Response : Boolean;
    VAR
      XMLDoc@1000000012 : Automation "{F5078F18-C551-11D3-89B9-0000F81FE221} 6.0:{88D96A05-F192-11D4-A65F-0040963251E5}:'Microsoft XML, v6.0'.DOMDocument60";
      WinHTTPRequest@1000000011 : Automation "{F5078F18-C551-11D3-89B9-0000F81FE221} 6.0:{88D96A0B-F192-11D4-A65F-0040963251E5}:'Microsoft XML, v6.0'.ServerXMLHTTP60";
      Node@1000000010 : Automation "{F5078F18-C551-11D3-89B9-0000F81FE221} 6.0:{2933BF80-7B36-11D2-B20E-00C04F983E60}:'Microsoft XML, v6.0'.IXMLDOMNode";
      StreamSupported@1000000009 : Boolean;
      Request@1000000008 : Text[1024];
      InS@1000000007 : InStream;
      Resp@1000000006 : Text[1024];
      NoofSign@1000000005 : Integer;
      ErrorFile@1000000004 : File;
      ErrorFileName@1000000003 : Text[500];
      ErrorText@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      Error200@1000000014 : TextConst 'DEU=Verbindung zum Webserver konnte nicht aufgebaut werden';
      Error300@1000000013 : TextConst 'DEU=Keine Antwort vom Webserver erhalten';
    BEGIN
      // XML senden (Antwort TRUE/FALSE)

      WebshopSetup.GET;
      IF WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector" <> '' THEN BEGIN

        StreamSupported := TRUE;
        ErrorFileName := TEMPORARYPATH + '\NavConnectError.log';

        Request := WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector" + 'navconnect.php?legacy=1&action=' + Action;
        CREATE(WinHTTPRequest);
        WinHTTPRequest.setTimeouts(60000,60000,60000,60000);
        WinHTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,FALSE);

        IF (GUIALLOWED AND ISSERVICETIER AND WebshopSetup."RTC Use Proxy Server")
           OR (GUIALLOWED AND NOT ISSERVICETIER AND WebshopSetup."Client Use Proxy Server")
           OR (NOT GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."NAS Use Proxy Server") THEN BEGIN
          WinHTTPRequest.setProxy(2,WebshopSetup."Name Proxy Server" + ':' + WebshopSetup."Port Proxy Server");
        END;

        WinHTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');

        CLEAR(XMLDoc);
        CREATE(XMLDoc);
        XMLDoc.async := FALSE;
        XMLDoc.load(XMLFileName);
        Node := XMLDoc.selectSingleNode('//file/pass');
        IF NOT ISCLEAR(Node) THEN
          Node.text(WebshopSetup."HTTP Password");

        WinHTTPRequest.send(XMLDoc);
        IF WinHTTPRequest.waitForResponse(60000) THEN BEGIN

          CLEAR(XMLDoc);

          IF (StreamSupported) THEN BEGIN
            InS := WinHTTPRequest.responseStream;
            NoofSign := InS.READ(Resp); //Read first 1024 Sign
          END ELSE BEGIN
            NoofSign := 0;
            Resp := WinHTTPRequest.responseText;
          END;

          IF Resp = '100' THEN BEGIN
            Response := TRUE
          END ELSE BEGIN
            Response := FALSE;

            IF Resp <> '' THEN BEGIN
              ErrorText := Resp;

              IF (StreamSupported) THEN BEGIN
                IF EXISTS(ErrorFileName) THEN
                  ERASE(ErrorFileName);
                ErrorFile.CREATE(ErrorFileName);
                ErrorFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                ErrorFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                WHILE (NoofSign > 0) DO BEGIN
                  ErrorFile.WRITE(Resp);
                  NoofSign := InS.READ(Resp);
                END;
                ErrorFile.CLOSE;
              END;

              ERROR(ErrorText);
            END ELSE
              ERROR(Error300);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          ERROR(Error200);
          Response := FALSE;
        END;
        CLEAR(WinHTTPRequest);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetRequest@1000000022(Action@1000000001 : Text[100];Data@1000000002 : Text[1024]) Response : Text[1024];
    VAR
      Request@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      HTTPRequest@1000000009 : Automation "{F5078F18-C551-11D3-89B9-0000F81FE221} 6.0:{88D96A0B-F192-11D4-A65F-0040963251E5}:'Microsoft XML, v6.0'.ServerXMLHTTP60";

    PROCEDURE CreateDocumentPDF@1000000070(Type@1000000016 : 'SalesOrder,SalesInvoice,SalesShipment,SalesCrMemoHeader';ShopCode@1000000004 : Code[10];LanguageCode@1000000007 : Code[10];DocumentNo@1000000001 : Code[20]) FileName : Text[100];
    VAR
      CurrLanguageId@1000000011 : Integer;
      CurrPrinterName@1000000012 : Text[250];
      Reportid@1000000014 : Integer;
      PrinterSelection@1000000013 : Record 78;
      Language@1000000009 : Record 5447302;
      NAVLanguage@1000000010 : Record 8;
      SalesHeader@1000000005 : Record 36;
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000002 : Record 112;
      SalesShipmentHeader@1000000008 : Record 110;
      NotDeletePrinterSelection@1000000015 : Boolean;
      i@1000000006 : Integer;
      bioPDF@1000000000 : Automation "{F6DDC304-D8FD-4D3E-B87C-6089279BCB69} 1.0:{946FA925-DEC3-4D35-AE0A-02B41FDE1F36}:'bioPDF'.PDFPrinterSettings";
      OK@1000000017 : Boolean;
      SalesCrMemoHeader@1000000003 : Record 114;
    BEGIN
      // PDF-Datei aus Beleg (Verkaufsauftrag, Verkaufsrechnung, Verkaufslieferung) erstellen
      IF Language.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode) AND (DocumentNo <> '') THEN BEGIN

        Language.TESTFIELD("PDF Temp File Path");

        // Systemsprache f�r mehrsprachige Belege umstellen
        NAVLanguage.GET(LanguageCode);
        IF NAVLanguage."Windows Language ID" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          CurrLanguageId := GLOBALLANGUAGE();
          GLOBALLANGUAGE(NAVLanguage."Windows Language ID");
        END;

        // Beleg ausw�hlen und Dateinamen festlegen
        CASE Type OF
          Type::SalesOrder: BEGIN
            Reportid := Language."Report PDF Order Confirmation";
            IF SalesHeader.GET(SalesHeader."Document Type"::Order,DocumentNo) THEN
              //FileName := FORMAT(SalesHeader."Document Type") + '_' + DocumentNo + '.pdf';
              FileName := DocumentNo + '.pdf';
          END;
          Type::SalesInvoice: BEGIN
            Reportid := Language."Report PDF Invoice";
            IF SalesInvoiceHeader.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              //FileName := SalesInvoiceHeader.TABLECAPTION + '_' + DocumentNo + '.pdf';
              FileName := DocumentNo + '.pdf';
          END;
          Type::SalesShipment: BEGIN
            Reportid := Language."Report PDF Shipment";
            IF SalesShipmentHeader.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              //FileName := SalesShipmentHeader.TABLECAPTION + '_' + DocumentNo + '.pdf';
              FileName := DocumentNo + '.pdf';
          END;
          Type::SalesCrMemoHeader: BEGIN
            Reportid := Language."Report PDF Cr.Memo";
            IF SalesCrMemoHeader.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              //FileName := SalesShipmentHeader.TABLECAPTION + '_' + DocumentNo + '.pdf';
              FileName := DocumentNo + '.pdf';
          END;
        END;

        IF FileName <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF EXISTS(Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName) THEN
            ERASE(Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName);

          IF ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
            // PDF drucken
            CASE Type OF
              Type::SalesOrder: BEGIN
                SalesHeader.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
                SalesHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesHeader."No.");
                OK := REPORT.SAVEASPDF(Reportid,Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName,SalesHeader);
              END;
              Type::SalesInvoice: BEGIN
                SalesInvoiceHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesInvoiceHeader."No.");
                OK := REPORT.SAVEASPDF(Reportid,Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName,SalesInvoiceHeader);
              END;
              Type::SalesShipment: BEGIN
                SalesShipmentHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesShipmentHeader."No.");
                OK := REPORT.SAVEASPDF(Reportid,Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName,SalesShipmentHeader);
              END;
              Type::SalesCrMemoHeader: BEGIN
                SalesCrMemoHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
                OK := REPORT.SAVEASPDF(Reportid,Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName,SalesCrMemoHeader);
              END;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF ISCLEAR(bioPDF) THEN
              CREATE(bioPDF);
            bioPDF.Init;
            bioPDF.SetValue('Output',Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName);
            bioPDF.SetValue('ConfirmOverwrite','no');
            bioPDF.SetValue('ShowSaveAS','never');
            bioPDF.SetValue('ShowSettings','never');
            bioPDF.SetValue('ShowPDF','no');
            bioPDF.SetValue('ShowProgress','no');
            bioPDF.SetValue('ShowProgressFinished','no');
            bioPDF.SetValue('DisableOptionDialog','yes');
            bioPDF.SetValue('SuppressErrors','yes');
            bioPDF.SetValue('Title',FileName);
            bioPDF.SetValue('Subject',FileName);
            IF (Language."PDF Background File" <> '') AND FILE.EXISTS(Language."PDF Background File") THEN
              bioPDF.SetValue('Superimpose',Language."PDF Background File");
            bioPDF.WriteSettings();

            // Standard-Drucker festlegen
            IF PrinterSelection.GET(USERID,Reportid) THEN BEGIN
              NotDeletePrinterSelection := TRUE;
              IF PrinterSelection."Printer Name" <> 'PDF Writer - bioPDF' THEN
                CurrPrinterName := PrinterSelection."Printer Name";
            END ELSE BEGIN
              PrinterSelection.INIT;
              PrinterSelection."User ID" := USERID;
              PrinterSelection."Report ID" := Reportid;
              PrinterSelection.INSERT;
            END;
            PrinterSelection."Printer Name" := 'PDF Writer - bioPDF';
            PrinterSelection.MODIFY;

            // PDF drucken
            CASE Type OF
              Type::SalesOrder: BEGIN
                SalesHeader.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
                SalesHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesHeader."No.");
                REPORT.RUN(Reportid,FALSE,FALSE,SalesHeader);
              END;
              Type::SalesInvoice: BEGIN
                SalesInvoiceHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesInvoiceHeader."No.");
                REPORT.RUN(Reportid,FALSE,FALSE,SalesInvoiceHeader);
              END;
              Type::SalesShipment: BEGIN
                SalesShipmentHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesShipmentHeader."No.");
                REPORT.RUN(Reportid,FALSE,FALSE,SalesShipmentHeader);
              END;
              Type::SalesCrMemoHeader: BEGIN
                SalesCrMemoHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesCrMemoHeader."No.");
                REPORT.RUN(Reportid,FALSE,FALSE,SalesShipmentHeader);
              END;

            END;
          END;
          IF (OK) OR (FILE.EXISTS(Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName)) THEN BEGIN
            // PDF auf Webserver laden
            i := 1;
            WHILE i < 100 DO BEGIN
              IF EXISTS(Language."PDF Temp File Path"+FileName) THEN BEGIN
                UploadAttachment(Language."PDF Temp File Path"+FileName,FileName);
                i := 100;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                SLEEP(500);
                i += 1;
              END;
            END;

            // Lokale PDF-Datei l�schen
            IF EXISTS(Language."PDF Temp File Path"+FileName) THEN
              ERASE(Language."PDF Temp File Path"+FileName);
          END;
          IF NOT ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
            // Standard-Drucker zur�cksetzten
            IF NotDeletePrinterSelection THEN BEGIN
              IF CurrPrinterName <> '' THEN BEGIN
                PrinterSelection."Printer Name" := CurrPrinterName;
                PrinterSelection.MODIFY;
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              PrinterSelection.DELETE;
            END;
          END;
          // Sprache zur�cksetzten
          IF CurrLanguageId <> 0 THEN
            GLOBALLANGUAGE(CurrLanguageId);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UploadAttachment@1000000067(Filename@1000000008 : Text[200];ServerFileName@1000000010 : Text[200]);
    VAR
      ConvertedFile@1000000001 : File;
      InputFileInStr@1000000002 : InStream;
      ConvertedFileOutStr@1000000004 : OutStream;
      ConvertedFileInStr@1000000003 : InStream;
      Data@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      i@1000000006 : Integer;
      TempData@1000000007 : Text[600];
      FileRecord@1000000009 : Text[1024];
      XMLHeader@1000000012 : Text[1024];
      TempFilePath@1000000011 : Text[200];
    BEGIN
      // Datei in tempor�res Webverzeichnis laden

      TempFilePath := TEMPORARYPATH + '\' + 'nav_attachment_' + FORMAT(RANDOM(10000000)) + '.xml';
      ConvertedFile.CREATE(TempFilePath);

      ConvertedFile.CREATEOUTSTREAM(ConvertedFileOutStr);

      ConvertedFileOutStr.WRITETEXT('<?xml version="1.0" ?><file><pass></pass>');
      ConvertedFileOutStr.WRITETEXT('<action>write</action>');
      ConvertedFileOutStr.WRITETEXT('<filename>'+ServerFileName+'</filename>' + '<filedata>');

      Base64EncodeStream(InputFileInStr,ConvertedFileOutStr);

      ConvertedFileOutStr.WRITETEXT('</filedata></file>');
      ConvertedFile.CREATEINSTREAM(ConvertedFileInStr);
      ConvertedFile.CLOSE;

      CLEAR(FileRecord);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'filename',ServerFileName);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'action','create');
      SendRequest('upload_attachment',FileRecord);


      SendXMLRequest('upload_attachment',TempFilePath);

      CLEAR(FileRecord);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'filename',ServerFileName);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'action','convert');
      SendRequest('upload_attachment',FileRecord);

      IF EXISTS(TempFilePath) THEN
        ERASE(TempFilePath);
    END;

    PROCEDURE "--- Allgemein ---"@1000000008();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetItemLink@1000000074(WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304) Link : Text[250];
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      Shop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000004 : Record 5447302;
      Customer@1000000003 : Record 18;
      CustomerNo@1000000005 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      // Link auf einen Webshopartikel erzeugen

      WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
        Shop.GET("Shop Code");
        Language.GET("Shop Code","Language Code");
        Shop.TESTFIELD("Frontend Link");
        IF (Shop."Shop Typ" = Shop."Shop Typ"::b2b) OR (Shop."Shop Typ" = Shop."Shop Typ"::salesperson) THEN BEGIN
          Shop.TESTFIELD("Base Login Customer");
          Customer.GET(Shop."Base Login Customer");
          Customer.TESTFIELD("Webshop Active");
          CustomerNo := Customer."No.";
        END;
        WebshopItem.TESTFIELD("Last Date Updated");
        WebshopItem.TESTFIELD(Active);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',"Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',"Language Code");
        IF CustomerNo <> '' THEN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no',CustomerNo);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'item_no',"Item No.");
        Link := Shop."Frontend Link" + GetRequest('get_item_link',CurrRec);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertSalesLine@1000000041(SalesHeader@1000000001 : Record 36;VAR SalesLine@1000000009 : Record 37;Typ@1000000003 : 'Text,Item,G/L Account,Resource,Charge';No@1000000005 : Code[20];VariantCode@1000000007 : Code[10];Quantity@1000000006 : Decimal;UnitPrice@1000000008 : Decimal;Description@1000000002 : Text[50];IsCouponItem@1000000010 : Boolean;LineDiscount@1000000011 : Decimal;UnitOfMeasure@1000000012 : Code[10];ToShip@1000000015 : Boolean;ToInvoice@1000000014 : Boolean);
    VAR
      LastSalesLine@1000000000 : Record 37;
      NewLineNo@1000000004 : Integer;
      TransferExtendedText@1000000013 : Codeunit 378;
    BEGIN
      // Einf�gen einer Verkaufszeile

      LastSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
      LastSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesHeader."No.");
      IF LastSalesLine.FINDLAST THEN
        NewLineNo := LastSalesLine."Line No." + 10000
      ELSE
        NewLineNo := 10000;

      SalesLine.INIT;
      SalesLine.VALIDATE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
      SalesLine.VALIDATE("Document No.",SalesHeader."No.");
      SalesLine.VALIDATE("Line No.",NewLineNo);
      SalesLine.INSERT(TRUE);

      CASE Typ OF
        Typ::Text: BEGIN
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Type,SalesLine.Type::" ");
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("No.",No);
          IF Description <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,Description);
          IF TransferExtendedText.SalesCheckIfAnyExtText(SalesLine,TRUE) THEN
            TransferExtendedText.InsertSalesExtText(SalesLine);
        END;
        Typ::Item: BEGIN
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Type,SalesLine.Type::Item);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("No.",No);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit of Measure",UnitOfMeasure);
          IF Description <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,Description);
          IF VariantCode <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Variant Code",VariantCode);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Quantity,Quantity);
          //MB 2015.10.30 - Kampagnenmodul - Immer Wert aus Web nehmen!
          //IF UnitPrice <> 0 THEN
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit Price",UnitPrice);
          IF IsCouponItem=TRUE THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Line Discount %",100)
          ELSE IF LineDiscount > 0 THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Line Discount %",LineDiscount);
          IF ToInvoice THEN
            SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" := Quantity;
          IF ToShip THEN
            SalesLine."Qty. to Ship" := Quantity;
        END;
        Typ::"G/L Account": BEGIN
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Type,SalesLine.Type::"G/L Account");
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("No.",No);
          IF Description <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,Description);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Quantity,Quantity);
          IF UnitPrice <> 0 THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit Price",UnitPrice);
          IF ToInvoice THEN
            SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" := Quantity;
          IF ToShip THEN
            SalesLine."Qty. to Ship" := Quantity;
        END;
        Typ::Resource: BEGIN
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Type,SalesLine.Type::Resource);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("No.",No);
          IF Description <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,Description);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Quantity,Quantity);
          IF UnitPrice <> 0 THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit Price",UnitPrice);
          IF ToInvoice THEN
            SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" := Quantity;
          IF ToShip THEN
            SalesLine."Qty. to Ship" := Quantity;
        END;
        Typ::Charge: BEGIN
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Type,SalesLine.Type::"Charge (Item)");
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("No.",No);
          IF Description <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,Description);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Quantity,Quantity);
          IF UnitPrice <> 0 THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit Price",UnitPrice);
          IF ToInvoice THEN
            SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" := Quantity;
          IF ToShip THEN
            SalesLine."Qty. to Ship" := Quantity;
        END;
      END;

      SalesLine.MODIFY(TRUE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalculateOnlineDiscount@1000000052(SalesHeader@1000000000 : Record 36);
    VAR
      Shop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      ShopLanguage@1000000006 : Record 5447302;
      SalesLine@1000000001 : Record 37;
      StandardText@1000000003 : Record 7;
      OnlineDiscountAmount@1000000004 : Decimal;
      OrderAmount@1000000005 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      // Online-Rabatt berechnen
      {
      OnlineDiscountAmount := 0;
      OrderAmount := 0;
      WebshopSetup.GET;
      IF Shop.GET(SalesHeader."Webshop Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
        IF ShopLanguage.GET(SalesHeader."Webshop Shop Code",SalesHeader."Webshop Language Code") THEN BEGIN
          SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
          SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesHeader."No.");
          SalesLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLine.Type::Item);
          IF SalesLine.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            IF SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" <> 0 THEN
              OrderAmount += SalesLine."Line Amount" / SalesLine.Quantity * SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice";
          UNTIL SalesLine.NEXT = 0;
          SalesLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLine.Type::"G/L Account");
          SalesLine.SETRANGE("No.",Shop."Online Discount");
          SalesLine.SETFILTER("Outstanding Quantity",'<>0');
          StandardText.GET(ShopLanguage."Online Discount Text");
          OnlineDiscountAmount := -ROUND(OrderAmount/100*Shop."Weborder Typ");
          IF SalesLine.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,Shop."Weborder Typ"));
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Quantity,1);
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit Price",OnlineDiscountAmount);
            SalesLine.MODIFY(TRUE);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF Shop."Weborder Typ" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
              InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,2,Shop."Online Discount",1,OnlineDiscountAmount,
                              STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,Shop."Weborder Typ"));
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      }
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetWebshopItemModified@1000000061(ItemNo@1000000000 : Code[20];UpdateAllWebshopItem@1000000006 : Boolean;UpdateParentItem@1000000004 : Boolean;UpdateVariants@1000000005 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000001 : Record 5447304;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000002 : Record 5447308;
      WebshopItem2@1000000003 : Record 5447304;
      WebshopItem3@1000000007 : Record 5447304;
    BEGIN
      // �nderungsdatum f�r Artikelaktualisierung setzten

      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.",ItemNo);

      IF NOT UpdateAllWebshopItem THEN BEGIN
        IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code",WebshopItem."Shop Code");
          WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code",WebshopItem."Language Code");
        END;
      END;

      IF WebshopItem.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
        WebshopItem3.GET(WebshopItem."Shop Code",WebshopItem."Language Code",WebshopItem."Item No.");
        WebshopItem3."Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME();
        WebshopItem3."Last User Modified" := USERID;
        WebshopItem3.MODIFY;

        // Zugeh�rige Varianten Aktualisieren
        IF UpdateVariants THEN BEGIN
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.",WebshopItem."Item No.");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",WebshopItem."Shop Code");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,WebshopItemLink.Typ::Variant);
          IF WebshopItemLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            WebshopItem2.SETRANGE("Shop Code",WebshopItemLink."Shop Code");
            WebshopItem2.SETRANGE("Item No.",WebshopItemLink."Linked Item No.");
            IF WebshopItem2.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
              WebshopItem2."Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME();
              WebshopItem2."Last User Modified" := USERID;
              WebshopItem2.MODIFY;
            UNTIL WebshopItem2.NEXT = 0;
          UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;
        END;

        // Zugeh�rigen Mutterartikel aktualisieren
        IF UpdateParentItem THEN BEGIN
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,WebshopItemLink.Typ::Variant);
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",WebshopItem."Shop Code");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Linked Item No.",WebshopItem."Item No.");
          IF WebshopItemLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            WebshopItem2.SETRANGE("Shop Code",WebshopItemLink."Shop Code");
            WebshopItem2.SETRANGE("Item No.",WebshopItemLink."Item No.");
            IF WebshopItem2.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
              WebshopItem2."Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME();
              WebshopItem2."Last User Modified" := USERID;
              WebshopItem2.MODIFY;
            UNTIL WebshopItem2.NEXT = 0;
          UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;
        END;

      UNTIL WebshopItem.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetWebshopCategoryModified@1000000015(ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[20];LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[20];LineNo@1000000002 : Integer);
    VAR

    PROCEDURE GetNextWorkday@1000000021(Date@1000000001 : Date;Direction@1000000000 : '>,<') NewDate : Date;
    VAR
      DateFormulaString@1000000002 : Text[10];
      CompanyInfo@1000000003 : Record 79;
      CalendarManagement@1000000004 : Codeunit 7600;
      NonWorkday@1000000005 : Boolean;
      DateDscr@1000000006 : Text[30];
      DateFormula@1000000007 : DateFormula;
    BEGIN
      //Gibt n�chsten oder letzen Werktag von Referenzdatum aus zur�ck
      NewDate := Date;

      IF Direction = Direction::">" THEN
        DateFormulaString := '<+1D>'
      ELSE
        DateFormulaString := '<-1D>';

      EVALUATE(DateFormula,DateFormulaString);

      CompanyInfo.GET;
      NonWorkday := CalendarManagement.CheckDateStatus(CompanyInfo."Base Calendar Code",NewDate,DateDscr);
      WHILE NonWorkday DO BEGIN
        NewDate := CALCDATE(DateFormula,NewDate);
        NonWorkday := CalendarManagement.CheckDateStatus(CompanyInfo."Base Calendar Code",NewDate,DateDscr);
      END;
      EXIT(NewDate);
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorOpen@1000000014(Title@1000 : Text[250]);
    VAR
      MaxTextLength@1001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(MainTableCount);
        NoOfMainTable := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord2 := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord3 := 0;
        NoOfRecords := 0;
        RecordNo := 0;
        NewProgress := 0;
        OldProgress := 0;
        NewProgress2 := 0;
        OldProgress2 := 0;
        NextRecordNo := 0;
        Title := DELCHR(Title,'>',' .');
        Title := DELCHR(Title,'<',' ');
        IF Title = '' THEN
          Title := Text55000;
        MaxTextLength := STRLEN(Text55001);
        IF STRLEN(Text55002) > MaxTextLength THEN
          MaxTextLength := STRLEN(Text55002);
        IF STRLEN(Text55003) > MaxTextLength THEN
          MaxTextLength := STRLEN(Text55003);
        IF STRLEN(Text55004) > MaxTextLength THEN
          MaxTextLength := STRLEN(Text55004);
        Window.OPEN(
          Title +'...\\' +
          PADSTR(Text55001,MaxTextLength,' ') + '  @1@@@@@@@@@@@@@\' +
          PADSTR(Text55002,MaxTextLength,' ') + '  #2#######################\' +
          PADSTR(Text55003,MaxTextLength,' ') + '  #3######\' +
          PADSTR(Text55004,MaxTextLength,' ') + '  @4@@@@@@@@@@@@@');
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorNewTable@75(TableCaption@1000 : Text[250];RecordCount@1001 : Integer);
    VAR
      i@1002 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        IF MainRecord THEN BEGIN
          NoOfMainRecord2 := NoOfMainRecord2 + NoOfMainRecord3;
          MainRecord := FALSE;
        END;
        Window.UPDATE(2,TableCaption);
        Window.UPDATE(3,RecordCount);
        Window.UPDATE(4,0);
        OldProgress := 0;
        RecordNo := 0;
        NoOfRecords := RecordCount;
        NextRecordNo := 0;
        IF (NoOfMainRecord <> 0) AND (RecordCount <> 0) THEN
          FOR i := 1 TO NoOfMainTable DO
            IF MainTableCaption[i] = TableCaption THEN BEGIN
              MainRecord := TRUE;
              NoOfMainRecord3 := MainTableCount[i];
              MainTableCaption[i] := '';
              EXIT;
            END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorUpdate@76();
    BEGIN
      IF GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        IF RecordNo >= NoOfRecords THEN
          EXIT;
        RecordNo := RecordNo + 1;
        IF RecordNo < NextRecordNo THEN
          EXIT;
        NextRecordNo := NextRecordNo + ROUND(NoOfRecords / 100,1);
        NewProgress := ROUND(RecordNo / NoOfRecords * 100,1);
        IF NewProgress <> OldProgress THEN BEGIN
          OldProgress := NewProgress;
          Window.UPDATE(4,NewProgress * 100);
          IF MainRecord THEN BEGIN
            NewProgress2 := ROUND((NoOfMainRecord2 + RecordNo) / NoOfMainRecord * 100,1);
            IF (NewProgress2 > OldProgress2) AND (NewProgress2 <= 100) THEN BEGIN
              OldProgress2 := NewProgress2;
              Window.UPDATE(1,NewProgress2 * 100);
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorRegisterTable@77(TableCaption@1000 : Text[250];RecordCount@1001 : Integer);
    BEGIN
      IF GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        NoOfMainTable := NoOfMainTable + 1;
        MainTableCaption[NoOfMainTable] := TableCaption;
        MainTableCount[NoOfMainTable] := RecordCount;
        NoOfMainRecord := NoOfMainRecord + RecordCount;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorClose@1();
    BEGIN
      IF GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        Window.CLOSE;
        CLEAR(MainTableCount);
        NoOfMainTable := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord2 := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord3 := 0;
        NoOfRecords := 0;
        RecordNo := 0;
        NewProgress := 0;
        OldProgress := 0;
        NewProgress2 := 0;
        OldProgress2 := 0;
        NextRecordNo := 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE "--- Stingkonvertierung ---"@1000000005();

    END;

    PROCEDURE Explode@1000000042(VAR TextRecord@1000000000 : Text[1024];Delimiter@1000000003 : Text[30]) TextField : Text[250];
    VAR
      Pos@1000000001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      Pos := STRPOS(TextRecord,Delimiter);
      IF Pos <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        TextField := (COPYSTR(TextRecord,1,Pos-1));
        TextRecord := (COPYSTR(TextRecord,Pos+STRLEN(Delimiter)));
      END ELSE BEGIN
        TextField := TextRecord;
        TextRecord := '';
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ExplodeByLenght@1000000025(VAR TextRecord@1000000000 : Text[1024];Pos@1000000001 : Integer) TextField : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      IF Pos < STRLEN(TextRecord) THEN BEGIN
        TextField := (COPYSTR(TextRecord,1,Pos-1));
        TextRecord := (COPYSTR(TextRecord,Pos));
      END ELSE BEGIN
        TextField := TextRecord;
        TextRecord := '';
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE URLEncode@1000000003(ASCIIText@1000000000 : Text[1024]) HTTPText : Text[1024];
    VAR
      Char@1000000001 : Char;
      CharInt@1000000002 : Integer;
      i@1000000003 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      CLEAR(HTTPText);
      ASCIIText := ConvertText(ASCIIText,TRUE);
      FOR i := 1 TO STRLEN(ASCIIText) DO BEGIN
        Char := ASCIIText[i];
        CharInt := Char;
        HTTPText := HTTPText + URLEncodeCharset(CharInt);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ConvertText@12(Text@1001 : Text[1024];DosToWindows@1004 : Boolean) ConvertedText : Text[1024];
    VAR
      Ascii@1003 : Text[191];
      Ansi@1000 : Text[191];
    BEGIN
      InitCharAscii(Ascii);
      InitCharAnsi(Ansi);

      IF DosToWindows THEN
        ConvertedText := CONVERTSTR(Text,Ascii,Ansi)
      ELSE
        ConvertedText := CONVERTSTR(Text,Ansi,Ascii);
    END;

    PROCEDURE Base64EncodeStream@1112400001(VAR inStr@1112400000 : InStream;VAR outStr@1112400001 : OutStream);
    VAR
      b@1112400002 : Binary[3];
      nRead@1112400003 : Integer;
      c@1112400004 : ARRAY [4] OF Char;
      nWritten@1112400005 : Integer;
      txt64Chars@1112400006 : Text[64];
    BEGIN
      txt64Chars := 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ';
      txt64Chars += 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
      txt64Chars += '0123456789+/';

      WHILE NOT inStr.EOS DO BEGIN
        // read up to 3 bytes...
        nRead := inStr.READ(b);

        // zero out bytes not read...
        IF nRead < 3 THEN
          b[3] := 0;
        IF nRead < 2 THEN
          b[2] := 0;

        // c[1] is high 6 bits of b[1]...
        c[1] := b[1] DIV 4;

        // c[2] is low 2 bits of b[1] and high 4 bits of b[2]...
        c[2] := ((b[1] MOD 4) * 16) + (b[2] DIV 16);

        // c[3] is low 4 bits of b[2] and high 2 bits of b[3]...
        c[3] := ((b[2] MOD 16) * 4) + (b[3] DIV 64);

        // c[4] is low 6 bits of b[3]...
        c[4] := b[3] MOD 64;

        // convert c[1..4] to the Base64Encoded char...
        c[1] := txt64Chars[c[1] + 1];
        c[2] := txt64Chars[c[2] + 1];
        IF nRead > 1 THEN
          c[3] := txt64Chars[c[3] + 1]
        ELSE
          c[3] := '=';
        IF nRead > 2 THEN
          c[4] := txt64Chars[c[4] + 1]
        ELSE
          c[4] := '=';

        // write 'em out...
        outStr.WRITE(c[1]);
        outStr.WRITE(c[2]);
        outStr.WRITE(c[3]);
        outStr.WRITE(c[4]);
        nWritten += 1;

        // write a cr/lf every 72 chars...
        IF nWritten = 18 THEN BEGIN
          c[1] := 13;
          outStr.WRITE(c[1]);
          c[1] := 10;
          outStr.WRITE(c[1]);
          nWritten := 0;
        END;
      END;

      // write the trailing cr/lf...
      IF nWritten <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        c[1] := 13;
        outStr.WRITE(c[1]);
        c[1] := 10;
        outStr.WRITE(c[1]);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE Base64DecodeStream@1112400002(VAR inStr@1112400001 : InStream;VAR outStr@1112400000 : OutStream);
    VAR
      b@1112400002 : Binary[3];
      c@1112400003 : ARRAY [4] OF Char;
      idxChar@1112400004 : Integer;
      inPos@1112400005 : Integer;
      nEqualSigns@1112400006 : Integer;
      txt64Chars@1112400007 : Text[64];
      Text000@1112400008 : TextConst 'DEU=Ung�ltiges Zeichen "%1" an Position %2 festgestellt.;ENU=Invalid character ''''%1'''' encountered at position %2.';
      Text001@1112400009 : TextConst 'DEU=Unerwarteten End-Of-Stream an Position %1 festgestellt.;ENU=Unexpected end-of-stream encountered at position %1.';
    BEGIN
      txt64Chars := 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ';
      txt64Chars += 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
      txt64Chars += '0123456789+/';

      inPos := 0;
      WHILE NOT inStr.EOS DO BEGIN
        // read 1st char (ignore leading white space)...
        inStr.READ(c[1],1);
        inPos += 1;
        WHILE (c[1] IN [9, 10, 13, 32]) AND (NOT inStr.EOS) DO BEGIN
          inStr.READ(c[1],1);
          inPos += 1;
        END;

        IF NOT inStr.EOS THEN BEGIN
          // check 1st char...
          idxChar := STRPOS(txt64Chars, FORMAT(c[1]));
          IF idxChar = 0 THEN
            ERROR(Text000, FORMAT(c[1]), inPos);
          c[1] := idxChar - 1;

          // get/check 2nd char...
          inStr.READ(c[2],1);
          inPos += 1;
          idxChar := STRPOS(txt64Chars, FORMAT(c[2]));
          IF idxChar = 0 THEN
            ERROR(Text000, FORMAT(c[2]), inPos);
          c[2] := idxChar - 1;

          // get/check 3rd char...
          IF inStr.EOS THEN
            ERROR(Text001, inPos);
          inStr.READ(c[3],1);
          inPos += 1;
          nEqualSigns := 0;
          IF c[3] = '=' THEN BEGIN
            c[3] := 0;
            nEqualSigns += 1;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            idxChar := STRPOS(txt64Chars, FORMAT(c[3]));
            IF idxChar = 0 THEN
              ERROR(Text000, FORMAT(c[3]), inPos);
            c[3] := idxChar - 1;
          END;

          // get/check 4th char...
          IF inStr.EOS THEN
            ERROR(Text001, inPos);
          inStr.READ(c[4],1);
          inPos += 1;
          IF (nEqualSigns > 0) AND (c[4] <> '=') THEN
            ERROR(Text000, FORMAT(c[4]), inPos);
          IF c[4] = '=' THEN BEGIN
            c[4] := 0;
            nEqualSigns += 1;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            idxChar := STRPOS(txt64Chars, FORMAT(c[4]));
            IF idxChar = 0 THEN
              ERROR(Text000, FORMAT(c[4]), inPos);
            c[4] := idxChar - 1;
          END;

          // b[1] is c[1] and bits(5,6) of c[2]...
          b[1] := (c[1] * 4) + (c[2] DIV 16);
          outStr.WRITE(b[1]);

          // b[2] is low 4 bits of c[2] and bits(3..6) of c[3]...
          IF nEqualSigns < 2 THEN BEGIN
            b[2] := ((c[2] MOD 16) * 16) + (c[3] DIV 4);
            outStr.WRITE(b[2]);
          END;

          // b[3] is low 2 bits of c[3] and the 6 bits in c[4]...
          IF nEqualSigns = 0 THEN BEGIN
            b[3] := ((c[3] MOD 4) * 64) + c[4];
            outStr.WRITE(b[3]);
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE EncodeFF@1108800000(Text@1108800000 : Text[1024]) : Text[1024];
    VAR
      I@1108800001 : Integer;
      J@1108800003 : Integer;
      NewText@1108800002 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      FOR I := 1 TO STRLEN(Text) DO
        IF I MOD 2 <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          J += 1;
          NewText[J] := 255 - Text[I];
        END;
      FOR I := STRLEN(Text) DOWNTO 2 DO
        IF I MOD 2 = 0 THEN BEGIN
          J += 1;
          NewText[J] := 255 - Text[I];
        END;
      EXIT(NewText);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DecodeFF@1108800001(Text@1108800000 : Text[1024]) : Text[1024];
    VAR
      I@1108800001 : Integer;
      J@1108800003 : Integer;
      NewText@1108800002 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      FOR I := 1 TO STRLEN(Text) DO
        IF I MOD 2 <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          J += 1;
          NewText[I] := 255 - Text[J];
        END;
      FOR I := STRLEN(Text) DOWNTO 2 DO
        IF I MOD 2 = 0 THEN BEGIN
          J += 1;
          NewText[I] := 255 - Text[J];
        END;
      EXIT(NewText);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CodeHasSpecialChars@1000000048(Text@1000000000 : Code[40]) : Boolean;

      IF STRPOS(Text,' ') <> 0 THEN

      FOR i := 1 TO ARRAYLEN(SpecialChars) DO BEGIN
        IF STRPOS(Text,SpecialChars[i]) <> 0 THEN
          EXIT(TRUE);
      END;

    PROCEDURE EncodeURIComponent@1200050003(uri@1200050000 : Text[1024]) encodedUri : Text[1024];
    VAR
      HexDigits@1200050001 : Text[1024];
      AsciiStr@1200050006 : Text[1024];
      AnsiStr@1200050005 : Text[1024];
      i@1200050002 : Integer;
      b@1200050003 : Char;
      CharVar@1200050004 : ARRAY [32] OF Char;
      by@1000000000 : Char;
    BEGIN
      // No URI Encoding in NAV - we do it ourself...
      // http://blogs.msdn.com/b/freddyk/archive/2010/01/22/connecting-to-nav-web-services-from-microsoft-dynamics-nav-2009-sp1.aspx
      HexDigits := '0123456789ABCDEF';
      encodedUri := '';
      AsciiStr := '����������������������������������������������������ݵ�����++��++--+-+��++--�-+';
      AsciiStr := AsciiStr +'������i���++�_���������������������=�������������';
      CharVar[1] := 196;
      CharVar[2] := 197;
      CharVar[3] := 201;
      CharVar[4] := 242;
      CharVar[5] := 220;
      CharVar[6] := 186;
      CharVar[7] := 191;
      CharVar[8] := 188;
      CharVar[9] := 187;
      CharVar[10] := 193;
      CharVar[11] := 194;
      CharVar[12] := 192;
      CharVar[13] := 195;
      CharVar[14] := 202;
      CharVar[15] := 203;
      CharVar[16] := 200;
      CharVar[17] := 205;
      CharVar[18] := 206;
      CharVar[19] := 204;
      CharVar[20] := 175;
      CharVar[21] := 223;
      CharVar[22] := 213;
      CharVar[23] := 254;
      CharVar[24] := 218;
      CharVar[25] := 219;
      CharVar[26] := 217;
      CharVar[27] := 180;
      CharVar[28] := 177;
      CharVar[29] := 176;
      CharVar[30] := 185;
      CharVar[31] := 179;
      CharVar[32] := 178;
      AnsiStr  := '��������������'+FORMAT(CharVar[1])+FORMAT(CharVar[2])+FORMAT(CharVar[3])+ '����'+FORMAT(CharVar[4]);
      AnsiStr := AnsiStr + '����'+FORMAT(CharVar[5])+'�����ᡢ����'+FORMAT(CharVar[6])+FORMAT(CharVar[7]);
      AnsiStr := AnsiStr + '���'+FORMAT(CharVar[8])+'��'+FORMAT(CharVar[9])+'___��' + FORMAT(CharVar[10])+FORMAT(CharVar[11]);
      AnsiStr := AnsiStr + FORMAT(CharVar[12]) + '���++��++--+-+�' + FORMAT(CharVar[13]) + '++--�-+���';
      AnsiStr  :=  AnsiStr +FORMAT(CharVar[14])+FORMAT(CharVar[15])+FORMAT(CharVar[16])+'i'+FORMAT(CharVar[17])+FORMAT(CharVar[18]);
      AnsiStr  :=  AnsiStr + '�++__�' + FORMAT(CharVar[19])+FORMAT(CharVar[20])+'�'+FORMAT(CharVar[21])+'���';
      AnsiStr  :=  AnsiStr + FORMAT(CharVar[22]) + '�' + FORMAT(CharVar[23]) + '�' + FORMAT(CharVar[24])+ FORMAT(CharVar[25]);
      AnsiStr  :=  AnsiStr + FORMAT(CharVar[26]) + '���' + FORMAT(CharVar[27]) + '�' + FORMAT(CharVar[28]) +'=�����'+
                             FORMAT(CharVar[29]);
      AnsiStr  :=  AnsiStr + '��' + FORMAT(CharVar[30]) +FORMAT(CharVar[31]) +FORMAT(CharVar[32]) +'_ ';
      uri := CONVERTSTR(uri,AsciiStr,AnsiStr);
      FOR i:=1 TO STRLEN(uri) DO
      BEGIN
        by := uri[i];
        b := uri[i];
        IF (by IN [36,38,43,44,47,58,59,61,63,64,32,34,60,62,35,37,123,125,124,92,94,126,91,93,96]) OR
           (by >= 128)
        THEN BEGIN
          encodedUri := encodedUri + '%  ';
          encodedUri[STRLEN(encodedUri)-1] := HexDigits[(by DIV 16)+1];
          encodedUri[STRLEN(encodedUri)] := HexDigits[(by MOD 16)+1];
        END ELSE BEGIN
           encodedUri := encodedUri + COPYSTR(uri,i,1);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE StrReplace@1000000019(String@1000000002 : Text[250];Find@1000000001 : Text[250];Replace@1000000000 : Text[250]) NewString : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      WHILE STRPOS(String,Find) > 0 DO
        String := DELSTR(String,STRPOS(String,Find)) + Replace + COPYSTR(String,STRPOS(String,Find) + STRLEN(Find));
      NewString := String;
    END;

    PROCEDURE URLEncodeReadable@1000000020(String@1000000000 : Text[1024]) URLEncodedReadable : Text[1024];
    VAR
      ModifiedString@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      Text001@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=''';
    BEGIN

      ModifiedString := StrReplace(String,'�','Ae');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'�','ae');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'�','Oe');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'�','oe');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'�','Ue');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'�','ue');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'�','ss');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'%','proz');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'�','+Grad');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'/','-');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'\','-');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'.','-');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,',','+');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,';','+');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,':','+');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'#','+');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,' ','+');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,Text001,'+');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(URLEncode(ModifiedString),'%2B','+');
      ModifiedString := StrReplace(ModifiedString,'%26','+');
      URLEncodedReadable := DELCHR(ModifiedString,'<>','+');
    END;

    PROCEDURE WebshopRandomize@1000000012() Seed : Integer;
    VAR
      bigseed@1000000000 : BigInteger;
    BEGIN
      //generiert auf Basis von GUID-Werten einen Seed zwischen 1 und 2.147.483.646.
      //bigseed ist vom Typ BigInteger
      EVALUATE(bigseed, COPYSTR(DELCHR(CREATEGUID(),'=','{}-ABCDEF'),1,10));
      Seed:= (bigseed MOD 2147483646) +1;
    END;

    PROCEDURE WebshopRandom@1000000016(Seed@1000000000 : Integer;Number@1000000001 : Integer) Rand : Integer;
    VAR
      a@1000000002 : Integer;
      m@1000000003 : Integer;
      q@1000000004 : Integer;
      r@1000000005 : Integer;
      hi@1000000006 : Integer;
      lo@1000000007 : Integer;
      test@1000000008 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //......................................................................................................
      //Code: Minimal Standard Pseudozufallszahlengenerator (linearer Kongruenzgenerator)
      //Quelle: Steve K. Park and Keith W. Miller "Random Number Generators: Good ones are hard to find", 1988
      //Sprache: Navision C/AL (modifiziert von winfy)
      //Limit: nur f�r 32 Bit signed Integer geeignet, erzeugt Pseudozufallszahlen von 1 bis Parameter "Number"
      //......................................................................................................

      //Definitionen
      //a,m,q,r sind Variablen/Konstanten vom Typ Integer
      //test, hi, lo sind Variablen vom Typ Integer
      //Seed als �bergabeparameter vom Typ Integer

      //Initialisierung der Werte
      a := 16807; //alternativ a:= 48271;
      m := 2147483647;
      q := 127773; //alternativ q:= 44488;
      r := 2836; //alternativ r:= 3399

      //alternative "gute" Werte (L'Ecuyer)
      //a := 39373;
      //m := 2147483647;
      //q := 54542;
      //r := 1481;

      //Pseudozufallszahl berechnen
      hi := Seed DIV q;
      lo := Seed MOD q;
      test := a * lo - r * hi;

      //positiven Wertebereich sicherstellen
      IF test > 0 THEN Seed := test
        ELSE Seed := test + m;

      //Ergebnis im Bereich 1 bis Number erzeugen
      IF (Number > 0) THEN EXIT((Seed MOD Number) + 1)
        ELSE EXIT(1); //alternativ EXIT(0) oder Fehlermeldung
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllWebshopSetup@1000000063(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      WITH WebshopSetup DO BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          UpdateWebshopSetup;
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopSetup@1000000039();
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Webshop-Setup �bertragen

      WebshopSetup.GET;
      GeneralLedgerSetup.GET;
      AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_setup');
      AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'data[nas_email_text_module]',WebshopSetup."NAS E-Mail Text");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'data[nas_email_recipient]',WebshopSetup."NAS E-Mail Recipient");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'data[nas_email_recipient_2]',WebshopSetup."NAS E-Mail Recipient 2");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'data[nas_email_sender]',WebshopSetup."NAS E-Mail Sender");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'data[default_currency_code]',GeneralLedgerSetup."LCY Code");
      WebshopManagement.AddDTVar(CurrRec,'data[last_datetime_nav_updated]',WebshopSetup."Last Update All Data");

      IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
        WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
      ELSE
        WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllShop@1000000049(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Shop@1000000003 : Record 5447301;
    BEGIN
      // Alle Webshops �bertragen

      WITH Shop DO BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          DeleteAllShop(FALSE);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateShop(Shop);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          DeleteAllShop(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateShop@1000000048(ParameterShop@1000000002 : Record 5447301);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      AttributeLink@1000000001 : Record 5447321;
      Shop@1000000003 : Record 5447301;
      IStream@1000000004 : InStream;
      EbayAuthTokenBigText@1000000005 : BigText;

        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cust_price_group_retail_price]',"Cust. Price Group Retail Price");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cust_disc_group_retail_price]',"Cust. Disc. Group Retail Price");

        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


        //Auf 2 Teile aufteilen wegen L�nge
        CLEAR(CurrRec);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shop');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[campain_no]',"Campain No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[prices_including_vat]',"Prices Including VAT");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vat_bus_posting_group]',"VAT Bus. Posting Group");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[select_req_delivery_date]',"Select Req. Delivery Date");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[addition_req_delivery_date]',"Addition Req. Delivery Date");

        ELSE

          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          CALCFIELDS("Ebay Auth Token");
          "Ebay Auth Token".CREATEINSTREAM(IStream);
          EbayAuthTokenBigText.READ(IStream);
          EbayAuthTokenBigText.GETSUBTEXT(EbayAuthToken,1);

          FirstLine := TRUE;
          IF STRLEN(EbayAuthToken) > 600 THEN REPEAT

            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
            FirstLine := FALSE;
          UNTIL EbayAuthToken = '';
        END;

        CLEAR(CurrRec);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shop');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);

        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);

        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


         // Zugeh�rige Merkmale aktualisieren (Sprach�bergreifend)
         AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Shop);
         AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",Code);
         IF AttributeLink.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
           UpdateAttributeLink(AttributeLink);
         UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

         //Google Merchant Funktions aufruf
        IF NOT GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
          IF (WebshopSetup."Update Google Merchant") AND ("Google FTP URL" <> '') THEN
            UpdateGoogleMerchant(Code);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteAllShop@1000000042(ConfirmDelete@1000000001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Alle Webshops l�schen

      AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_shop');
      AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'confirm_delete',ConfirmDelete);
      WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);
      IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
        WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
      ELSE
        WebshopManagement.SendRequest('delete',CurrRec);
    END;

        // Zugeh�rige W�hrungen �bertragen

    PROCEDURE CheckItemPlaceholderImage@1000000071(WebshopLanguage@1000000000 : Record 5447302) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text[1024];
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Platzhalter Artikelbild pr�fen

      WITH WebshopLanguage DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_language');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'field','item_placeholder_image');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
        IF GetRec = '' THEN
          Ok := FALSE
        ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
          Ok := TRUE
        ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetRec)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetRec);
              EXIT(TRUE);
            END ELSE
              ERROR('No Logfile specified');
          END;
        END;
        IF "Item Placeholder Image" <> Ok THEN BEGIN
          "Item Placeholder Image" := Ok;
          MODIFY;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllCountry@1000000003(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopCountry@1000000003 : Record 5447310;
    BEGIN
      // Alle L�ndereinstellungen �bertragen

      WITH WebshopCountry DO BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          DeleteAllCountry(FALSE);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateCountry(WebshopCountry);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          DeleteAllCountry(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckTextModule@1000000024(WebshopTextModule@1000000000 : Record 5447309) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text[1024];
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Textbaustein auf Inhalt �berpr�fen

      WITH WebshopTextModule DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_text_module');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'field','content');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
        IF GetRec = '' THEN
          OK := FALSE
        ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
          OK := TRUE
        ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetRec)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetRec);
              EXIT(TRUE);
            END ELSE
              ERROR('No Logfile specified');
          END;
        END;

        IF OK <> Content THEN BEGIN
          Content := OK;
          MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckTextModuleAttachment@1000000136(WebshopTextModule@1000000000 : Record 5447309;AttachmentNo@1000000003 : Integer) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text[1024];
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Textbaustein auf Inhalt der Anh�nge �berpr�fen

      WITH WebshopTextModule DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_text_module');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'field','attachment_'+FORMAT(AttachmentNo));
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
        IF GetRec = '' THEN
          OK := FALSE
        ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
          OK := TRUE
        ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetRec)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetRec);
              EXIT(TRUE);
            END ELSE
              ERROR('No Logfile specified');
          END;
        END;

        IF (AttachmentNo=1) AND (OK <> "Attachment 1") THEN BEGIN
          "Attachment 1" := OK;
          MODIFY(TRUE);
        END ELSE IF (AttachmentNo=2) AND (OK <> "Attachment 2") THEN BEGIN
          "Attachment 2" := OK;
          MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

        IF OK <> Logo THEN BEGIN
          Logo := OK;
          MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckPaymentOption@1000000210(WebshopPaymentOption@1000000000 : Record 5447312) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text[1024];
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // PaymentOption Content auf Inhalt �berpr�fen

      WITH WebshopPaymentOption DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_payment_option');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'field','content');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
        IF GetRec = '' THEN
          OK := FALSE
        ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
          OK := TRUE
        ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetRec)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetRec);
              EXIT(TRUE);
            END ELSE
              ERROR('No Logfile specified');
          END;
        END;

        IF OK <> Content THEN BEGIN
          Content := OK;
          MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAndSaveLastSolrUpdateDT@1000000148();
    VAR
      GetRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      ResultText@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      ResultDateTime@1000000003 : DateTime;
      WebshopSetup@1000000000 : Record 5447300;
    BEGIN
      //Letzte Datum-Uhrzeit Solr-Update abholen und speichern

      ResultText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_solr_update_datetime',GetRec);
      IF ResultText = '0' THEN
        ResultDateTime := 0DT
      ELSE
        EVALUATE(ResultDateTime,ResultText);
      WebshopSetup.GET;
      WebshopSetup."Last Update-Index Solr" := ResultDateTime;
      WebshopSetup.MODIFY(FALSE);
    END;
