    PROCEDURE UpdateAllWebshopItem@1000000009(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;
    BEGIN
      // Alle Webshop-Artikel �bertragen

      WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            IF ("Last Date Updated" < "Last Date Modified") OR ("Last Date Updated" = 0DT) THEN
              UpdateWebshopItem(WebshopItem);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopItemManually@1000000065(WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304);
    VAR
      Language@1000000002 : Record 8;
      UnitOfMeasure@1000000003 : Record 204;
      UnitOfMeasureTranslation@1000000010 : Record 5402;
      WebshopVarianType@1000000004 : Record 5447302;
      WebshopItemRecord@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      WebshopItemFile@1000000005 : Record 5447307;
      WebshopItemDescription@1000000006 : Record 5447306;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000007 : Record 5447308;
      Item@1000000008 : Record 27;
      SalesPrice@1000000009 : Record 7002;
      WebshopItemCategory@1000000011 : Record 5447305;
      WebshopCategory@1000000012 : Record 5447303;
      ParamKeys@1000000014 : ARRAY [20] OF Text[20];
      ParamValues@1000000013 : ARRAY [20] OF Text[20];
      WebshopItemCustomize@1000000015 : Record 5447348;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelnen Webshop-Artikel mit zugeh�rigen Kategorien �bertragen

      WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
        UpdateWebshopItem(WebshopItem);
        WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");
        WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
        WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
        IF WebshopItemCategory.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
          IF WebshopCategory.GET("Shop Code","Language Code",WebshopItemCategory."Category Line No.") THEN BEGIN
            UpdateCategory(WebshopCategory);
            UpdateWebshopItemCategory(WebshopItemCategory);
          END;
        UNTIL WebshopItemCategory.NEXT = 0;
        WebshopItemCustomize.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");
        WebshopItemCustomize.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
        WebshopItemCustomize.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
        IF WebshopItemCustomize.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
          UpdateItemCustomizations(WebshopItemCustomize);
        UNTIL WebshopItemCustomize.NEXT = 0;
        WebshopMarketplaceManagement.UpdateMarketplaceItem(WebshopItem);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopItem@1000000008(ParameterWebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304);
    VAR
      UnitOfMeasure@1000000003 : Record 204;
      UnitOfMeasureTranslation@1000000010 : Record 5402;
      WebshopItemFile@1000000005 : Record 5447307;
      WebshopItemDescription@1000000006 : Record 5447306;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000007 : Record 5447308;
      ItemCrossReference@1000000002 : Record 5717;
      ItemVariant@1000000004 : Record 5401;
      ItemVariantTranslation@1000000015 : Record 30;
      Item@1000000008 : Record 27;
      SalesPrice@1000000009 : Record 7002;
      Item2@1000000011 : Record 27;
      ItemUnitOfMeasure@1000000012 : Record 5404;
      Shop@1000000013 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000014 : Record 5447302;
      TempWebshopItem@1000000016 : Record 5447304;
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      AttributeLink@1000000017 : Record 5447321;
      AttributeTranslation@1000000018 : Record 5447323;
      WebshopItem@1000000019 : Record 5447304;
      Vendor@1000000020 : Record 23;
      Multiplier@1000000021 : Decimal;
      ItemUnitOfMeasure2@1000000022 : Record 5404;
      ItemCondition@1000000023 : Integer;
      WebshopShop@1000000024 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopItemComment@1000000026 : Record 5447317;
      WebshopItemCustomizations@1000000025 : Record 5447348;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelnen Webshop-Artikel �bertragen

      WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
        GET(ParameterWebshopItem."Shop Code",ParameterWebshopItem."Language Code",ParameterWebshopItem."Item No.");
        IF Item.GET("Item No.") AND Shop.GET("Shop Code") AND Language.GET("Shop Code","Language Code") THEN BEGIN

          // Datens�tze Webshop-Artikel �bertragen
          CALCFIELDS("Parent Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
          IF UnitOfMeasure.GET("Unit of Measure Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF UnitOfMeasureTranslation.GET("Unit of Measure Code",Language.Code) THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[base_unit_of_measure]',UnitOfMeasureTranslation.Description)
            ELSE
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[base_unit_of_measure]',UnitOfMeasure.Description);
          END;
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[unit_of_measure_code]',"Unit of Measure Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[variant_type]',"Variant Description");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',Active);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[validity_from]',"Validity from");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[validity_to]',"Validity to");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[summary]',Summary);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[main_picture_line_no]',"Main Picture Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[main_category_line_no]',"Main Category Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[insufficient_inventory_limit]',"Insufficient Inventory Limit");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[creation_date]',"Creation Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[allow_gift_package]',"Allow Gift Package");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[is_gift_package]',"Is Gift Package");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[minimum_order_quantity]',"Minimum Order Quantity");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[quantity_packing_unit]',"Quantity Packing Unit");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[order_per_packing_unit]',"Order per Packing Unit");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[search_query]',"Search Query");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          // Datensatz wg. L�nge in zwei Teilen senden
          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[meta_keywords]',"Meta Keywords");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[meta_description]',"Meta Description");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[site_title]',"Site Title");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          // Datensatz wg. L�nge in drei Teilen senden
          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[parent_item_no]',"Parent Item No.");

          // Datens�tze aus NAV-Artikel �bertragen
          Item.CALCFIELDS("Qty. on Purch. Order");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[quantity_on_purchase_order]',Item."Qty. on Purch. Order");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[discount_group]',Item."Item Disc. Group");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[allow_invoice_discount]',Item."Allow Invoice Disc.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vendor_no]',Item."Vendor No.");
          IF Vendor.GET(Item."Vendor No.") THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vendor_name]',Vendor.Name);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[price_includes_vat]',Item."Price Includes VAT");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[vat_prod_posting_group]',Item."VAT Prod. Posting Group");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[nav_base_unit_code]',Item."Base Unit of Measure");
          IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.GET("Item No.","Unit of Measure Code") THEN BEGIN
            IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.Weight <> 0 THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[weight]',ItemUnitOfMeasure.Weight)
            ELSE
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[weight]',Item."Gross Weight");
            IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.Width <> 0 THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[width]',ItemUnitOfMeasure.Width);
            IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.Height <> 0 THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[height]',ItemUnitOfMeasure.Height);
            IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.Length <> 0 THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[length]',ItemUnitOfMeasure.Length);
            IF ItemUnitOfMeasure.Cubage <> 0 THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[volume]',ItemUnitOfMeasure.Cubage);
            IF ItemUnitOfMeasure2.GET("Item No.",Item."Base Unit of Measure") THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[multiplier]',
                      (ItemUnitOfMeasure."Qty. per Unit of Measure"/ItemUnitOfMeasure2."Qty. per Unit of Measure"))
            ELSE
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[multiplier]','1');
          END;
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[net_weight]',Item."Net Weight");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          // Funktionen aus Webshop-Artikel �bertragen
          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[retail_price]',WebshopRetailPrice);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[base_price]',WebshopBasePrice);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[inventory]',WebshopInventory(''));
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[order_ranking]',WebshopOrderRanking);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customization_price]',WebshopCustomizePrice);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customizable]',Customizable);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shipping_category]',"Shipping Category");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[always_available]',"Item Always Available");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_product_category]',"Google Product Category");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[is_greeting_card]',"Is Greeting Card");

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          // Marketplace
          WebshopShop.GET("Shop Code");
          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
          CASE "Item Condition" OF
            "Item Condition"::"1000": ItemCondition := 1000;
            "Item Condition"::"1500": ItemCondition := 1500;
            "Item Condition"::"1750": ItemCondition := 1750;
            "Item Condition"::"2000": ItemCondition := 2000;
            "Item Condition"::"2500": ItemCondition := 2500;
            "Item Condition"::"3000": ItemCondition := 3000;
            "Item Condition"::"4000": ItemCondition := 4000;
            "Item Condition"::"5000": ItemCondition := 5000;
            "Item Condition"::"6000": ItemCondition := 6000;
            "Item Condition"::"7000": ItemCondition := 7000;
          END;

          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[item_condition]',ItemCondition);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[item_location_zip]',WebshopShop."Item Loc. Post Code");
          IF Shop."Marketplace Type" = Shop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN BEGIN
            IF "Marketplace Ebay Item Template" <> '' THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_ebay_item_template]',"Marketplace Ebay Item Template")
            ELSE
              AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_ebay_item_template]',Language."Marketplace Ebay Item Template")
          END;

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          UpdateWebshopItemMarketplace(WebshopItem,TRUE,TRUE,TRUE,TRUE);

          // Aktualisierungsdatum setzten
          "Last Updated Inventory" := WebshopInventory('');
          "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);

          // Zugeh�rige Bilder, Videos und Dateien aktualisieren
          WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
          WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          IF WebshopItemFile.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateWebshopItemFile(WebshopItemFile);
          UNTIL WebshopItemFile.NEXT = 0;

          // Zugeh�rige Beschreibung aktualisieren
          WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
          WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE(Type,WebshopItemDescription.Type::Description);
          IF WebshopItemDescription.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateWebshopItemDescription(WebshopItemDescription);
          UNTIL WebshopItemDescription.NEXT = 0;

          // Zugeh�rige Kommentare aktualisieren
          WebshopItemComment.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemComment.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
          WebshopItemComment.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          IF WebshopItemComment.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateWebshopItemComment(WebshopItemComment);
          UNTIL WebshopItemComment.NEXT = 0;

          // Nur aktualisieren wenn in den letzten 30 Sekunden keine gleiche Artikelnr. im gleichen Shop �bertragen wurde
          TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          TempWebshopItem.SETFILTER("Last Date Updated",'>%1',CREATEDATETIME(TODAY,DT2TIME("Last Date Updated")-30000));
          IF TempWebshopItem.COUNT <= 1 THEN BEGIN

            // Zugeh�rige Verkn�pfungen aktualisieren (Sprach�bergreifend)
            WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ);
            WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
            WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            IF WebshopItemLink.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateWebshopItemLink(WebshopItemLink);
            UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;

            // Zugeh�rige Merkmale aktualisieren (Sprach�bergreifend)
            AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Item);
            AttributeLink.SETRANGE("No.","Item No.");
            IF AttributeLink.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateAttributeLink(AttributeLink);
              //Zugeh�rige �bersetzungen �bertragen
              AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeTranslation.Type::"Attribute Link");
              AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Link No.",AttributeLink."No.");
              IF AttributeTranslation.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                UpdateAttributeTranslation(AttributeTranslation);
              UNTIL AttributeTranslation.NEXT = 0;
            UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

          END;

          // Nur aktualisieren wenn in den letzten 30 Sekunden keine gleiche Artikelnr. �bertragen wurde
          TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code");
          TempWebshopItem.SETFILTER("Last Date Updated",'>%1',CREATEDATETIME(TODAY,DT2TIME("Last Date Updated")-30000));
          IF TempWebshopItem.COUNT <= 1 THEN BEGIN

            // Zugeh�rige Referenzen aktualisieren (Shop- und Sprach�bergreifend)
            ItemCrossReference.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            ItemCrossReference.SETFILTER("Cross-Reference Type",'<>%1',ItemCrossReference."Cross-Reference Type"::Vendor);
            DeleteItemCrossReference("Item No.",FALSE);
            IF ItemCrossReference.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateItemCrossReference(ItemCrossReference);
            UNTIL ItemCrossReference.NEXT = 0;
            DeleteItemCrossReference("Item No.",TRUE);

            // Zugeh�rige Artikelvarianten aktualisieren (Shop- und Sprach�bergreifend)
            WebshopSetup.GET;
            IF WebshopSetup."Update Item Variant" THEN BEGIN
              ItemVariant.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
              ItemVariant.SETRANGE("Webshop Active",TRUE);
              DeleteItemVariant("Item No.",FALSE);
              IF ItemVariant.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
                UpdateItemVariant(ItemVariant);
              UNTIL ItemVariant.NEXT = 0;
              DeleteItemVariant("Item No.",TRUE);
            END;

            // Zugeh�rige Varianten�bersetzungen aktualisieren (Shop- und Sprach�bergreifend)
            ItemVariantTranslation.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            ItemVariantTranslation.SETFILTER("Variant Code",'<>%1','');
            DeleteItemVariantTranslation("Item No.",FALSE);
            IF ItemVariantTranslation.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateItemVariantTranslation(ItemVariantTranslation);
            UNTIL ItemVariantTranslation.NEXT = 0;
            DeleteItemVariantTranslation("Item No.",TRUE);

            // Zugeh�rige Preise aktualisieren (Shop- und Sprach�bergreifend)
            UpdateSalesPrice("Item No.");

          END;

          //Artikelindividualisierung
          WebshopItemCustomizations.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          WebshopItemCustomizations.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemCustomizations.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
          DeleteItemCustomization("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.",FALSE);
          IF WebshopItemCustomizations.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateItemCustomizations(WebshopItemCustomizations);
          UNTIL WebshopItemCustomizations.NEXT = 0;
          DeleteItemCustomization("Shop Code","Language Code","Item No.",TRUE);

          COMMIT;

        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteWebshopItem@1000000020(WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304);
    VAR
      Language@1000000002 : Record 8;
      UnitOfMeasure@1000000003 : Record 204;
      WebshopVarianType@1000000004 : Record 5447302;
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      WebshopItemFile@1000000005 : Record 5447307;
      WebshopItemDescription@1000000006 : Record 5447306;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000007 : Record 5447308;
      TempWebshopItem@1000000008 : Record 5447304;
      WebshopItemCategory@1000000009 : Record 5447305;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelnen Webshop-Artikel l�schen

      WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[language_code]',"Language Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[item_no]',"Item No.");
        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec);


        // Zugeh�rige Kategorien l�schen
        WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Shop Code","Shop Code");
        WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item Language Code","Language Code");
        WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
        WebshopItemCategory.SetInWebshopItemDelete(TRUE);
        IF WebshopItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          WebshopItemCategory.DELETE(TRUE);
        UNTIL WebshopItemCategory.NEXT = 0;

        // Zugeh�rige Bilder, Videos und Dateien l�schen
        WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
        WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
        WebshopItemFile.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
        IF WebshopItemFile.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          WebshopItemFile.DELETE(TRUE);
        UNTIL WebshopItemFile.NEXT = 0;

        // Zugeh�rige Beschreibung l�schen
        WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
        WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
        WebshopItemDescription.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
        IF WebshopItemDescription.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          WebshopItemDescription.DELETE(TRUE);
        UNTIL WebshopItemDescription.NEXT = 0;

        // Nur l�schen wenn kein anderer Artikel zu Artikelnr. und Shopcode vorhanden ist
        TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
        TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
        IF TempWebshopItem.COUNT <= 1 THEN BEGIN

          // Zugeh�rige Verkn�pfungen l�schen (wenn kein anderer Webshopartikel zu Artikelnummer vorhanden)
          WebshopItemLink.SetDeleteItem(TRUE);
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
          IF WebshopItemLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            WebshopItemLink.DELETE(TRUE);
          UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;

        END;

        // Nur l�schen wenn kein anderer Artikel zu Artikelnr. vorhanden ist
        TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code");
        TempWebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
        IF TempWebshopItem.COUNT <= 1 THEN BEGIN

          // Zugeh�rige Referenzen l�schen
          DeleteItemCrossReference("Item No.",FALSE);
          DeleteItemCrossReference("Item No.",TRUE);

          // Zugeh�rige Varianten l�schen
          DeleteItemVariant("Item No.",FALSE);
          DeleteItemVariant("Item No.",TRUE);

          // Zugeh�rige Varianten�bersetzungen l�schen
          DeleteItemVariantTranslation("Item No.",FALSE);
          DeleteItemVariantTranslation("Item No.",TRUE);

          // Zugeh�rige Preise l�schen (wenn kein anderer Webshopartikel zu Artikelnummer vorhanden)
          DeleteSalesPricePerItem(FALSE,"Item No.");
          DeleteSalesPricePerItem(TRUE,"Item No.");

        END;

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopItemMarketplace@1000000228(ParaWebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;ParaUpdate@1000000005 : Boolean;ParaUpdateImages@1000000006 : Boolean;ParaUpdatePrice@1000000007 : Boolean;ParaUpdateInventory@1000000008 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopShop@1000000001 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopLanguage@1000000002 : Record 5447302;
      WebshopItem@1000000003 : Record 5447304;
      CurrRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      WebshopSetup.GET;

      WebshopItem.GET(ParaWebshopItem."Shop Code",ParaWebshopItem."Language Code",ParaWebshopItem."Item No.");
      WebshopShop.SETRANGE(WebshopShop."Use Items from Shop Code",WebshopItem."Shop Code");
      WebshopShop.SETRANGE(WebshopShop."Shop Typ",WebshopShop."Shop Typ"::marketplace);
      IF WebshopShop.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
        WebshopLanguage.SETRANGE(WebshopLanguage."Shop Code",WebshopShop.Code);
        IF WebshopLanguage.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
          CLEAR(CurrRec);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_item_update');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',WebshopShop.Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',WebshopLanguage.Code);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_shop_code]',WebshopItem."Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_language_code]',WebshopItem."Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',WebshopItem."Item No.");

          WebshopManagement.AddDTVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_last_nav_update]',CURRENTDATETIME);
          IF ParaUpdate THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update]',TRUE);
          IF ParaUpdateImages THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_images]',TRUE);
          IF ParaUpdatePrice THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_price]',TRUE);
          IF ParaUpdateInventory THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_inventory]',TRUE);

          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

        UNTIL WebshopLanguage.NEXT = 0;
      UNTIL WebshopShop.NEXT = 0;

      CLEAR(WebshopShop);
      WebshopShop.RESET;
      IF WebshopShop.GET(WebshopItem."Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
        IF WebshopShop."Shop Typ" = WebshopShop."Shop Typ"::marketplace THEN BEGIN
          WebshopLanguage.SETRANGE(WebshopLanguage."Shop Code",WebshopShop.Code);
          IF WebshopLanguage.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_item_update');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',WebshopShop.Code);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',WebshopLanguage.Code);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_shop_code]',WebshopItem."Shop Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_language_code]',WebshopItem."Language Code");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',WebshopItem."Item No.");

            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update]',TRUE);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_images]',TRUE);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_price]',TRUE);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_update_inventory]',TRUE);

            IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
              WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
            ELSE
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          UNTIL WebshopLanguage.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllWebshopItemInventory@1000000045(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean);

          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckWebshopItemDescription@1000000017(WebshopItemDescription@1000000000 : Record 5447306) OK : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text[1024];
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Inhalt von Webshop Artikelbeschreibung pr�fen

      WITH WebshopItemDescription DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_description');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'field','content');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
        IF GetRec = '' THEN
          OK := FALSE
        ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
          OK := TRUE
        ELSE BEGIN
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetRec)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetRec);
              EXIT(TRUE);
            END ELSE
              ERROR('No Logfile specified');
          END;
        END;

        IF OK <> Content THEN BEGIN
          Content := OK;
          MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllItemVariantInventory@1000000079(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304;
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU="Lagerbestand "';
      ItemVariant@1000000003 : Record 5401;
    BEGIN
      // Lagerbestand f�r alle Webshop-Artikelvarianten �bertragen

      WITH ItemVariant DO BEGIN
        ItemVariant.SETRANGE("Webshop Active",TRUE);
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001 + TABLECAPTION,ItemVariant.COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001 + TABLECAPTION,ItemVariant.COUNT);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
            IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              IF WebshopItem.WebshopInventory(Code) <> "Webshop Last Updated Inventory" THEN
                UpdateItemVariant(ItemVariant);
              END;
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateItemVariant@1000000098(ParameterItemVariant@1000000000 : Record 5401);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      WebshopItem@1000000002 : Record 5447304;
      VariantInventory@1000000003 : Decimal;
      ItemVariant@1000000004 : Record 5401;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelne Artikelvariante �bertragen

      WITH ItemVariant DO BEGIN
        GET(ParameterItemVariant."Item No.",ParameterItemVariant.Code);
        // Lagerbestand ausrechnen
        WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.","Item No.");
        IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN
          VariantInventory := WebshopItem.WebshopInventory(Code);

        // Daten �bertragen
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_variant');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description_2]',"Description 2");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[inventory]',VariantInventory);
        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


        // �bertragenen Lagerbestand speichern
        "Webshop Last Updated Inventory" := VariantInventory;
        MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAllItemComments@1000000117(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Bestellungen abrufen';
      NoOfOrder@1000000002 : Integer;
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1002 : File;
      LogResponse@1001 : Text[1024];
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Alle neuen Kommentare abrufen

      WebshopSetup.GET;
      AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_comments');
      AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
      CLEAR(GetResponseText);
      GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
      IF EVALUATE(NoOfOrder,WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec)) THEN BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfOrder);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfOrder);
          GetItemComment;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
          ERROR(GetResponseText)
        ELSE BEGIN
          IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
            LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
            LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
            IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
             IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
              ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
            END;
            LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
            LogFile.WRITE(GetResponseText);
            LogFile.CLOSE;
            IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
              MESSAGE(GetResponseText);
          END ELSE
            ERROR('No Logfile specified');
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetItemComment@1000000116();
    VAR
      Shop@1000000012 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000011 : Record 5447302;
      Text001@1000000076 : TextConst 'DEU=Lastschriftkonto: %1';
      Text002@1000000080 : TextConst 'DEU=Gutscheinrabatt Code %1';
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1118900000 : Codeunit 5447301;
      ExtendedTextLine@1000000082 : Record 280;
      Delimiter@1000000000 : Text[5];
      WebshopItemComment@1000000001 : Record 5447317;
      NextWebshopItemComment@1000000002 : Boolean;
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      ItemCommentId@1000000005 : Integer;
      ShopCode@1000000006 : Code[10];
      LanguageCode@1000000007 : Code[10];
      ItemNo@1000000008 : Code[10];
      Rating@1000000009 : Integer;
      City@1000000010 : Text[100];
      Header@1000000013 : Text[100];
      UserEmail@1000000014 : Text[100];
      CreationDate@1000000015 : DateTime;
      Item@1000000016 : Record 27;
      SetRec@1000000017 : Text[1024];
      LineNo@1118900001 : Integer;
      WebshopItemComment2@1118900002 : Record 5447317;
      UserName@1118900003 : Text[100];
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      LogResponse@1000 : Text[1024];
      Released@1000000025 : Boolean;
      CommentValue@1000000024 : Text[1024];
      DateValue@1000000023 : Text[1024];
      intDay@1000000022 : Integer;
      intMonth@1000000021 : Integer;
      IntYear@1000000020 : Integer;
      ValueDate@1000000019 : Date;
      ValueTime@1000000018 : Time;
    BEGIN
      // Neuen Kommentar abrufen

      Delimiter := '-;-';

      WITH WebshopItemComment DO REPEAT
        NextWebshopItemComment := FALSE;

        CLEAR(CurrRec);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_comments');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','id,shop_code,language_code,item_no,rating,city,header,email,' +
                                'name,creation_date,released,comment');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
        IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          EVALUATE(ItemCommentId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShopCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(LanguageCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ItemNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(Rating,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(City,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(Header,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(UserEmail,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(UserName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(DateValue,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(intDay,COPYSTR(DateValue,1,2));
          EVALUATE(intMonth,COPYSTR(DateValue,4,2));
          EVALUATE(IntYear,COPYSTR(DateValue,7,4));

          ValueDate := DMY2DATE(intDay,intMonth,IntYear);
          EVALUATE(ValueTime,COPYSTR(DateValue,12,8));

          CreationDate := CREATEDATETIME(ValueDate,ValueTime);

          EVALUATE(Released,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(CommentValue,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));

          IF (ShopCode <> '') AND (LanguageCode <> '') AND (ItemNo <>'') THEN BEGIN
            IF Item.GET(ItemNo) THEN BEGIN

              // Zeilennr. generieren
              WebshopItemComment2.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
              WebshopItemComment2.SETRANGE("Language Code",LanguageCode);
              WebshopItemComment2.SETRANGE("Item No.",Item."No.");
              IF WebshopItemComment2.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
                LineNo := WebshopItemComment2."Line No." + 10000;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                LineNo := 10000;
              END;

              INIT();
              VALIDATE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
              VALIDATE("Language Code",LanguageCode);
              VALIDATE("Item No.",Item."No.");
              VALIDATE("Comment Header",Header);
              VALIDATE("Line No.", LineNo);
              VALIDATE("Comment Date", CreationDate);
              VALIDATE("Comment Rating", Rating);
              VALIDATE("Comment User Name", UserName);
              VALIDATE("Comment User City", City);
              VALIDATE(Comment, CommentValue);
              VALIDATE("Comment Released", Released);
              VALIDATE("Comment User Email", UserEmail);
              VALIDATE( "Last Date Updated", CURRENTDATETIME);
              VALIDATE( "Last Date Modified", CURRENTDATETIME);
              INSERT(TRUE);

              // Kommentar als �bertragen markieren
              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_item_comments');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',ItemCommentId);
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[line_no]',LineNo);
              IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
                WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',SetRec,FALSE,TRUE)
              ELSE
                WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

              NextWebshopItemComment := TRUE;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
              COMMIT;
            END;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                    ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END;
        END;
      UNTIL NOT NextWebshopItemComment;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllWebshopItemComment@1000000224(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopItemComment@1000000000 : Record 5447317;
    BEGIN
      // Alle Webshop-Artikel �bertragen

      WITH WebshopItemComment DO BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            IF ("Last Date Updated" < "Last Date Modified") OR ("Last Date Updated" = 0DT) THEN
              UpdateWebshopItemComment(WebshopItemComment);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

        END ELSE BEGIN

              UpdateWebshopItemStdPrices(WebshopItem);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllAttribute@1000000105(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Attribute@1000000001 : Record 5447320;
    BEGIN
      // Alle Merkmale �bertragen

      WITH Attribute DO BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          DeleteAllAttribute(FALSE);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateAttribute(Attribute);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          DeleteAllAttribute(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAttribute@1000000106(ParameterAttribute@1000000000 : Record 5447320);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      AttributeTranslation@1000000002 : Record 5447323;
      Attribute@1000000003 : Record 5447320;
      WebshopShop@1000000004 : Record 5447301;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelnes Merkmal �bertragen

      WITH Attribute DO BEGIN
        GET(ParameterAttribute.Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_attribute');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[attribute_type]',"Attribute Type");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[display_type]',"Display Type");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[multiple_choices]',"Multiple Choice");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[data_type]',"Data Type");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[navision_value]',"Existing Data");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[filter_all]',"Attribute Filter");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[filter_category]',"Attribute Comparision");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_on_card]',"Attribute Info");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[use_for_comparison]',"Attribute Comparision");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[auto_link]',"Auto Link");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[map_to_attribute]',"Map-to Attribute Code");
        IF "Marketplace Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF WebshopShop.GET("Marketplace Code") THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_type]',WebshopShop."Marketplace Type");
        END ELSE
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[marketplace_type]',0);

        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_in_header]',"Show in Header");

        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


        //Zugeh�rige �bersetzungen �bertragen
        AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeTranslation.Type::Attribute);
        AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Code",Attribute.Code);
        IF AttributeTranslation.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
          UpdateAttributeTranslation(AttributeTranslation);
        UNTIL AttributeTranslation.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllAttributeOption@1000000111(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      AttributeOption@1000000001 : Record 5447322;
    BEGIN
      // Alle Merkmalsoptionen �bertragen

      WITH AttributeOption DO BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          DeleteAllAttributeOption(FALSE);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateAttributeOption(AttributeOption);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
          DeleteAllAttributeOption(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAttributeOption@1000000112(ParameterAttributeOption@1000000000 : Record 5447322);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      AttributeTranslation@1000000002 : Record 5447323;
      AttributeOption@1000000003 : Record 5447322;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelne Merkmalsoption �bertragen

      WITH AttributeOption DO BEGIN
        GET(ParameterAttributeOption."Attribute Code",ParameterAttributeOption.Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_attribute_option');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[attribute_code]',"Attribute Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[filter]',Filter);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[link]',Link);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sorting]',Sorting);

        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


        //Zugeh�rige �bersetzungen �bertragen
        AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeTranslation.Type::"Attribute Option");
        AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Link No.",AttributeOption.Code);
        IF AttributeTranslation.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
          UpdateAttributeTranslation(AttributeTranslation);
        UNTIL AttributeTranslation.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckFilterImage@1000000114(AttributeOption@1000000000 : Record 5447322) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      LogResponse@1000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Einzelne Webshop Artikeldatei pr�fen

      WITH AttributeOption DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_attribute_option');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'field','icon');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[attribute_code]',"Attribute Code");
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
        IF GetRec = '' THEN
          Ok := FALSE
        ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
          Ok := TRUE
        ELSE
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetRec)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetRec);
            END ELSE
              ERROR('No Logfile specified');
          END;
        IF Ok <> Icon THEN BEGIN
          Icon := Ok;
          MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllCategory@1000000027(CountRecords@1000000001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303;
    BEGIN
      // Alle Webshop Kategorien �bertragen

      WITH WebshopCategory DO BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            IF ("Last Date Updated" = 0DT) OR ("Last Date Updated" < "Last Date Modified") THEN
              UpdateCategory(WebshopCategory);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCategory@1000000022(ParameterWebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      TempWebshopCategory@1000000005 : Record 5447303;
      NextCategory@1000000006 : Boolean;
      ParentLineNo@1000000003 : Integer;
      RootLineNo@1000000007 : Integer;
      Sorting@1000000004 : Integer;
      AttributeLink@1000000002 : Record 5447321;
      AttributeTranslation@1000000008 : Record 5447323;
      WebshopCategory@1000000009 : Record 5447303;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelne Webshop Kategorie �bertragen

      WITH WebshopCategory DO BEGIN
        GET(ParameterWebshopCategory."Shop Code",ParameterWebshopCategory."Language Code",ParameterWebshopCategory."Line No.");
        IF Name <> '' THEN BEGIN

          TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");

          // Feld Sorting f�r Web ermitteln
          Sorting := 1;
          TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER(Level,'<=%1',Level);
          TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
          IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN REPEAT
            IF TempWebshopCategory.Level <> WebshopCategory.Level THEN
              NextCategory := TRUE;
            IF NOT NextCategory THEN
              Sorting := Sorting + 1;
          UNTIL TempWebshopCategory.NEXT(-1) = 0;

          // Feld "parent_line_no" f�r Web ermitteln
          ParentLineNo := 0;
          TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER(Level,'<%1',Level);
          TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
          IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
            ParentLineNo := TempWebshopCategory."Line No.";
          END;

          // Feld "root_line_no" f�r Web ermitteln
          RootLineNo := 0;
          IF Level > 1 THEN BEGIN
            TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE(Level,1);
            TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
            IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
              RootLineNo := TempWebshopCategory."Line No.";
            END;
          END;

          // Kagegorie Teil 1 �bertragen
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sorting]',Sorting);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[root_line_no]',RootLineNo);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[parent_line_no]',ParentLineNo);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[name]',Name);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[level]',Level);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[code]',"Search Code");
          //AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_random_items]',"Show Random Items");
          //AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_random_items]',"No of Random Items");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_random_items]',0);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_random_items]',0);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_campain_items]',"Show Campain Items");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_campain_items]',"No of Campain Items");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[user_sorting]',"User Sorting");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sort_items]',"Sort Items");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_all_items]',"Show All Items");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_sub_categorys]',"Show Sub Categorys");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',Active);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[validity_from]',"Validity from");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[validity_to]',"Validity to");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_active]',"Promtion Active");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_validity_from]',"Promtion Validity from");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_validity_to]',"Promtion Validity to");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_label]',"Promotion Label");
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          CLEAR(CurrRec);

           // Kagegorie Teil 2 �bertragen
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[meta_keywords]',"Meta Keywords");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[meta_description]',"Meta Description");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[site_titel]',"Site Title");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[search_query]',"Search Query");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[url]',GetCategoryURL);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_product_category]',"Google Product Category");
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          // Zugeh�rige Merkmale aktualisieren
          AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
          AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.","Line No.");
          AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
          IF AttributeLink.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateAttributeLink(AttributeLink);
            //Zugeh�rige �bersetzungen �bertragen
            AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeTranslation.Type::"Attribute Link");
            AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Link No.",AttributeLink."No.");
            IF AttributeTranslation.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateAttributeTranslation(AttributeTranslation);
            UNTIL AttributeTranslation.NEXT = 0;
          UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

          //Als �bertragen kennzeichnen
          "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);

        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteCategory@1000000076(WebshopCategory@1000000001 : Record 5447303);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Einzelne Webshop Kategorie l�schen

      WITH WebshopCategory DO BEGIN

        // Kategoriebild l�schen wenn vorhanden
        IF "Category Picture" THEN
          DeleteCategoryFiles(WebshopCategory,0);

        // Kategoriesymbol l�schen wenn vorhanden
        IF "Category Icon" THEN
          DeleteCategoryFiles(WebshopCategory,1);

        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[language_code]',"Language Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[line_no]',"Line No.");
        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec,FALSE,FALSE)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckCategoryFiles@1000000054(WebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303;Type@1000000002 : 'Picture,Icon,Description,Promotion Description,Description 2,Excerpt') Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      Field@1000000003 : Text[30];
      LogFile@1001 : File;
      LogResponse@1000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Dateien an Webshop-Kategorie pr�fen

      WITH WebshopCategory DO BEGIN
        CASE Type OF
          Type::Picture: Field := 'category_picture';
          Type::Icon: Field := 'category_icon';
          Type::Description: Field := 'category_description';
          Type::"Promotion Description": Field := 'promotion_description';
          Type::"Description 2": Field := 'category_description_2';
          Type::Excerpt: Field := 'category_description_excerpt';
        END;
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'field',Field);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
        IF GetRec = '' THEN
          Ok := FALSE
        ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
          Ok := TRUE
        ELSE
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetRec)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetRec);
            END ELSE
              ERROR('No Logfile specified');
          END;
        GET("Shop Code","Language Code","Line No.");
        CASE Type OF
          Type::Picture: "Category Picture" := Ok;
          Type::Icon: "Category Icon" := Ok;
          Type::Description: "Category Description" := Ok;
          Type::"Promotion Description": "Promotion Description" := Ok;
          Type::"Description 2": "Category Description 2" := Ok;
          Type::Excerpt: "Category Excerpt" := Ok;
        END;
        MODIFY;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteCategoryFiles@1000000053(WebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303;Type@1000000003 : 'Picture,Icon,Description,Promotion Description,Description 2,Excerpt');

    PROCEDURE UpdateWebshopItemCategory@1000000010(ParameterWebshopItemCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447305);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      WebshopItem@1000000003 : Record 5447304;
      WebshopCategory@1000000004 : Record 5447303;
      WebshopItemCategory@1000000002 : Record 5447305;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelne Webshopartikel-Kategorie �bertragen

      WITH WebshopItemCategory DO BEGIN

        GET(ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Item Shop Code",ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Item Language Code",
            ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Item No.",ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Category Shop Code",
            ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Category Language Code",ParameterWebshopItemCategory."Category Line No.");

        IF WebshopItem.GET("Item Shop Code","Item Language Code","Item No.") AND
           WebshopCategory.GET("Category Shop Code","Category Language Code","Category Line No.") AND
           (WebshopItem."Last Date Updated" <> 0DT) THEN BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item_has_category');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Item Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Item Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[item_no]',"Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[category_shop_code]',"Category Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[category_language_code]',"Category Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[category_line_no]',"Category Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sorting]',Sorting);
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          //Als �bertragen kennzeichnen
          "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);

        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCategoryManually@1000000163(ParameterWebshopCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      TempWebshopCategory@1000000005 : Record 5447303;
      NextCategory@1000000006 : Boolean;
      ParentLineNo@1000000003 : Integer;
      RootLineNo@1000000007 : Integer;
      Sorting@1000000004 : Integer;
      AttributeLink@1000000002 : Record 5447321;
      AttributeTranslation@1000000008 : Record 5447323;
      WebshopCategory@1000000009 : Record 5447303;
      WebshopItemCategory@1000000010 : Record 5447305;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelne Webshop Kategorie �bertragen

      WITH WebshopCategory DO BEGIN
        GET(ParameterWebshopCategory."Shop Code",ParameterWebshopCategory."Language Code",ParameterWebshopCategory."Line No.");
        IF Name <> '' THEN BEGIN

          TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");

          // Feld Sorting f�r Web ermitteln
          Sorting := 1;
          TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER(Level,'<=%1',Level);
          TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
          IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN REPEAT
            IF TempWebshopCategory.Level <> WebshopCategory.Level THEN
              NextCategory := TRUE;
            IF NOT NextCategory THEN
              Sorting := Sorting + 1;
          UNTIL TempWebshopCategory.NEXT(-1) = 0;

          // Feld "parent_line_no" f�r Web ermitteln
          ParentLineNo := 0;
          TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER(Level,'<%1',Level);
          TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
          IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
            ParentLineNo := TempWebshopCategory."Line No.";
          END;

          // Feld "root_line_no" f�r Web ermitteln
          RootLineNo := 0;
          IF Level > 1 THEN BEGIN
            TempWebshopCategory.SETRANGE(Level,1);
            TempWebshopCategory.SETFILTER("Line No.",'<%1',"Line No.");
            IF TempWebshopCategory.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
              RootLineNo := TempWebshopCategory."Line No.";
            END;
          END;

          // Kagegorie Teil 1 �bertragen
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sorting]',Sorting);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[root_line_no]',RootLineNo);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[parent_line_no]',ParentLineNo);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[name]',Name);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[level]',Level);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[code]',"Search Code");
          //AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_random_items]',"Show Random Items");
          //AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_random_items]',"No of Random Items");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_random_items]',0);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_random_items]',0);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_campain_items]',"Show Campain Items");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_campain_items]',"No of Campain Items");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[user_sorting]',"User Sorting");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[sort_items]',"Sort Items");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_all_items]',"Show All Items");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[show_sub_categorys]',"Show Sub Categorys");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',Active);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[validity_from]',"Validity from");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[validity_to]',"Validity to");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_active]',"Promtion Active");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_validity_from]',"Promtion Validity from");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_validity_to]',"Promtion Validity to");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[promotion_label]',"Promotion Label");
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

          CLEAR(CurrRec);

           // Kagegorie Teil 2 �bertragen
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_category');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[language_code]',"Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[meta_keywords]',"Meta Keywords");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[meta_description]',"Meta Description");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[site_titel]',"Site Title");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[search_query]',"Search Query");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[url]',GetCategoryURL);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[g_product_category]',"Google Product Category");
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


          // Zugeh�rige Merkmale aktualisieren
          AttributeLink.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeLink.Type::Category);
          AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Category Line No.","Line No.");
          AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code","Shop Code");
          AttributeLink.SETRANGE("Language Code","Language Code");
          IF AttributeLink.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateAttributeLink(AttributeLink);
            //Zugeh�rige �bersetzungen �bertragen
            AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE(Type,AttributeTranslation.Type::"Attribute Link");
            AttributeTranslation.SETRANGE("Attribute Link No.",AttributeLink."No.");
            IF AttributeTranslation.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
              UpdateAttributeTranslation(AttributeTranslation);
            UNTIL AttributeTranslation.NEXT = 0;
          UNTIL AttributeLink.NEXT = 0;

          //Als �bertragen kennzeichnen
          "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);

          //Zugeh�rige Artikelverkn�pfungen wo n�tig mit �bertragen
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Shop Code","Shop Code");
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Language Code","Language Code");
          WebshopItemCategory.SETRANGE("Category Line No.","Line No.");
          IF WebshopItemCategory.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            IF (WebshopItemCategory."Last Date Updated" = 0DT) OR (WebshopItemCategory."Last Date Updated" < WebshopItemCategory.
      "Last Date Modified") THEN
              UpdateWebshopItemCategory(WebshopItemCategory);
          UNTIL WebshopItemCategory.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE "--- Debitoren ---"@1000000034();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllCustomer@1000000033(CountRecords@1000000002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Customer@1000000000 : Record 18;
    BEGIN
      // Alle Debitoren �bertragen

      WITH Customer DO BEGIN
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        IF NOT WebshopSetup."Update All Customer" THEN
          SETFILTER("Webshop Login Sent",'<>%1',0DT);
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            IF ("Webshop Last Date Updated" < "Webshop Last Date Modified") OR ("Webshop Last Date Updated" = 0DT) THEN
              UpdateCustomer(Customer);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCustomer@1000000031(ParameterCustomer@1000000000 : Record 18);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      CustomerActive@1000000004 : Boolean;
      BillToCustomer@1000000006 : Record 18;
      CustInvoiceDisc@1000000007 : Record 19;
      PaymentTerms@1000000008 : Record 3;
      Customer@1000000001 : Record 18;
      ShipToAddress@1000000002 : Record 222;
      tmpShipToAddress@1000000005 : TEMPORARY Record 222;
    BEGIN
      // Debitor �bertragen

      WITH Customer DO BEGIN
        GET(ParameterCustomer."No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_customer');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[customer_no]',"No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shop_code]',"Webshop Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[language_code]',"Webshop Language Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[name]',Name);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[name_2]',"Name 2");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[address]',Address);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[address_2]',"Address 2");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[post_code]',"Post Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[city]',City);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[country]',"Country/Region Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[phone_no]',"Phone No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[fax_no]',"Fax No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[email]',"E-Mail");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[homepage]',"Home Page");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[salesperson_code]',"Salesperson Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[currency_code]',"Currency Code");

        IF "Bill-to Customer No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
          BillToCustomer.GET("Bill-to Customer No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customer_price_group]',BillToCustomer."Customer Price Group");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customer_disc_group]',BillToCustomer."Customer Disc. Group");
          IF PaymentTerms.GET(BillToCustomer."Payment Terms Code") THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[payment_terms]',PaymentTerms.Description);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customer_price_group]',"Customer Price Group");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customer_disc_group]',"Customer Disc. Group");
          IF PaymentTerms.GET("Payment Terms Code") THEN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'data[payment_terms]',PaymentTerms.Description);
        END;
        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


        CLEAR(CurrRec);

        //FK 17.01.2013 - Aufgesplittet wegen m�glicher �berschreitung von 1024 Zeichen
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_customer');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[customer_no]',"No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_customer_no]',BillToCustomer."No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name]',BillToCustomer.Name);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name_2]',BillToCustomer."Name 2");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address]',BillToCustomer.Address);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address_2]',BillToCustomer."Address 2");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_post_code]',BillToCustomer."Post Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_city]',BillToCustomer.City);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_country]',BillToCustomer."Country/Region Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[invoice_disc_code]',"Invoice Disc. Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',("Webshop Active" AND NOT (Blocked = Blocked::All)));

        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);


        "Webshop Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;

        //�bertragen der Liefer-An Adressen inkl. Std. Debitor (Rechnungs)adresse
        tmpShipToAddress.DELETEALL(FALSE);
        CLEAR(tmpShipToAddress);
        ShipToAddress.SETRANGE("Customer No.","No.");
        IF ShipToAddress.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
          tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
          tmpShipToAddress.TRANSFERFIELDS(ShipToAddress);
          tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
        UNTIL ShipToAddress.NEXT = 0;

        IF "Bill-to Customer No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
          BillToCustomer.GET("Bill-to Customer No.");
          tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
          tmpShipToAddress."Customer No." := "No.";
          tmpShipToAddress.Code := 'DEFAULT';
          tmpShipToAddress.Name := BillToCustomer.Name;
          tmpShipToAddress."Name 2" := BillToCustomer."Name 2";
          tmpShipToAddress.Address := BillToCustomer.Address;
          tmpShipToAddress."Address 2" := BillToCustomer."Address 2";
          tmpShipToAddress.City := BillToCustomer.City;
          tmpShipToAddress."Post Code" := BillToCustomer."Post Code";
          tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
          tmpShipToAddress."Customer No." := "No.";
          tmpShipToAddress.Code := 'DEFAULT';
          tmpShipToAddress.Name := Name;
          tmpShipToAddress."Name 2" := "Name 2";
          tmpShipToAddress.Address := Address;
          tmpShipToAddress."Address 2" := "Address 2";
          tmpShipToAddress.City := City;
          tmpShipToAddress."Post Code" := "Post Code";
          tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
        END;

        tmpShipToAddress.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE);
        REPEAT
          UpdateShipToAddress(tmpShipToAddress);
        UNTIL tmpShipToAddress.NEXT = 0;

        //Webshop Debitor Verkn�pfung
        UpdateWebshopCustomerLink(Customer);


        MODIFY(FALSE);
        COMMIT;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteCustomer@1000000030(Customer@1000000000 : Record 18);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Einzelnen Debitor l�schen

      WITH Customer DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_customer');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[customer_no]',"No.");
        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec,FALSE,FALSE)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec);

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendCustomerLogin@1000000029(Customer@1000000002 : Record 18);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      ShipToAddress@1000000000 : Record 222;
      Language@1000000003 : Record 5447302;
      BillToCustomer@1000000004 : Record 18;
      tmpShipToAddress@1000000005 : TEMPORARY Record 222;
    BEGIN
      // Zugangsdaten an Debitor versenden

      WITH Customer DO BEGIN

        // Aktiv setzten
        "Webshop Active" := TRUE;
        MODIFY(FALSE);
        Language.GET("Webshop Shop Code","Webshop Language Code");
        Language.TESTFIELD("E-Mail Login");

        // Debitor aktualisieren
        UpdateCustomer(Customer);
        GET("No.");
        // Einmaliges �bertragen der Liefer-An Adressen inkl. Std. Debitor (Rechnungs)adresse
        tmpShipToAddress.DELETEALL(FALSE);
        CLEAR(tmpShipToAddress);
        ShipToAddress.SETRANGE("Customer No.","No.");
        IF ShipToAddress.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
          tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
          tmpShipToAddress.TRANSFERFIELDS(ShipToAddress);
          tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
        UNTIL ShipToAddress.NEXT = 0;

        IF Customer."Bill-to Customer No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
          BillToCustomer.GET("Bill-to Customer No.");
          tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
          tmpShipToAddress."Customer No." := Customer."No.";
          tmpShipToAddress.Code := 'DEFAULT';
          tmpShipToAddress.Name := BillToCustomer.Name;
          tmpShipToAddress."Name 2" := BillToCustomer."Name 2";
          tmpShipToAddress.Address := BillToCustomer.Address;
          tmpShipToAddress."Address 2" := BillToCustomer."Address 2";
          tmpShipToAddress.City := BillToCustomer.City;
          tmpShipToAddress."Post Code" := BillToCustomer."Post Code";
          tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          tmpShipToAddress.INIT;
          tmpShipToAddress."Customer No." := Customer."No.";
          tmpShipToAddress.Code := 'DEFAULT';
          tmpShipToAddress.Name := Customer.Name;
          tmpShipToAddress."Name 2" := Customer."Name 2";
          tmpShipToAddress.Address := Customer.Address;
          tmpShipToAddress."Address 2" := Customer."Address 2";
          tmpShipToAddress.City := Customer.City;
          tmpShipToAddress."Post Code" := Customer."Post Code";
          tmpShipToAddress.INSERT(FALSE);
        END;

        tmpShipToAddress.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE);
        REPEAT
          UpdateShipToAddress(tmpShipToAddress);
        UNTIL tmpShipToAddress.NEXT = 0;



        // E-Mail mit Kennwort �bertragen
        AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no',"No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'email',"Webshop Login E-Mail");
        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('send_customer_login',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('send_customer_login',CurrRec);


        // Aktualisierungsdatum setzten
        "Webshop Login Sent" := CURRENTDATETIME;
        MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE OpenCustomerLogin@1000000032(Customer@1000000000 : Record 18) SessionID : Text[200];
    VAR
      Shop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      Text001@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=F�r Debitor %1 ist kein Webzugang angelegt';
    BEGIN
      // Hauptuser eines Kunden anmelden

      Shop.GET(Customer."Webshop Shop Code");
      Shop.TESTFIELD("Frontend Link");
      IF Customer."Webshop Login Sent" = 0DT THEN
        ERROR(Text001,Customer."No.");
      AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no',Customer."No.");
      SessionID := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('open_customer_login',CurrRec);
      HYPERLINK(Shop."Frontend Link" + '/?sid=' + SessionID);
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateShipToAddress@1000000074(VAR ParameterShipToAddress@1000000000 : TEMPORARY Record 222);

      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Liefer-an Adresse �bertragen - �bergabe als Referenz auf tempor�ren Datensatz

        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
