      CouponOrderAmountVAT3@1000000091 : Decimal;
      ResetRec@1000000096 : Text[1024];
      ShippingCoupon@1000000097 : Boolean;
      SalespersonCode@1000000098 : Code[10];
      DimSetID@1000000099 : Integer;
      DimMgt@1000000100 : Codeunit 408;
      DimValue@1000000103 : Record 349;
      DimValueID@1000000102 : Integer;
      Text003@1000000108 : TextConst 'DEU=Gutscheinrabatt Code %1;ENU=<Gutscheinrabatt Code %1>';
      DocumentDimension@1000000101 : Record 357;
      VatPostingSetup@1000000109 : Record 325;
      SubscriptionCode@1000000113 : Code[30];
      SubscriptionCustLineNo@1000000114 : Integer;
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000115 : Record 5447340;
      Item@1000000116 : Record 27;
      FilterUserEmail@1000000120 : Text[80];
      FilterBillToPostCode@1000000119 : Text[30];
      FilterBillToName@1000000118 : Text[50];
      FilterBillToAddress@1000000117 : Text[50];
      FilterBillToCountryCode@1000000124 : Text[50];
      CustomerTemplateCode@1000000121 : Code[10];
      PoolCustomerNo@1000000122 : Code[20];
      VatId@1000000123 : Text[30];
      SalesOrderText@1000000125 : Text[80];
    BEGIN
      // Alle neuen Bestellungen abrufen
      Delimiter := '-;-';

      WITH SalesHeader DO REPEAT
        NextSalesHeader := FALSE;

        // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 1 anfordern
        CLEAR(CurrRec);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','id,shop_code,language_code,order_no,customer_no,user_name,user_email,order_date,' +
      'requested_delivery_date,ship_to_name,ship_to_name_2,ship_to_address,ship_to_address_2,ship_to_post_code,'+
                               'ship_to_city,ship_to_country,ship_to_contact,ship_to_telephone_no,payment_transaction_id,pay_id,' +
                               'process_payment,payment_processed,shop_customer_id,salesperson_code');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[order_error]',FALSE);
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
        IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN

          // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 1 in Variablen speichern
          EVALUATE(SalesHeaderId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShopCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(LanguageCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(OrderNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(CustomerNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(UserName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(UserEmail,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(OderDate,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(RequestedDeliveryDate,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToName2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToAddress,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToAddress2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToPostCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToCity,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToCountryCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToContact,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToPhoneNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(PaymentTransactionID,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(PaymentID,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ProcessPayment,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(PaymentProcessed,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(CustomerId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(SalespersonCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          IF (ShopCode <> '') AND (LanguageCode <> '') AND (OrderNo <>'') AND (UserEmail <> '') THEN BEGIN
            // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 2 anfordern
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_header');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','user_email,user_phone_no,user_salutation,bill_to_customer_no,bill_to_name,bill_to_name_2,' +
                                    'bill_to_address,bill_to_address_2,bill_to_post_code,bill_to_city,bill_to_country,your_reference,' +
                                    'your_comment,total,shipping_option_line_no,payment_option_line_no,currency_code,drop_shipment,' +
                                    'shipping_advice,coupon_amount,value_coupon,coupon_code,shipping_coupon,newsletter_registration');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[order_error]',FALSE);
            GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
            IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
              // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 2 in Variablen speichern
              EVALUATE(UserEmail,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(UserPhoneNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(UserSalutation,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(BillToCustomerNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(BillToName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(BillToName2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(BillToAddress,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(BillToAddress2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(BillToPostCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(BillToCity,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(BillToCountryCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(YourReference,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(YourComment,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(OnlineAmount,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(ShippingOptionLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(PaymentOptionLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(CurrencyCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(DropShipment,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(ShippingAdvice,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(CouponDiscountAmount,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(ValueCoupon,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(CouponCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(ShippingCoupon,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(NewsletterRegistration,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));

              // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 3 anfordern (Billpaydaten) ---
              CLEAR(CurrRec);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_header');
              AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','bp_acc_owner,bp_acc_nr,bp_acc_iban,bp_bank,bp_invoice_ref,bon_check,sepa,dc_order,' +
                                      'shipping_cost,subscription_code,subscription_cust_line_no,vat_id');
              AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
              AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[order_error]',FALSE);
              GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);

              IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 3 in Variablen speichern
                EVALUATE(BpAccOwner,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(BpAccNr,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(BpAccIBAN,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(BpBank,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(BpInvoiceRef,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(BonCheck,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(Sepa,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(DCOrder,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(ShippingCost,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(SubscriptionCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(SubscriptionCustLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                EVALUATE(VatId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
                // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 3 anfordern (Billpaydaten) +++



                // Einrichtung ermitteln
                WebshopSetup.GET;
                Shop.GET(ShopCode);
                Language.GET(Shop.Code,LanguageCode);
                CLEAR(PaymentOption);
                CLEAR(ShippingOption);
                CLEAR(BillToCountry);
                CLEAR(ShipToCountry);
                IF Shop."Shop Typ" IN [Shop."Shop Typ"::b2c,Shop."Shop Typ"::marketplace] THEN BEGIN
                  IF PaymentOptionLineNo <> 0 THEN
                    PaymentOption.GET(Shop.Code,Language.Code,PaymentOptionLineNo);
                  IF ShippingOptionLineNo <> 0 THEN
                    ShippingOption.GET(Shop.Code,Language.Code,ShippingOptionLineNo);
                  IF BillToCountryCode <> '' THEN
                    BillToCountry.GET(Shop.Code,Language.Code,BillToCountryCode);
                  IF ShipToCountryCode <> '' THEN
                    ShipToCountry.GET(Shop.Code,Language.Code,ShipToCountryCode);
                END;

                CASE Shop."Typ Discounts/Costs" OF
                  Shop."Typ Discounts/Costs"::Item: SalesLineTyp := 1;
                  Shop."Typ Discounts/Costs"::"G/L Account": SalesLineTyp := 2;
                  Shop."Typ Discounts/Costs"::Resource: SalesLineTyp := 3;
                  Shop."Typ Discounts/Costs"::Charge: SalesLineTyp := 4;
                END;

                // Debitor ermitteln f�r Endkundenshops
                CLEAR(Customer);
                IF Shop."Shop Typ" IN [Shop."Shop Typ"::b2b,Shop."Shop Typ"::catalog, Shop."Shop Typ"::salesperson] THEN BEGIN
                  Customer.GET(CustomerNo);
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  ExistingCustomer := FALSE;
                  // Sammeldebitor verwenden
                  IF Shop."Use Pool Customer" THEN BEGIN

                    IF (BillToCountry."Enable Reverse Charge") THEN BEGIN
                      BillToCountry.TESTFIELD("Reverse Charge Pool Cust. No.");
                      PoolCustomerNo := BillToCountry."Reverse Charge Pool Cust. No.";
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      BillToCountry.TESTFIELD("Pool Customer No.");
                      PoolCustomerNo := BillToCountry."Pool Customer No.";
                    END;

                    Customer.GET(PoolCustomerNo);
                    ExistingCustomer := TRUE;
                  END;
                  // Debitor aus Webshop �bergen
                  IF (CustomerNo <> '') AND NOT ExistingCustomer THEN BEGIN
                    IF Customer.GET(CustomerNo) THEN
                      ExistingCustomer := TRUE;
                  END;

                  FilterUserEmail := WebshopManagement.FilterChars(UserEmail);
                  FilterBillToPostCode := WebshopManagement.FilterChars(BillToPostCode);
                  FilterBillToName := WebshopManagement.FilterChars(BillToName);
                  FilterBillToAddress := WebshopManagement.FilterChars(BillToAddress);
                  FilterBillToCountryCode := WebshopManagement.FilterChars(BillToCountryCode);

                  // Dublettenpr�fung E-Mail/PLZ/CountryCode
                  IF NOT ExistingCustomer THEN BEGIN
                    Customer.RESET;
                    Customer.SETRANGE(Blocked,Customer.Blocked::" ");
                    Customer.SETFILTER("E-Mail",'@*'+FilterUserEmail+'*');
                    Customer.SETFILTER("Post Code",'@*'+FilterBillToPostCode+'*');
                    Customer.SETFILTER("Country/Region Code",'@*'+FilterBillToCountryCode+'*');
                    IF Customer.FINDLAST THEN
                      ExistingCustomer := TRUE;
                  END;
                  // Dublettenpr�fung E-Mail/Name/CountryCode
                  IF NOT ExistingCustomer THEN BEGIN
                    Customer.RESET;
                    Customer.SETRANGE(Blocked,Customer.Blocked::" ");
                    Customer.SETFILTER("E-Mail",'@*'+FilterUserEmail+'*');
                    Customer.SETFILTER(Name,'@*'''+FilterBillToName+'''*');
                    Customer.SETFILTER("Country/Region Code",'@*'+FilterBillToCountryCode+'*');
                    IF Customer.FINDLAST THEN
                      ExistingCustomer := TRUE;
                  END;
                  // Dublettenpr�fung Name/Adresse/PLZ/CountryCode
                  IF NOT ExistingCustomer THEN BEGIN
                    Customer.RESET;
                    Customer.SETRANGE(Blocked,Customer.Blocked::" ");
                    Customer.SETFILTER(Name,'@*'''+FilterBillToName+'''*');
                    Customer.SETFILTER(Address,'@*'''+FilterBillToAddress+'''*');
                    Customer.SETFILTER("Post Code",'@*'+FilterBillToPostCode+'*');
                    Customer.SETFILTER("Country/Region Code",'@*'+FilterBillToCountryCode+'*');
                    IF Customer.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
                      ExistingCustomer := TRUE;
                    END;
                  END;
                  Customer.RESET;

                  // Debitor anlegen wenn nicht vorhanden
                  IF NOT ExistingCustomer THEN BEGIN
                    Customer.INIT;
                    IF Shop."Customer No. Series Code" <> '' THEN
                      Customer."No. Series" := Shop."Customer No. Series Code";
                    Customer."No." := '';
                    Customer.INSERT(TRUE);
                    Customer."No. Series" := '';
                    // Werte aus Debitorenvorlage �bernehmen

                    IF BillToCountry."Enable Reverse Charge" THEN BEGIN
                      BillToCountry.TESTFIELD("Reverse Charge Cust. Tmp. Code");
                      CustomerTemplateCode := BillToCountry."Reverse Charge Cust. Tmp. Code";
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      BillToCountry.TESTFIELD("Customer Template Code");
                      CustomerTemplateCode := BillToCountry."Customer Template Code";
                    END;

                    IF CustomerTemplate.GET(CustomerTemplateCode) THEN BEGIN
                      Customer."Customer Posting Group" := CustomerTemplate."Customer Posting Group";
                      Customer."Customer Price Group" := CustomerTemplate."Customer Price Group";
                      Customer."Invoice Disc. Code" := CustomerTemplate."Invoice Disc. Code";
                      Customer."Customer Disc. Group" := CustomerTemplate."Customer Disc. Group";
                      Customer."Allow Line Disc." := CustomerTemplate."Allow Line Disc.";
                      Customer."Gen. Bus. Posting Group" := CustomerTemplate."Gen. Bus. Posting Group";
                      Customer."VAT Bus. Posting Group" := CustomerTemplate."VAT Bus. Posting Group";
                      Customer."Payment Terms Code" := CustomerTemplate."Payment Terms Code";
                      Customer."Payment Method Code" := CustomerTemplate."Payment Method Code";
                      Customer."Shipment Method Code" := CustomerTemplate."Shipment Method Code";
                      DefaultDim.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Customer Template");
                      DefaultDim.SETRANGE("No.",CustomerTemplate.Code);
                      IF DefaultDim.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                        CLEAR(DefaultDim2);
                        DefaultDim2.INIT;
                        DefaultDim2.VALIDATE("Table ID",DATABASE::Customer);
                        DefaultDim2."No." := Customer."No.";
                        DefaultDim2.VALIDATE("Dimension Code",DefaultDim."Dimension Code");
                        DefaultDim2.VALIDATE("Dimension Value Code",DefaultDim."Dimension Value Code");
                        DefaultDim2."Value Posting" := DefaultDim."Value Posting";
                        DefaultDim2.INSERT(TRUE);
                       UNTIL DefaultDim.NEXT = 0;
                    END;
                    IF BillToCountry."Customer Salesperson Code" <> '' THEN
                       Customer."Salesperson Code" := BillToCountry."Customer Salesperson Code";
                    IF BillToCountry."Customer Reminder Terms Code" <> '' THEN
                      Customer."Reminder Terms Code" := BillToCountry."Customer Reminder Terms Code";
                    IF BillToCountry."Customer Location Code" <> '' THEN
                      Customer."Location Code" := BillToCountry."Customer Location Code";
                    Customer."Prices Including VAT" := Shop."Prices Including VAT";
                    IF CurrencyCode <> '' THEN
                      Customer."Currency Code" := CurrencyCode;
                    IF VatId <> '' THEN
                      Customer."VAT Registration No." := VatId;
                  END;
                  IF NOT Shop."Use Pool Customer" THEN BEGIN
                    Customer.VALIDATE(Name,BillToName);
                    Customer.VALIDATE("Name 2",BillToName2);
                    Customer.VALIDATE(Address,BillToAddress);
                    Customer.VALIDATE("Address 2",BillToAddress2);
                    Customer.VALIDATE("Webshop Active",TRUE);
                    Customer.VALIDATE("Webshop Shop Code",Shop.Code);
                    Customer.VALIDATE("Webshop Language Code",LanguageCode);
                    Customer.City := BillToCity;
                    Customer."Post Code" := BillToPostCode;
                    Customer.VALIDATE("Country/Region Code",BillToCountryCode);
                    IF Language."NAV Language Code" <> '' THEN
                      Customer.VALIDATE("Language Code",Language."NAV Language Code");
                    Customer.VALIDATE("E-Mail",UserEmail);
                    Customer.VALIDATE("Phone No.",UserPhoneNo);
                    Customer."Webshop Active" := TRUE;
                    Customer.MODIFY(TRUE);
                    UpdateContFromCust.OnModify(Customer);

                    // �nderungen im Kontakt
                    ContactBusinessRelation.SETRANGE("Link to Table",ContactBusinessRelation."Link to Table"::Customer);
                    ContactBusinessRelation.SETRANGE("No.",Customer."No.");
                    IF ContactBusinessRelation.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
                      IF Contact.GET(ContactBusinessRelation."Contact No.") THEN BEGIN
                        CASE UserSalutation OF
                          0: Contact."Salutation Code" := Shop."Salutation Code Company";
                          1: Contact."Salutation Code" := Shop."Salutation Code Male";
                          2: Contact."Salutation Code" := Shop."Salutation Code Female";
                        END;
                        IF (UserSalutation > 0) AND (STRPOS(Contact.Name,' ') <> 0) THEN BEGIN
                          Contact."First Name" := COPYSTR(Contact.Name,1,STRPOS(Contact.Name,' '));
                          Contact.Surname := COPYSTR(Contact.Name,STRPOS(Contact.Name,' ')+1);
                        END;
                        Contact.MODIFY(TRUE);
                      END;
                    END;
                  END;
                END;

                IF (COPYSTR(CustomerNo,1,4) = 'TEMP') AND (NOT Shop."Use Pool Customer") THEN BEGIN
                  // Neue Kundennummer �bertragen in shop_customer und shop_user und shop_sales_header
                  CLEAR(SetRec);
                  AddVar(SetRec,'customer_id',CustomerId);
                  AddVar(SetRec,'temp_customer_no',CustomerNo);
                  AddVar(SetRec,'customer_no',Customer."No.");
                  WebshopManagement.SendRequest('replace_temp_customer',SetRec);
                  CLEAR(SetRec);
                  COMMIT;
                END;

                //Newsletter-Empf�nger anlegen, wenn gew�nscht
                IF NewsletterRegistration = 1 THEN BEGIN
                  CLEAR(SetRec);
                  AddVar(SetRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
                  AddVar(SetRec,'sales_header_id',SalesHeaderId);
                  WebshopManagement.SendRequest('send_newsletter_confirm_mail',SetRec);
                END;

                // Bankverbindung anlegen
                ShowBankAccount := FALSE;
                IF (BankAccountNo <> '') AND (PaymentOption.Checkout = PaymentOption.Checkout::Lastschrift) THEN BEGIN
                  CustomerBankAccount.SETRANGE("Customer No.",Customer."No.");
                  CustomerBankAccount.SETRANGE("Bank Account No.",BankAccountNo);
                  CustomerBankAccount.SETRANGE("Bank Branch No.",BankBranchNo);
                  IF NOT CustomerBankAccount.FINDLAST THEN BEGIN
                    IF NOT CustomerBankAccount.GET(Customer."No.",BankAccountNo) THEN BEGIN
                      CustomerBankAccount.INIT;
                      CustomerBankAccount."Customer No." := Customer."No.";
                      CustomerBankAccount.Code := BankAccountNo;
                      CustomerBankAccount.INSERT(TRUE);
                    END;
                    CustomerBankAccount.VALIDATE("Bank Branch No.",BankBranchNo);
                    CustomerBankAccount.VALIDATE("Bank Account No.",BankAccountNo);
                    IF CustomerBankAccount.Name = '' THEN
                      CustomerBankAccount.VALIDATE(Name,BankName);
                    CustomerBankAccount.MODIFY(TRUE);
                  END;
                  IF CustomerBankAccount.COUNT > 1 THEN
                    ShowBankAccount := TRUE;
                END;

                // Gesperrten Kunden entsperren
                CustomerBlockedState := 0;
                IF Customer.Blocked <> Customer.Blocked::" " THEN BEGIN
                  CustomerBlockedState := Customer.Blocked;
                  Customer.Blocked := Customer.Blocked::" ";
                  Customer.MODIFY(FALSE);
                END;

                //Abo-Kundenverkn?pfung abholen wo n�tig
                IF SubscriptionCode <> '' THEN
                  GetSubscCustLinkByPrimary(SubscriptionCode,Customer."No.",UserEmail,SubscriptionCustLineNo);


                // Verkaufsangebot oder Verkaufsauftrag in NAV erstellen
                INIT;
                CASE Shop."Weborder Typ" OF
                  Shop."Weborder Typ"::Order: BEGIN
                    VALIDATE("Document Type","Document Type"::Order);
                    IF NOT Shop."Use Online Order No." THEN
                      "No. Series" := Shop."Order No. Series Code";
                  END;
                  Shop."Weborder Typ"::Quote: BEGIN
                    VALIDATE("Document Type","Document Type"::Quote);
                    IF NOT Shop."Use Online Order No." THEN
                      "No. Series" := Shop."Quote No. Series Code";
                  END;
                END;

                IF Shop."Use Online Order No." THEN
                  "No." := OrderNo
                ELSE
                  "No." := '';

                INSERT(TRUE);
                "No. Series" := '';

                VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer No.",Customer."No.");
                VALIDATE("Sell-to Contact",UserName);
                IF Shop."Use Pool Customer" THEN BEGIN
                  VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer Name",BillToName);
                  VALIDATE("Sell-to Customer Name 2",BillToName2);
                  VALIDATE("Sell-to Address",BillToAddress);
                  VALIDATE("Sell-to Address 2",BillToAddress2);
                  "Sell-to Post Code" := BillToPostCode;
                  "Sell-to City" := BillToCity;
                  VALIDATE("Sell-to Country/Region Code",BillToCountryCode);
                VALIDATE("Sell-to Country/Region Code",BillToCountryCode);
                  VALIDATE("Bill-to Name",BillToName);
                  VALIDATE("Bill-to Name 2",BillToName2);
                  VALIDATE("Bill-to Address",BillToAddress);
                  VALIDATE("Bill-to Address 2",BillToAddress2);
                  "Bill-to Post Code" := BillToPostCode;
                  "Bill-to City" := BillToCity;
                  VALIDATE("Bill-to Country/Region Code",BillToCountryCode);
                VALIDATE("Bill-to Country/Region Code",BillToCountryCode);
                END;
                VALIDATE("Ship-to Name",ShipToName);
                VALIDATE("Ship-to Name 2",ShipToName2);
                VALIDATE("Ship-to Address",ShipToAddress);
                VALIDATE("Ship-to Address 2",ShipToAddress2);
                "Ship-to Post Code" := ShipToPostCode;
                "Ship-to City" := ShipToCity;
                VALIDATE("Ship-to Country/Region Code",ShipToCountryCode);
                VALIDATE("Ship-to Contact",ShipToContact);
                VALIDATE("Sell-to Country/Region Code",ShipToCountryCode);
                VALIDATE("Requested Delivery Date",RequestedDeliveryDate);
                VALIDATE("Your Reference",YourReference);
                "Webshop Order No." := OrderNo;
                "Webshop Sync Date" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                "Webshop Shop Code" := ShopCode;
                "Webshop Language Code" := LanguageCode;
                "Webshop Pay. Opt. Line No." := PaymentOption."Line No.";
                "Webshop Ship. Opt. Line No." := ShippingOption."Line No.";
                "Webshop Original Amount" := OnlineAmount;
                "Webshop Payment Transaction Id" := PaymentTransactionID;
                "Webshop Payment ID" := PaymentID;
                "Webshop Process Payment" := ProcessPayment;
                "Webshop Payment Processed" := PaymentProcessed;
                "Webshop Clear Payment" := PaymentOption."Auto Clearing";
                "Webshop Subscription Code" := SubscriptionCode;
                "Webshop Sub. Cust. Line No." := SubscriptionCustLineNo;
                "Webshop User E-Mail" := UserEmail;

                IF Shop."Responsibility Center" <> '' THEN
                  VALIDATE("Responsibility Center",Shop."Responsibility Center");

                IF Language."E-Mail Order 2" <> '' THEN
                  "Webshop Send Order Mail 2" := TRUE;
                IF Language."E-Mail Order 3" <> '' THEN
                  "Webshop Send Order Mail 3" := TRUE;
                IF Language."E-Mail Order 4" <> '' THEN
                  "Webshop Send Order Mail 4" := TRUE;
                IF CurrencyCode <> '' THEN
                  VALIDATE("Currency Code",CurrencyCode);
                IF Shop."Campain No." <> '' THEN
                  VALIDATE("Campaign No.",Shop."Campain No.");
                IF (Shop."Default Salesperson Code" <> '') AND (NOT (Shop."Shop Typ" = Shop."Shop Typ"::salesperson)) THEN
                  "Salesperson Code" := Shop."Default Salesperson Code"
                ELSE IF (Shop."Shop Typ" = Shop."Shop Typ"::salesperson) AND (STRLEN(SalespersonCode)>0) THEN
                  "Salesperson Code" := SalespersonCode;
                IF PaymentOption."Payment Terms Code" <> '' THEN
                  "Payment Terms Code" := PaymentOption."Payment Terms Code";
                IF PaymentOption."Payment Method Code" <> '' THEN
                  "Payment Method Code" := PaymentOption."Payment Method Code";
                IF ShippingOption."Shipping Agent Code" <> '' THEN
                  "Shipping Agent Code" := ShippingOption."Shipping Agent Code";
                IF ShippingOption."Shipping Agent Service Code" <> '' THEN
                  "Shipping Agent Service Code" := ShippingOption."Shipping Agent Service Code";
                IF ShippingOption."Shipment Method Code" <> '' THEN
                   "Shipment Method Code" := ShippingOption."Shipment Method Code";
                IF Shop."Cust. Price Group Base Price" <> '' THEN
                  VALIDATE("Customer Price Group",Shop."Cust. Price Group Base Price");
                IF Shop."Cust. Disc. Group Base Price" <> '' THEN
                  VALIDATE("Customer Disc. Group",Shop."Cust. Disc. Group Base Price");

                //PH 01.05.2013 Bonit�t speichern
                SalesHeader."Webshop Payment Credit Worth" := BonCheck;

                IF Shop."Invoice No. Series Code" <> '' THEN
                  VALIDATE("Posting No. Series",Shop."Invoice No. Series Code");

                IF Shop."Shipment No. Series Code" <> '' THEN
                  VALIDATE("Shipping No. Series",Shop."Shipment No. Series Code");

                MODIFY(TRUE);


                // Dimension f�r Angebot/Auftrag setzten
                IF Language."Report Dimension Value" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  IF NOT DocumentDimension.GET(DATABASE::"Sales Header","Document Type","No.",0,Language."Report Dimension") THEN BEGIN
                    DocumentDimension.INIT;
                    DocumentDimension.VALIDATE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Sales Header");
                    DocumentDimension.VALIDATE("Document Type","Document Type");
                    DocumentDimension.VALIDATE("Document No.","No.");
                    DocumentDimension.VALIDATE("Dimension Code",Language."Report Dimension");
                    DocumentDimension.VALIDATE("Dimension Value Code",Language."Report Dimension Value");
                    DocumentDimension.INSERT(TRUE);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    DocumentDimension.VALIDATE("Dimension Value Code",Language."Report Dimension Value");
                    DocumentDimension.MODIFY(TRUE);
                  END;
                END;



                // Kommentar einf�gen
                i := 0;
                CommentLineNo := 10000;
                WHILE i <= 320 DO BEGIN
                  IF COPYSTR(YourComment,i+1,80) <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    SalesCommentLine.INIT;
                    SalesCommentLine."Document Type" := "Document Type";
                    SalesCommentLine."No." := SalesHeader."No.";
                    SalesCommentLine."Line No." := CommentLineNo;
                    SalesCommentLine.Comment := COPYSTR(YourComment,i+1,80);
                    SalesCommentLine.INSERT(TRUE);
                  END;
                  CommentLineNo += 10000;
                  i+= 80;
                END;

                // Bestellnummer einf�gen
                IF Language."Online Order No Text" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  StandardText.GET(Language."Online Order No Text");
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,Language."Online Order No Text",'',0,0,
                                  STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,OrderNo),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
                END;

                // Referenztext einf�gen
                IF YourReference <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  IF Language."Your Reference Text" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    StandardText.GET(Language."Your Reference Text");
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,Language."Your Reference Text",'',0,0,
                                      STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,YourReference),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
                  END;
                END;

                // Leeerzeile einf�gen
                WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);

                // Artikelzeilen zur�cksetzen
                CLEAR(ResetRec);
                AddVar(ResetRec,'online_order_no',OrderNo);
                WebshopManagement.SendRequest('reset_sales_lines',ResetRec);

                IF (SubscriptionCode <> '')
                    AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(SubscriptionCode))
                    AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem)
                    AND (NOT(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::Once))
                    AND (NOT(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::PerTurn)) THEN BEGIN

                    Item.GET(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Item No. Subscription Item");

                    //SalesLine f. Artikel mit 100% Zeilenrabatt
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLine.Type::Item,Item."No.",'',1,
                                                      WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Price per Billing Interval",'',FALSE,
                                                      100,Item."Sales Unit of Measure",FALSE,FALSE);

                    //Leerzeile
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);

                    //Tats�chliche SalesLine schreiben - G/L Account
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,2,Shop."G/L Account Subscriptions",'',1,
                                                      WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Price per Billing Interval",Text002,FALSE,
                                                      0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
                    OrderAmount := WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Price per Billing Interval";

                END ELSE BEGIN

                  // Artikelzeilen einf�gen
                  CLEAR(OrderAmount);
                  GetSalesLines(SalesHeader,SalesLine,OrderAmount);

                END;


                IF (SubscriptionCode <> '') AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(SubscriptionCode)) AND
                    (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption) THEN BEGIN
                  "Webshop Subscr. Paym. Order" := TRUE;
                  "Webshop Subscr. Shipping Order" := TRUE;
                END;

                // Leeerzeile einf�gen
                WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);

                //MwSt-S�tze als Texte holen;
                IF VatPostingSetup.GET(Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group",Shop."VAT Identifier 1") THEN
                  VATGroupHighPercentageText := FORMAT(VatPostingSetup."VAT %") + '%';
                IF VatPostingSetup.GET(Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group",Shop."VAT Identifier 2") THEN
                  VATGroupMediumPercentageText := FORMAT(VatPostingSetup."VAT %") + '%';
                IF VatPostingSetup.GET(Shop."VAT Bus. Posting Group",Shop."VAT Identifier 3") THEN
                  VATGroupLowPercentageText := FORMAT(VatPostingSetup."VAT %") + '%';


                // Geb�hren Versandart einf�gen
                IF NOT DCOrder THEN BEGIN
                  IF (ShippingOption."Coupon Shipping Cost" > 0) AND (ShippingCost > 0) AND (ShippingCoupon) THEN BEGIN
                    IF ((OnlineAmount-ShippingCost) < ShippingOption.Exemption) OR (ShippingOption.Exemption = 0) THEN BEGIN
                      Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No. Shipping Cost");
                      WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Shipping Cost",'',1,
                                    ShippingOption."Coupon Shipping Cost",ShippingOption.Description,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                    END;
                  END ELSE IF (ShippingOption."Shipping Cost" > 0) AND (ShippingCost > 0) THEN BEGIN
                    IF ((OnlineAmount-ShippingCost) < ShippingOption.Exemption) OR (ShippingOption.Exemption = 0) THEN BEGIN
                      Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No. Shipping Cost");
                      WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Shipping Cost",'',1,
                                    ShippingOption."Shipping Cost",ShippingOption.Description,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                    END;
                  END;
                END;

                IF ShippingOption."Sales Order Text" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  StandardText.GET(ShippingOption."Sales Order Text");
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,ShippingOption."Sales Order Text",FALSE,0,'',FALSE
      ,FALSE);
                END;

                // Geb�hren Zahlungsart einf�gen
                IF PaymentOption."Payment Cost" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                  Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No. Payment Cost");
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Payment Cost",'',1,
                                    PaymentOption."Payment Cost",PaymentOption.Description,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                END;

                // Geb�hr Mindestbestellwert einf�gen
                IF Shop."Small Quantity Charge" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                  IF OrderAmount < Shop."Small Quantity Charge Limit" THEN BEGIN
                    Language.TESTFIELD("Small Quantity Charge Text");
                    Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No Small Quantity Charge");
                    StandardText.GET(Language."Small Quantity Charge Text");
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No Small Quantity Charge",'',1,
                                      Shop."Small Quantity Charge",STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description),FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                  END;
                END;

                // Online-Rabatt einf�gen
                IF Shop."Online Discount" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                  Language.TESTFIELD("Online Discount Text");
                  //Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No. Online Discount");
                  Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No. Online Discount 1");
                  Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No. Online Discount 2");
                  Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No. Online Discount 3");

                  StandardText.GET(Language."Online Discount Text");
                  OnlineDiscountAmount := ROUND(OrderAmount/100*Shop."Online Discount");

                  TempSalesLine.RESET;
                  TempSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesLine."Document Type");
                  TempSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesLine."Document No.");

                  IF TempSalesLine.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
                     OnlineDiscountOrderAmount += TempSalesLine."Line Amount";
                     IF Shop."VAT Identifier 1" = TempSalesLine."VAT Prod. Posting Group" THEN
                       OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT1 += TempSalesLine."Line Amount";
                     IF Shop."VAT Identifier 2" = TempSalesLine."VAT Prod. Posting Group" THEN
                       OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT2 += TempSalesLine."Line Amount";
                     IF Shop."VAT Identifier 3" = TempSalesLine."VAT Prod. Posting Group" THEN
                       OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT3 += TempSalesLine."Line Amount";
                  UNTIL TempSalesLine.NEXT = 0;
                  IF OnlineDiscountAmount < OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT1 THEN BEGIN
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Online Discount 1",'',1,
                                                      -OnlineDiscountAmount,STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,Shop."Online Discount")
      + ' ' + VATGroupHighPercentageText,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT1 <> 0 THEN
                      WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Online Discount 1",'',1,
                                                      -OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT1,STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,Shop.
      "Online Discount") + ' ' + VATGroupHighPercentageText,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                    IF OnlineDiscountAmount < (OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT1 + OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT2) THEN BEGIN
                      WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Online Discount 2",'',1,
                                                      -(OnlineDiscountAmount-OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT1),STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.
      Description,Shop."Online Discount") + ' ' + VATGroupMediumPercentageText,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      IF OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT2 <> 0 THEN
                        WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Online Discount 2",'',1,
                                                         -(OnlineDiscountOrderAmountVAT2),STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,Shop.
      "Online Discount") + ' ' + VATGroupMediumPercentageText,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                    END;
                  END;
                END;

                //Gutscheinrabatt einf�gen
                IF CouponDiscountAmount <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                  IF DCOrder THEN BEGIN
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,2,Shop."G/L Account Coupons",'',1,CouponDiscountAmount,
                                    STRSUBSTNO(Text003,CouponCode),FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF ValueCoupon THEN BEGIN
                      Shop.TESTFIELD("G/L Account Coupons");
                      WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,2,Shop."G/L Account Coupons",'',1,-CouponDiscountAmount,
                                                        STRSUBSTNO(Text003,CouponCode),FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No. Coupon Discount 1");
                      Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No. Coupon Discount 2");
                      Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No. Coupon Discount 3");

                      TempSalesLine.RESET;
                      TempSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesLine."Document Type");
                      TempSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesLine."Document No.");
                      IF TempSalesLine.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
                         CouponOrderAmount += TempSalesLine."Line Amount";
                         IF Shop."VAT Identifier 1" = TempSalesLine."VAT Prod. Posting Group" THEN
                           CouponOrderAmountVAT1 += TempSalesLine."Line Amount";
                         IF Shop."VAT Identifier 2" = TempSalesLine."VAT Prod. Posting Group" THEN
                           CouponOrderAmountVAT2 += TempSalesLine."Line Amount";
                         IF Shop."VAT Identifier 3" = TempSalesLine."VAT Prod. Posting Group" THEN
                           CouponOrderAmountVAT3 += TempSalesLine."Line Amount";
                      UNTIL TempSalesLine.NEXT = 0;

                      IF CouponDiscountAmount < CouponOrderAmountVAT1 THEN BEGIN
                        WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Coupon Discount 1",'',
                                                    1,-CouponDiscountAmount,STRSUBSTNO(Text003,CouponCode),FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                      END ELSE BEGIN
                        IF CouponOrderAmountVAT1 <> 0 THEN
                          WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Coupon Discount 1",'',
                                                          1,-CouponOrderAmountVAT1,STRSUBSTNO(Text003,CouponCode),FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                        IF CouponDiscountAmount < (CouponOrderAmountVAT1 + CouponOrderAmountVAT2) THEN BEGIN
                          WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Coupon Discount 2",'',
                                     1,-(CouponDiscountAmount-CouponOrderAmountVAT1),STRSUBSTNO(Text003,CouponCode),FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE
      );
                        END ELSE BEGIN
                          IF CouponOrderAmountVAT2 <> 0 THEN
                            WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Coupon Discount 2",'',
                                              1,-CouponOrderAmountVAT2,STRSUBSTNO(Text002,CouponCode),FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                          IF CouponDiscountAmount > (CouponOrderAmountVAT1 + CouponOrderAmountVAT2) THEN
                            WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Coupon Discount 3",'',
                                     1,-(CouponDiscountAmount-CouponOrderAmountVAT1-CouponOrderAmountVAT2),STRSUBSTNO(Text003,CouponCode
      ),FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE)
                        END;
                      END;
                    END;
                  END;
                END;

                // Leeerzeile einf�gen
                WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
                //US Sales Tax einf�gen
                GetUSSalesTax(SalesHeader,SalesLine,OrderAmount,SalesLineTyp);

                // Hinweis Bankkonto einf�gen
                IF ShowBankAccount AND (BankAccountNo <> '') THEN BEGIN
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,STRSUBSTNO(Text001,BankAccountNo),FALSE,0,'',FALSE
      ,FALSE);
                END;

                //PH 01.05.2013 Anbindung Billpay: Bankdaten als SalesLine schreiben +++

                // Leeerzeile einf�gen
                WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);

                i := 0;
                //Kontoinhaber
                WHILE i <= 255 DO BEGIN
                  IF COPYSTR(BpAccOwner,i+1,50) <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,
                                     COPYSTR(BpAccOwner,i+1,50),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
                  END;
                  i+= 50;
                END;

                IF Sepa THEN BEGIN
                  //Kontonummer
                  IF BpAccNr <> '' THEN
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,INSSTR(BpAccNr,'IBAN: ',1),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE)
      ;

                  //Bankleitzahl
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,INSSTR(BpAccIBAN,'BIC: ',1),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE
      );
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  //Kontonummer
                  IF BpAccIBAN <> '' THEN
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,INSSTR(BpAccIBAN,'BLZ: ',1),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE
      );

                  //Kontonummer
                  IF BpAccNr <> '' THEN
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,INSSTR(BpAccNr,'Kontonummer: ',1),FALSE,0,'',FALSE
      ,FALSE);
                END;

                IF ((BpAccNr <> '') OR (BpAccIBAN <> '')) AND (BpAccOwner <> '') THEN
                  //F�lligkeitsdatum
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,STRSUBSTNO('F�lligkeitsdatum: %1',CALCDATE(
      '<+14D>')),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);

                //Bank
                i := 0;
                WHILE i <= 255 DO BEGIN
                  IF COPYSTR(BpBank,i+1,50) <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,
                                     COPYSTR(BpBank,i+1,50),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
                  END;
                  i+= 50;
                END;

                // Verwendungszweck
                i := 0;
                WHILE i <= 255 DO BEGIN
                  IF COPYSTR(BpAccOwner,i+1,50) <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,
                                     COPYSTR(BpInvoiceRef,i+1,50),FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
                  END;
                  i+= 50;
                END;

                //PH 01.05.2013 Anbindung Billpay: Bankdaten als SalesLine schreiben ---

                IF PaymentOption."Sales Order Text" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,PaymentOption."Sales Order Text",'',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,
      FALSE);
                END;

                // Gesperrten Kunden zur�cksetzten
                IF CustomerBlockedState <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                  Customer.Blocked := CustomerBlockedState;
                  Customer.MODIFY(FALSE);
                END;

                // Bestellung als �bertragen markieren
                CLEAR(SetRec);
                AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_sales_header');
                AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',SalesHeaderId);
                AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
                WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);
                NextSalesHeader := TRUE;
                WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
                COMMIT;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
                  IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                    ERROR(GetRec)
                  ELSE BEGIN
                    IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                      LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                      LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                      IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                       IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                        ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                      END ELSE BEGIN
                        IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                          ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                      END;
                      LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                      LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                      LogFile.CLOSE;
                      IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                        MESSAGE(GetRec);
                    END ELSE
                      ERROR('No Logfile specified');
                  END;
                END;
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
                IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
                  ERROR(GetRec)
                ELSE BEGIN
                  IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                    LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                    LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                    IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                     IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                      ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                        ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                    END;
                    LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                    LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                    LogFile.CLOSE;
                    IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                      MESSAGE(GetRec);
                  END ELSE
                    ERROR('No Logfile specified');
                END;
              END;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //leeren Datensatz abfangen
            IF (SalesHeaderId <> '') AND (UserEmail = '') THEN BEGIN
              // Bestellung als �bertragen markieren
              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_sales_header');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',SalesHeaderId);
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);
              NextSalesHeader := TRUE;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            END;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                    ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END;
        END;
      UNTIL NOT NextSalesHeader;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetSalesLines@1000000082(VAR SalesHeader@1000000006 : Record 36;VAR SalesLine@1000000007 : Record 37;VAR OrderAmount@1000000009 : Decimal);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      SetRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      NextSalesLine@1000000003 : Boolean;
      ItemNo@1000000004 : Code[20];
      ItemQuantity@1000000005 : Decimal;
      Delimiter@1000000008 : Text[10];
      SalesLineId@1000000010 : Integer;
      Item@1000000011 : Record 27;
      Text001@1000000012 : TextConst 'DEU=!!! Artikel %1, Menge %2 fehlt !!!;ENU=!!! Item %1, Qty %2 does not exist !!!';
      VariantCode@1000000013 : Code[10];
      ErrorText@1000000014 : Text[100];
      Text002@1000000015 : TextConst 'DEU=!!! Artikel %1, Menge %2 gesperrt !!!;ENU=!!! Item %1, Qty %2 blocked !!!';
      IsCouponItem@1000000016 : Boolean;
      SalespersonDiscount@1000000017 : Decimal;
      ShopCode@1000000018 : Code[10];
      LanguageCode@1000000019 : Code[10];
      Shop@1000000020 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopItem@1000000021 : Record 5447304;
      LogFile@1000 : File;
      UnitPrice@1000000022 : Decimal;
      "--- Customization ---"@1000000023 : Integer;
      Customized@1000000024 : Boolean;
      NextSalesLineCustomize@1000000025 : Boolean;
      SalesLineCustomize@1000000026 : Record 37;
      CustomizeId@1000000027 : Integer;
      CustomizeFieldName@1000000028 : Text[50];
      CustomizeValue@1000000029 : Text[250];
      CurrRecCust@1000000030 : Text[1024];
      GetRecCust@1000000031 : Text[1024];
      LastSalesLine@1000000032 : Record 37;
      SalesLineCustomize2@1000000033 : Record 37;
      NewLineNoCustomize@1000000034 : Integer;
      i@1000000036 : Integer;
      SetRecCust@1000000035 : Text[1024];
      GreetingCardText@1000000037 : Text[250];
      PackageForItem@1000000038 : Text[30];
      PackageForItemVarCode@1000000039 : Text[10];
      Text003@1000000040 : TextConst 'DEU=Verpackung f�r Artikel %1;ENU=Package for Item %1';
      Text004@1000000041 : TextConst 'DEU=, Variante %1;ENU=, Variant %1';
      PackageDescription@1000000042 : Text[50];
      "--- St�ckliste ---"@1000000053 : Integer;
      BOMSalesLineAmount@1000000052 : Decimal;
      BOMItemAmount@1000000051 : Decimal;
      BOMDifferenceAmount@1000000050 : Decimal;
      BOMVATAmount1@1000000049 : Decimal;
      BOMVATAmount2@1000000048 : Decimal;
      BOMVATAMount3@1000000047 : Decimal;
      BOMLineNo@1000000046 : Integer;
      BOMSalesLine@1000000045 : Record 37;
      SalesExplodeBOM@1000000044 : Codeunit 63;
      SalesLineTyp@1000000043 : Integer;
      Text005@1000000054 : TextConst 'DEU=Set-Rabatt';
    BEGIN
      // Verkaufszeile abholen

      OnBeforeGetSalesLines(SalesHeader);

      Delimiter := '-;-';

      WITH SalesLine DO REPEAT
        NextSalesLine := FALSE;

        // Daten von Webshop-Bestellungszeilen anfordern
        CLEAR(CurrRec);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_sales_line');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','id,item_no,variant_code,quantity,is_coupon_item,salesperson_discount,shop_code,' +
                                'language_code,unit_price,customized,greeting_card_text,package_for_item,package_for_variant_code');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[order_no]',SalesHeader."Webshop Order No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);

        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);

        IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN

          // Verkaufszeile einf�gen
          EVALUATE(SalesLineId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ItemNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(VariantCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ItemQuantity,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(IsCouponItem,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(SalespersonDiscount,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShopCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(LanguageCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(UnitPrice,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(Customized,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(GreetingCardText,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(PackageForItem,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(PackageForItemVarCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));

          Shop.GET(ShopCode);
          IF Shop."Use Items from Shop Code" <> '' THEN
            ShopCode := Shop."Use Items from Shop Code";
          WebshopItem.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode,ItemNo);
          IF (SalesLineId <> 0) AND (ItemNo <> '') THEN BEGIN
            ErrorText := '';
            IF NOT Item.GET(ItemNo) THEN
              ErrorText := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,ItemNo,ItemQuantity);
            IF Item.Blocked THEN
              ErrorText := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,ItemNo,ItemQuantity);
            IF ErrorText = '' THEN BEGIN

              Item.CALCFIELDS("Bill of Materials");

              IF Item."Bill of Materials" AND (SalesLine.Description <> '') THEN BEGIN
                WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
              END;

              WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,1,ItemNo,VariantCode,ItemQuantity,UnitPrice,'',IsCouponItem,
                                                SalespersonDiscount,WebshopItem."Unit of Measure Code",FALSE,FALSE);


              IF Customized THEN BEGIN

                  WITH SalesLineCustomize DO REPEAT
                    NextSalesLineCustomize := FALSE;
                    CLEAR(CurrRecCust);
                    AddVar(CurrRecCust,'table','shop_sales_line_customize');
                    AddVar(CurrRecCust,'delimiter',Delimiter);
                    AddVar(CurrRecCust,'fields','id,field_name,value');
                    AddVar(CurrRecCust,'filter[sales_line_id]',SalesLineId);
                    AddVar(CurrRecCust,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
                    GetRecCust := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRecCust);
                    IF STRPOS(GetRecCust,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
                      EVALUATE(CustomizeId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRecCust,Delimiter));
                      EVALUATE(CustomizeFieldName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRecCust,Delimiter));
                      EVALUATE(CustomizeValue,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRecCust,Delimiter));
                      IF CustomizeValue <> '' THEN BEGIN
                        LastSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
                        LastSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesHeader."No.");
                        IF LastSalesLine.FINDLAST THEN
                          NewLineNoCustomize := LastSalesLine."Line No." + 10000
                        ELSE
                          NewLineNoCustomize := 10000;
                        IF CustomizeValue <> '' THEN BEGIN
                          i := 0;
                          WHILE i <= 250 DO BEGIN
                            IF COPYSTR(CustomizeValue,i+1,50) <> '' THEN BEGIN
                              SalesLineCustomize2.INIT;
                              SalesLineCustomize2.VALIDATE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
                              SalesLineCustomize2.VALIDATE("Document No.",SalesHeader."No.");
                              SalesLineCustomize2.VALIDATE("Line No.",NewLineNoCustomize);
                              SalesLineCustomize2.VALIDATE(Type,SalesLine.Type::" ");
                              SalesLineCustomize2.INSERT(TRUE);
                              NewLineNoCustomize := NewLineNoCustomize + 10000;
                              IF CustomizeValue <> '' THEN
                                SalesLineCustomize2.VALIDATE(Description,COPYSTR(CustomizeValue,i+1,50));
                              IF CustomizeFieldName <> '' THEN
                                SalesLineCustomize2.VALIDATE("Description 2",CustomizeFieldName);
                              SalesLineCustomize2.MODIFY(TRUE);
                            END;
                            i+= 50;
                          END;
                        END;
                      END;
                      // Webshop-Bestellungszeile als �bertragen kennzeichnen
                      CLEAR(SetRecCust);
                      AddVar(SetRecCust,'table','shop_sales_line_customize');
                      AddVar(SetRecCust,'primary[id]',CustomizeId);
                      AddVar(SetRecCust,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
                      WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRecCust);
                      NextSalesLineCustomize := TRUE
                    END;
                  UNTIL NOT NextSalesLineCustomize;
                END;

              IF GreetingCardText <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  WHILE i <= 320 DO BEGIN
                    IF COPYSTR(GreetingCardText,i+1,50) <> '' THEN BEGIN
                      WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,COPYSTR(GreetingCardText,i+1,50),FALSE,0,'',
      FALSE,FALSE);
                    END;
                    i+= 50;
                  END;
              END;

              IF PackageForItem <> '' THEN BEGIN
                PackageDescription := STRSUBSTNO(Text003,PackageForItem);
                IF PackageForItemVarCode <> '' THEN
                  PackageDescription := PackageDescription + STRSUBSTNO(Text004,PackageForItemVarCode);
                WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,PackageDescription,FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
              END;

              IF NOT IsCouponItem THEN
                OrderAmount += SalesLine."Line Amount";

              // St�ckliste entfalten und entsprechenden Rabatt einf�gen ---
              IF Item."Bill of Materials" THEN BEGIN
                BOMSalesLineAmount := SalesLine."Line Amount";
                BOMLineNo := SalesLine."Line No.";
                CLEAR(BOMItemAmount);
                CLEAR(BOMVATAmount1);
                CLEAR(BOMVATAmount2);
                CLEAR(BOMVATAMount3);
                SalesExplodeBOM.SetSelectionDisabled(TRUE);
                SalesExplodeBOM.RUN(SalesLine);
                BOMSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesLine."Document Type");
                BOMSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesLine."Document No.");
                BOMSalesLine.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>=%1',BOMLineNo);
                BOMSalesLine.SETRANGE(Type,BOMSalesLine.Type::Item);
                IF BOMSalesLine.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
                   BOMItemAmount += BOMSalesLine."Line Amount";
                   IF Shop."VAT Identifier 1" = BOMSalesLine."VAT Identifier" THEN
                     BOMVATAmount1 += BOMSalesLine."Line Amount";
                   IF Shop."VAT Identifier 2" = BOMSalesLine."VAT Identifier" THEN
                     BOMVATAmount2 += BOMSalesLine."Line Amount";
                   IF Shop."VAT Identifier 3" = BOMSalesLine."VAT Identifier" THEN
                     BOMVATAMount3 += BOMSalesLine."Line Amount";
                UNTIL BOMSalesLine.NEXT = 0;
                CASE Shop."Typ Discounts/Costs" OF
                  Shop."Typ Discounts/Costs"::Item: SalesLineTyp := 1;
                  Shop."Typ Discounts/Costs"::"G/L Account": SalesLineTyp := 2;
                  Shop."Typ Discounts/Costs"::Resource: SalesLineTyp := 3;
                END;
                BOMDifferenceAmount := BOMItemAmount - BOMSalesLineAmount;
                IF BOMDifferenceAmount > 0 THEN BEGIN
                  IF BOMDifferenceAmount < BOMVATAmount1 THEN BEGIN
                    WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Set Discount 1",'',1,-
      BOMDifferenceAmount,Text005,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF BOMVATAmount1 <> 0 THEN
                      WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Set Discount 1",'',1,-
      BOMVATAmount1,Text005,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                    IF BOMDifferenceAmount < (BOMVATAmount1 + BOMVATAmount2) THEN BEGIN
                      WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Set Discount 2",'',1,-(
      BOMDifferenceAmount-BOMVATAmount1),Text005,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      IF BOMVATAmount2 <> 0 THEN
                        WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Set Discount 2",'',1,-
      BOMVATAmount2,Text005,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                      WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,SalesLineTyp,Shop."Sales No. Set Discount 3",'',1,-(
      BOMDifferenceAmount-BOMVATAmount1-BOMVATAmount2),Text005,FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);
                    END;
                  END;
                END;
                WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,'',FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
              END;
              //+++

            END ELSE BEGIN
              WebshopManagement.InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,0,'','',0,0,ErrorText,FALSE,0,'',FALSE,FALSE);
            END;

            // Webshop-Bestellungszeile als �bertragen kennzeichnen
            CLEAR(SetRec);
            AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_sales_line');
            AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',SalesLineId);
            AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);
            NextSalesLine := TRUE
          END;

        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                    ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END;

        END;
      UNTIL NOT NextSalesLine;
    END;

          Shop.GET(ShopCode);
          Shop.TESTFIELD("Sales No US Sales Tax");

                                    TaxCollectable,STRSUBSTNO(Text001,CombinedTaxRate,TaxableAmount),FALSE,0,'',TRUE,TRUE);

          // Zeile als �bertragen kennzeichnen
          //CLEAR(SetRec);
          //AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_order_us_sales_tax');
          //AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',SalesLineId);
          //AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
          //WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);
          //NextSalesLine := TRUE

        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                    ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END;

        END;
      //UNTIL NOT NextSalesLine;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendAllOrderMail2@1000000118(CountRecords@1140000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1000000000 : Record 36;
    BEGIN
      // Alle Auftragsbest�tigungen versendenn

      WITH SalesHeader DO BEGIN
        SETCURRENTKEY("Webshop Order No.","Webshop Send Order Mail 2","Webshop Order Mail 2 Sent");
        SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type"::Order);
        SETRANGE("Webshop Send Order Mail 2",TRUE);
        SETRANGE("Webshop Order Mail 2 Sent",0DT);
        SETFILTER("Document Date",'%1..%2',TODAY-7,TODAY);
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            IF ((("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::Quote,"Document Type"::Order]) AND ("Webshop Order No." <> ''))
              OR (("Document Type" = "Document Type"::"Return Order") AND ("Webshop Return Order No." <> ''))) THEN BEGIN
              SendOrderMail2(SalesHeader,"Webshop Shop Code","Webshop Language Code",'');
              GET("Document Type","No.");
              "Webshop Send Order Mail 2" := FALSE;
              "Webshop Order Mail 2 Sent" := CURRENTDATETIME;
              MODIFY(FALSE);
            END;
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendOrderMail2@1000000115(ParameterSalesHeader@1140000 : Record 36;ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[10];LanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];RequestMail@1140001 : Text[80]);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1140002 : Text[1024];
      FileName@1140003 : Text[100];

      SalesHeader@1000000002 : Record 36;

      SalesHeader.GET(ParameterSalesHeader."Document Type",ParameterSalesHeader."No.");

        IF Language."E-Mail Order 2" <>  '' THEN BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'type','order_mail_2');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',ShopCode);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',LanguageCode);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'order_no',SalesHeader."Webshop Order No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'request_mail',RequestMail);
          IF Language."Report PDF Order Confirmation" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            FileName := WebshopManagement.CreateDocumentPDF(0,ShopCode,LanguageCode,SalesHeader."No.");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'attachment',FileName);
          END;
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('send_customer_email',CurrRec);

        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendAllOrderMail3@1140027(CountRecords@1140000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      ShipmentHeader@1140001 : Record 110;
      SubscriptionHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447340;
    BEGIN
      // Alle Lieferbest�tigungen versenden

      WITH ShipmentHeader DO BEGIN
        SETCURRENTKEY("Webshop Order No.","Webshop Send Order Mail 3","Webshop Order Mail 3 Sent");
        SETFILTER("Webshop Order No.",'<>%1','');
        SETRANGE("Webshop Send Order Mail 3",TRUE);
        SETRANGE("Webshop Order Mail 3 Sent",0DT);
        SETFILTER("Package Tracking No.",'<>%1','');
        SETFILTER("Document Date",'%1..%2',TODAY-7,TODAY);
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          IF FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            //Nur normale Lieferbest�tigung versenden wenn keine Abo-Bestellung
            //oder erstmalige Abo-Bestellung (aus nicht aus autom. Anlage)
            IF ("Webshop Subscription Code" = '') OR (SubscriptionHeader.GET("Webshop Subscription Code") AND
                NOT((SubscriptionHeader.Type = SubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption) OR ("Webshop Subscr. Shipping Order" =
      TRUE)))
                THEN BEGIN
                IF (CURRENTDATETIME > ShipmentHeader."Webshop Send Order Mail 3 Time") THEN BEGIN
                  SendOrderMail3(ShipmentHeader,"Webshop Shop Code","Webshop Language Code",'');
                  GET("No.");
                  "Webshop Send Order Mail 3" := FALSE;
                  "Webshop Order Mail 3 Sent" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                  MODIFY(FALSE);
                END;
            END;
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

              FileName := WebshopManagement.CreateDocumentPDF(2,ShopCode,LanguageCode,ShipmentHeader."No.");

          END;

      END;
    END;
