      SubscriptionHeader@72001 : Record 5447340;
      SetRec@72002 : Text[1024];
      InitialOrderNo@72003 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      Delimiter := '-;-';

      WITH SubscrCustLink DO BEGIN

        //Teil 1 anfordern
        CLEAR(CurrRec);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_subscr_customer_link');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'fields',
      'id,subscription_code,customer_no,webshop_order_email,line_no,bill_to_name,bill_to_name_2,bill_to_address,' +
      'bill_to_address_2,bill_to_city,ship_to_name,ship_to_name_2,ship_to_address,ship_to_address_2,ship_to_city,'+
      'currency_code,bill_to_post_code,bill_to_county,bill_to_country_region_code,ship_to_post_code,ship_to_county,' +
      'ship_to_country_region_code,webshop_shop_code,webshop_language_code,webshop_pay_opt_line_no,webshop_ship_opt_line_no');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[subscription_code]',ParamSubscriptionCode);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[customer_no]',ParamCustomerNo);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[webshop_order_email]',ParamWebshopOrderEmail);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[line_no]',ParamLineNo);
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
        IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN

          // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 1 in Variablen speichern
          EVALUATE(CustLinkID,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(SubscriptionCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(CustomerNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(OrderEmail,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(LineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(BillToName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(BillToName2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(BillToAddress,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(BillToAddress2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(BillToCity,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToName2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToAddress,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToAddress2,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToCity,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(CurrencyCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(BillToPostCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(BillToCounty,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(BillToCountryRegion,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToPostCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToCounty,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ShipToCountryRegion,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(WebshopShopCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(WebshopLanguageCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(WebshopPayOptLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(WebshopShipOptLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));

          IF (WebshopShipOptLineNo = 0) AND (SubscriptionHeader.GET(SubscriptionCode))
            AND (SubscriptionHeader."Shipping Option Line No." <> 0) THEN
            WebshopShipOptLineNo := SubscriptionHeader."Shipping Option Line No.";


          IF
              (WebshopShopCode <> '') AND
              (WebshopLanguageCode <> '') AND
              (CustomerNo <>'') AND
              (OrderEmail <> '') AND
              (SubscriptionCode <> '') THEN BEGIN
            //Teil 2 anfordern
            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_subscr_customer_link');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'fields',
      'customer_pseudo_pay_line_no,signup_date,cancellation_date,next_order_creation_date,next_order_w_payment_date,' +
      'no_of_turns_processed,user_name,subscription_item_no,next_sequence_step_line_no,subscription_qty_per_turn');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[subscription_code]',ParamSubscriptionCode);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[customer_no]',ParamCustomerNo);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[webshop_order_email]',ParamWebshopOrderEmail);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[line_no]',ParamLineNo);
            GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);

            IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
              // Webshop-Bestellung Teil 2 in Variablen speichern
              EVALUATE(CustPseudoPayLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(TMPDateString,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              IF (TMPDateString <> '0000-00-00') AND (TMPDateString <> '00-00-0000') AND (TMPDateString <> '1970-01-01') AND (
      TMPDateString <> '01-01-1970') THEN BEGIN
                //MESSAGE('DATE-STR:' + TMPDateString);
                EVALUATE(SignupDate,TMPDateString);
                CLEAR(TMPDateString);
              END;
              EVALUATE(TMPDateString,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              IF (TMPDateString <> '0000-00-00') AND (TMPDateString <> '00-00-0000') AND (TMPDateString <> '1970-01-01') AND (
      TMPDateString <> '01-01-1970') THEN BEGIN
                  //      MESSAGE('DATE-STR:' + TMPDateString);
                  EVALUATE(CancellationDate,TMPDateString);
                  CLEAR(TMPDateString);
              END;
              EVALUATE(TMPDateString,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              IF (TMPDateString <> '0000-00-00') AND (TMPDateString <> '00-00-0000') AND (TMPDateString <> '1970-01-01') AND (
      TMPDateString <> '01-01-1970') THEN BEGIN
                  //    MESSAGE('DATE-STR:' + TMPDateString);
                  EVALUATE(NextOrderCreationDate,TMPDateString);
                  CLEAR(TMPDateString);
              END;
              EVALUATE(TMPDateString,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              IF (TMPDateString <> '0000-00-00') AND (TMPDateString <> '00-00-0000') AND (TMPDateString <> '1970-01-01') AND (
      TMPDateString <> '01-01-1970') THEN BEGIN
                  //      MESSAGE('DATE-STR:' + TMPDateString);
                  EVALUATE(NextOrderWPaymentDate,TMPDateString);
                  CLEAR(TMPDateString);
              END;
              EVALUATE(NoOfTurnsProcessed,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(UserName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(SubscriptionItemNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(NextSeqStepLineNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(SubscriptionQtyPerTurn,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(InitialOrderNo,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));

              "Subscription Code" := SubscriptionCode;
              "Customer No." := ParamCustomerNo;
              "Webshop Order Email" := ParamWebshopOrderEmail;
              "Line No." := LineNo;
              "Bill-to Name" := BillToName;
              "Bill-to Name 2" := BillToName2;
              "Bill-to Address" := BillToAddress;
              "Bill-to Address 2" := BillToAddress2;
              "Bill-to City" := BillToCity;
              "Ship-to Name" := ShipToName;
              "Ship-to Name 2" := ShipToName2;
              "Ship-to Address" := ShipToAddress;
              "Ship-to Address 2" := ShipToAddress2;
              "Ship-to City" := ShipToCity;
              "Currency Code" := CurrencyCode;
              "Bill-to Post Code" := BillToPostCode;
              "Bill-to County" := BillToCounty;
              "Bill-to Country/Region Code" := BillToCountryRegion;
              "Ship-to Post Code" := ShipToPostCode;
              "Ship-to County" := ShipToCounty;
              "Ship-to Country/Region Code" := ShipToCountryRegion;
              "Webshop Shop Code" := WebshopShopCode;
              "Webshop Language Code" := WebshopLanguageCode;
              "Webshop Pay. Opt. Line No." := WebshopPayOptLineNo;
              "Webshop Ship. Opt. Line No." := WebshopShipOptLineNo;
              "Customer Pseudo-Pay. Line No." := CustPseudoPayLineNo;
              "Signup Date" := SignupDate;
              "Cancellation Date" := CancellationDate;
              "Next Order Creation Date" := NextOrderCreationDate;
              "Next Order w. Payment Date" := NextOrderWPaymentDate;
              "No. of turns processed" := NoOfTurnsProcessed;
              "User Name" := UserName;
              "Subscription Item No." := SubscriptionItemNo;
              "Next Sequence Step Line No." := NextSeqStepLineNo;
              "Subscription Qty. Per Turn" := SubscriptionQtyPerTurn;
              "Webshop Initial Order No." := InitialOrderNo;
              INSERT;

            END ELSE BEGIN
              ErrState := TRUE;
              ErrMsg := STRSUBSTNO(Error001,GetRec);
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            ErrState := TRUE;
            ErrMsg := STRSUBSTNO(Error002,CustLinkID,GetRec);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          ErrState := TRUE;
          ErrMsg := STRSUBSTNO(Error001,GetRec);
        END;

        IF ErrState THEN
          ERROR(ErrMsg);

        CLEAR(SetRec);
        AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_subscr_customer_link');
        AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',CustLinkID);
        AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
        WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdAllSubscCustLink@1000000176(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000001 : Record 5447343;
    BEGIN
      WITH WebshopSubscrCustomerLink DO BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          IF FINDSET(TRUE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
            UpdateSubscriptionCustLink(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink);
            "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
            MODIFY(FALSE);
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdAllSubscCustLinkForHeader@1000000187(WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447340);
    VAR
      WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000001 : Record 5447343;
    BEGIN
      WITH WebshopSubscrCustomerLink DO BEGIN
        SETFILTER("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
        IF FINDSET(TRUE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
          UpdateSubscriptionCustLink(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink);
          "Last Date Updated" := CURRENTDATETIME;
          MODIFY(FALSE);
        UNTIL NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSubscriptionCustLink@1000000177(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000001 : Record 5447343);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      WITH WebshopSubscrCustomerLink DO BEGIN
        IF GET("Subscription Code","Customer No.","Webshop Order Email","Line No.") THEN BEGIN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_subscr_customer_link');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[subscription_code]',"Subscription Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[customer_no]',"Customer No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[webshop_order_email]',"Webshop Order Email");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name]',"Bill-to Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_name_2]',"Bill-to Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address]',"Bill-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_address_2]',"Bill-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_city]',"Bill-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_name]',"Ship-to Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_name_2]',"Ship-to Name 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_address]',"Ship-to Address");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_address_2]',"Ship-to Address 2");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_city]',"Ship-to City");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[currency_code]',"Currency Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_post_code]',"Bill-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_county]',"Bill-to County");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[bill_to_country_region_code]',"Bill-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_post_code]',"Ship-to Post Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_county]',"Ship-to County");
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
          CLEAR(CurrRec);

          AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_subscr_customer_link');
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[subscription_code]',"Subscription Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[customer_no]',"Customer No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[webshop_order_email]',"Webshop Order Email");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[line_no]',"Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[ship_to_country_region_code]',"Ship-to Country/Region Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[webshop_shop_code]',"Webshop Shop Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[webshop_language_code]',"Webshop Language Code");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[webshop_pay_opt_line_no]',"Webshop Pay. Opt. Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[webshop_ship_opt_line_no]',"Webshop Ship. Opt. Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[customer_pseudo_pay_line_no]',"Customer Pseudo-Pay. Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[signup_date]',"Signup Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cancellation_date]',"Cancellation Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[next_order_creation_date]',"Next Order Creation Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[next_order_w_payment_date]',"Next Order w. Payment Date");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[no_of_turns_processed]',"No. of turns processed");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[user_name]',"User Name");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[subscription_item_no]',"Subscription Item No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[next_sequence_step_line_no]',"Next Sequence Step Line No.");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[subscription_qty_per_turn]',"Subscription Qty. Per Turn");
          AddVar(CurrRec,'data[initial_order_no]',"Webshop Initial Order No.");
          IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
            WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
          ELSE
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteSubscriptionCustLink@72009(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000001 : Record 5447343);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];

      WITH WebshopSubscrCustomerLink DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_subscr_customer_link');

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate@1000000168(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000000 : Record 5447343;AlternativeBaseDate@1000000011 : Date;BaseDateHandling@1000000017 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded') NextPlannedShippingDate : Date;
    VAR
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447340;
      SalesHeader@1000000002 : Record 36;
      ShipmentHeader@1000000003 : Record 110;
      NextTurnDateFormula@1000000004 : DateFormula;
      Shop@1000000005 : Record 5447301;
      ReferenceDate@1000000006 : Date;
      Error001@1000000007 : TextConst 'DEU=Es kann kein n�chstes geplantes Lieferdatum f�r eine Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung erzeugt werden, die bereits einen oder mehrere Turni durchlaufen hat, zu der jedoch keine Verkaufsk�pfe mehr bestehen.;ENU=It is not possible to determine a next planned shipping date for a Subscription-Customer-link which has already completed one or more turns, but for which no Sales Headers exist.';
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000008 : Record 5447341;
      MinSeqStepLineNo@1000000009 : Integer;
      CancellationCutoffDate@1000000010 : Date;
      IntermediateCalculationDate@1000000012 : Date;
      CurrReferenceTurn@1000000013 : Integer;
      BaseDate@1000000014 : Date;
      CurrReferenceStepLineNo@1000000015 : Integer;
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000016 : '>,<';
      Error002@1000000018 : TextConst 'DEU=Das Abonnement enth�lt keine Sequenzschritte.;ENU=The Subscription contains no sequence steps.';
    BEGIN
      //Ermittelt das N�chste Lieferdatum f�r eine Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung

      BaseDate := WORKDATE;
      IF (AlternativeBaseDate > 0D) AND (AlternativeBaseDate <> WORKDATE) THEN
        BaseDate := AlternativeBaseDate;

      WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Code");

      IF (NOT WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Active) OR
         ((BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded) AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid from" <> 0D) AND (BaseDate <
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid from")) OR
         ((BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded) AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid to" <> 0D) AND (BaseDate >
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid to")) OR
         ((BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded) AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid from" <> 0D) AND (BaseDate <=
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid from")) OR
         ((BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded) AND (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid to" <> 0D) AND (BaseDate >=
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid to")) THEN BEGIN
        NextPlannedShippingDate := 0D;
        EXIT(NextPlannedShippingDate);
      END;

      IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption THEN BEGIN

        CurrReferenceTurn := 1;
        ReferenceDate := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date";
        NextTurnDateFormula := WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Turn Interval Formula";
        IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Date Formula",ReferenceDate);

        IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
        IF (((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
           ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded))) THEN REPEAT
          ReferenceDate := IntermediateCalculationDate;
          IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(NextTurnDateFormula,ReferenceDate);
          IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
          CurrReferenceTurn += 1;
        UNTIL ((IntermediateCalculationDate >= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
              ((IntermediateCalculationDate > BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded));

        IF ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
           ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN BEGIN
          NextPlannedShippingDate := 0D;
          EXIT(NextPlannedShippingDate);
        END;

      END ELSE IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem THEN BEGIN

        WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
        WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
        IF NOT WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN
          ERROR(Error002);
        MinSeqStepLineNo := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Line No.";
        IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date" > 0D THEN
          IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date"
        ELSE
          IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date");

        IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type"::Anew THEN
          IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,IntermediateCalculationDate)
        ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
          WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',MinSeqStepLineNo);

          IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
          IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDSET AND
             ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
             ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN REPEAT
            IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date" > 0D THEN
              IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date"
            ELSE
              IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,IntermediateCalculationDate);
            IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
          UNTIL (WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.NEXT = 0) OR ((IntermediateCalculationDate >= BaseDate)
                AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
                ((IntermediateCalculationDate > BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded));

          IF ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
             ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN BEGIN
            NextPlannedShippingDate := 0D;
            EXIT(NextPlannedShippingDate);
          END;
        END;

      END;

      NextPlannedShippingDate := IntermediateCalculationDate;
      IF WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Cancellation Date" > 0D THEN BEGIN
        CancellationCutoffDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Cancel. Period Date Formula",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.
                                           "Cancellation Date");
        IF NextPlannedShippingDate >= CancellationCutoffDate THEN
          NextPlannedShippingDate := 0D;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubscrNextShipOrderDate@1000000178(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000000 : Record 5447343;Shop@1000000001 : Record 5447301;AlternativeBaseDate@1000000003 : Date;BaseDateHandling@1000000006 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded') NextOrderCreationDate : Date;
    VAR
      NextPlannedShippingDate@1000000002 : Date;
      BaseDate@1000000004 : Date;
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000005 : '>,<';
    BEGIN
      //Ermittelt das n�chste Datum f�r die Erzeugung eines Verkaufsauftrags f�r
      //Abonnement-Lieferabwicklung von einem gegebenen Referenzdatum aus

      BaseDate := WORKDATE;
      IF (AlternativeBaseDate <> WORKDATE) AND (AlternativeBaseDate > 0D) THEN
        BaseDate := AlternativeBaseDate;

      NextPlannedShippingDate := GetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,AlternativeBaseDate,BaseDateHandling);

      IF NextPlannedShippingDate = 0D THEN
        EXIT(0D);

      Shop.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Shop Code");
      NextOrderCreationDate := CALCDATE(Shop."Forerun Subscr. Order Create",NextPlannedShippingDate);
      IF ((NextOrderCreationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
         ((NextOrderCreationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN BEGIN
        NextPlannedShippingDate := GetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,NextOrderCreationDate,BaseDateHandling
      ::BaseDateExcluded);
        NextOrderCreationDate := CALCDATE(Shop."Forerun Subscr. Order Create",NextPlannedShippingDate);
      END;
      NextOrderCreationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(NextOrderCreationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubscrNextSeqStepLineNo@1000000181(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000000 : Record 5447343;AlternativeBaseDate@1000000007 : Date;BaseDateHandling@1000000006 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded') NextLineNo : Integer;
    VAR
      SalesHeader@1000000001 : Record 36;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000002 : Record 5447341;
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000003 : Record 5447340;
      Error001@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Es kann keine n�chste Sequenz-Schritt-Zeilennummer f�r ein Abonnement vom Typ Bestelloption ermittelt werden.;ENU=It is not possible to generate a next Sequence Step Line No. for a subscription of type Order Option.';
      BaseDate@1000000005 : Date;
      IntermediateCalculationDate@1000000008 : Date;
      IntermediateSeqStepLineNo@1000000009 : Integer;
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000010 : '>,<';
    BEGIN
      //Ermittelt die n�chste Abonnement-Sequenz-Schritt Zeilennummer f�r eine
      //Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung von einem gegebenen Referenzdatum aus

      BaseDate := WORKDATE;
      IF (AlternativeBaseDate <> WORKDATE) AND (AlternativeBaseDate > 0D) THEN
        BaseDate := AlternativeBaseDate;

      WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Code");
      IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type <> WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem THEN
        ERROR(Error001);

      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Code");
      IF NOT WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT(0);

      IntermediateSeqStepLineNo := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Line No.";
      IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date";
      IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date" = 0D THEN
        IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date");

      IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
      IF ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
         ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN REPEAT
        IntermediateSeqStepLineNo := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Line No.";
        IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date" > 0D THEN BEGIN
          IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date";
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,IntermediateCalculationDate);
        END;
        IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
      UNTIL (WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.NEXT = 0) OR ((IntermediateCalculationDate >= BaseDate) AND
            (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR ((IntermediateCalculationDate > BaseDate) AND
            (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded));

      IF ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
         ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN
         EXIT(0);

      NextLineNo := IntermediateSeqStepLineNo;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubscrNextPayOrderDate@1000000188(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000000 : Record 5447343;AlternativeBaseDate@1000000009 : Date;BaseDateHandling@1000000008 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded') PayOrderCreateDate : Date;
    VAR
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447340;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000002 : Record 5447341;
      Shop@1000000003 : Record 5447301;
      PayIntervalFormula@1000000004 : DateFormula;
      StartDate@1000000005 : Date;
      IntermediateCalculationDate@1000000006 : Date;
      BaseDate@1000000010 : Date;
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000007 : '>,<';
    BEGIN
      //Ermittelt das n�chste Datum f�r die Erzeugung eines Verkaufsauftrags f�r
      //Abonnement-Lieferabwicklung von einem gegebenen Referenzdatum aus

      BaseDate := WORKDATE;
      IF (AlternativeBaseDate <> WORKDATE) AND (AlternativeBaseDate > 0D) THEN
        BaseDate := AlternativeBaseDate;

      WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Code");
      Shop.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Webshop Shop Code");

      CASE WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type" OF
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::Once:
          BEGIN
            PayOrderCreateDate := 0D;
            EXIT(PayOrderCreateDate);
          END;
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::PerTurn:
          BEGIN
            PayOrderCreateDate := GetCustSubscrNextShipOrderDate(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,Shop,AlternativeBaseDate,
      BaseDateHandling);
            EXIT(PayOrderCreateDate);
          END;
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::Monthly:
          EVALUATE(PayIntervalFormula,'<CD+1M>');
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::Quarterly:
          EVALUATE(PayIntervalFormula,'<CD+3M>');
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::SemiAnnually:
          EVALUATE(PayIntervalFormula,'<CD+6M>');
        WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Type"::Annually:
          EVALUATE(PayIntervalFormula,'<CD+12M>');
      END;

      IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption THEN BEGIN
        StartDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Date Formula",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date");
      END ELSE IF (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem) AND
              (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type"::Anew) THEN BEGIN
        WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
        WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
        IF NOT WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          PayOrderCreateDate := 0D;
          EXIT(PayOrderCreateDate);
        END;
        StartDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date");
      END ELSE IF (WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem) AND
              (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type" = WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Start Type"::Join) THEN BEGIN
        WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.RESET;
        WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
        IF NOT WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          PayOrderCreateDate := 0D;
          EXIT(PayOrderCreateDate);
        END;
        StartDate := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date";
      END;

      IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(Shop."Forerun Payment Capture",StartDate);
      IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");

      IF ((IntermediateCalculationDate < BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
         ((IntermediateCalculationDate <= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded)) THEN REPEAT
        IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(PayIntervalFormula,IntermediateCalculationDate);
        IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(Shop."Forerun Payment Capture",IntermediateCalculationDate);
        IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopManagement.GetNextWorkday(IntermediateCalculationDate,NextWorkdayDirection::">");
      UNTIL ((IntermediateCalculationDate >= BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded)) OR
            ((IntermediateCalculationDate > BaseDate) AND (BaseDateHandling = BaseDateHandling::BaseDateIncluded));

      PayOrderCreateDate := IntermediateCalculationDate;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubscrItQtyInDateRange@1000000179(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000002 : Record 5447343;StartDate@1000000000 : Date;EndDate@1000000001 : Date) TotalItemQuantity : Decimal;
    VAR
      NoOfTurns@1000000003 : Integer;
      IntermediateCalculationDate@1000000004 : Date;
      BaseDateHandling@1000000005 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded';
    BEGIN
      //Ermittelt die insgesamt zu liefernde Menge des abonnierten Artikels
      //innerhalb eines Zeitrahmens f�r eine Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung


      NoOfTurns := GetCustSubNoOfTurnsInDateRange(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,StartDate,EndDate);
      TotalItemQuantity := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Qty. Per Turn" * NoOfTurns;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetCustSubNoOfTurnsInDateRange@1000000182(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000002 : Record 5447343;StartDate@1000000000 : Date;EndDate@1000000001 : Date) NoOfTurns : Integer;
    VAR
      IntermediateCalculationDate@1000000004 : Date;
      BaseDateHandling@1000000005 : 'BaseDateIncluded,BaseDateExcluded';
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000006 : Record 5447340;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000003 : Record 5447341;
      EmptyDateFormula@1000000007 : DateFormula;
      NextWorkdayDirection@1000000008 : '>,<';
    BEGIN
      //Ermittelt Die Anzahl Turni f�r eine Abonnement-Kundenzuweisung innerhalb eines gewissen Zeitrahmens

      WebshopSubscriptionHeader.GET(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Subscription Code");
      NoOfTurns := 0;

      IntermediateCalculationDate := StartDate;

      IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionOrderOption THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          IntermediateCalculationDate := GetCustSubscNextDeliveryDate(WebshopSubscrCustomerLink,IntermediateCalculationDate,
      BaseDateHandling::BaseDateExcluded);
          IF (IntermediateCalculationDate > 0D) AND (IntermediateCalculationDate > StartDate) AND (IntermediateCalculationDate <=
      EndDate) THEN
            NoOfTurns += 1;
        UNTIL (IntermediateCalculationDate = 0D) OR (IntermediateCalculationDate > EndDate);
      END ELSE IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type = WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Type::SubscriptionSequenceItem THEN BEGIN
        WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
        IF NOT WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.FINDFIRST THEN
          EXIT(0);
        IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscrCustomerLink."Signup Date";
        REPEAT
          IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date" <> 0D THEN
            IntermediateCalculationDate := WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep."Fix Date"
          ELSE IF WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval <> EmptyDateFormula THEN
            IntermediateCalculationDate := CALCDATE(WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.Interval,IntermediateCalculationDate);
          IF (IntermediateCalculationDate > StartDate) AND (IntermediateCalculationDate <= EndDate) THEN
            NoOfTurns += 1;
        UNTIL (WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep.NEXT = 0) OR (IntermediateCalculationDate > EndDate);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendAllSubscInvoiceMail@1000000195(CountRecords@1000000009 : Boolean);
    VAR
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000000 : Record 112;
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447340;
      WebshopSubscriptionSeqStep@1000000002 : Record 5447341;
      WebshopSubscrCustomerLink@1000000003 : Record 5447343;
      PostFieldsString@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      TextModule@1000000005 : Record 5447309;
      WebshopLanguage@1000000006 : Record 5447302;
      AttachmentFileName@1000000007 : Text[255];
      WebshopShop@1000000008 : Record 5447301;
      CheckAttachmentPostString@1000000013 : Text[1024];
      CheckAttachmentResponse@1000000012 : Text[1024];
      AttachmentPresent@1000000011 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader.SETRANGE(Active,TRUE);
      WebshopSubscriptionHeader.SETFILTER("Payment Email Text",'<>%1','');
      IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
        WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.TABLECAPTION,WebshopSubscriptionHeader.COUNT)
      END ELSE BEGIN
        WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(WebshopSubscriptionHeader.TABLECAPTION,WebshopSubscriptionHeader.COUNT);
        IF WebshopSubscriptionHeader.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          IF ((WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid from" = 0D) OR (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid from" <= WORKDATE)) AND
              ((WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid to" = 0D) OR (WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Valid to" >= WORKDATE)) THEN BEGIN
              TextModule.GET(WebshopSubscriptionHeader."Payment Email Text");
              SalesInvoiceHeader.SETRANGE("Webshop Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
              SalesInvoiceHeader.SETRANGE("Webshop Send Order Mail 4",TRUE);
              SalesInvoiceHeader.SETRANGE("Webshop Order Mail 4 Sent",0DT);
              SalesInvoiceHeader.SETFILTER("Posting Date",'>%1',0D);
              IF SalesInvoiceHeader.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                WebshopShop.GET(SalesInvoiceHeader."Webshop Shop Code");
                WebshopLanguage.GET(SalesInvoiceHeader."Webshop Language Code");
                WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Subscription Code",WebshopSubscriptionHeader.Code);
                WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Customer No.",SalesInvoiceHeader."Sell-to Customer No.");
                WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.SETRANGE("Line No.",SalesInvoiceHeader."Webshop Sub. Cust. Line No.");
                WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.FINDFIRST;
                AddVar(PostFieldsString,'customer_no',SalesInvoiceHeader."Sell-to Customer No.");
                AddVar(PostFieldsString,'customer_name',SalesInvoiceHeader."Bill-to Name");
                AddVar(PostFieldsString,'invoice_no',SalesInvoiceHeader."No.");
                IF WebshopLanguage."Report PDF Invoice" <> 0 THEN
                  AttachmentFileName := WebshopManagement.CreateDocumentPDF(1,WebshopLanguage."Shop Code",WebshopLanguage.Code,
      SalesInvoiceHeader."No.");
                SendTextModuleMail(WebshopLanguage,TextModule,WebshopShop."E-Mail Sender",WebshopSubscrCustomerLink.
      "Webshop Order Email",PostFieldsString,AttachmentFileName);
                SalesInvoiceHeader."Webshop Send Order Mail 4" := FALSE;
                SalesInvoiceHeader."Webshop Order Mail 4 Sent" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                SalesInvoiceHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
                WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
              UNTIL SalesInvoiceHeader.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        UNTIL WebshopSubscriptionHeader.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllCampaignHeader@1000000190(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);

      WITH WebshopCampaignHeader DO BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN

        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(TABLECAPTION,COUNT);
          IF FINDSET THEN REPEAT

            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
          UNTIL NEXT=0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCampaignHeader@1000000191(ParamCampaignHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447345);
    VAR
      WebshopCampaignHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447345;
      CurrRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      WITH WebshopCampaignHeader DO BEGIN
        GET(ParamCampaignHeader.Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_campaign_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[shop_code]',"Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[language_code]',"Language Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[description]',Description);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active]',Active);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active_from_date]',"Active from Date");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active_to_date]',"Active to Date");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active_from_time]',"Active from Time");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[active_to_time]',"Active to Time");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[multiply_applicable]',"Multiply applicable");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[priority]',FORMAT(Priority));
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cond_sum_amnt_all_items_gte]',"Cond. Sum Amnt. All Items GTE");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cond_sum_amnt_sing_item_gte]',"Cond. Sum Amnt. Sing. Item GTE");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cond_sum_amnt_each_item_gte]',"Cond. Sum Amnt. Each Item GTE");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cond_no_diff_items_gte]',"Cond. No. diff. Items GTE");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cond_total_item_qty_gte]',"Cond. Total Item Qty. GTE");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cond_qty_single_item_gte]',"Cond. Qty. Single Item GTE");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cond_qty_each_item_gte]',"Cond. Qty. Each Item GTE");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[cond_basket_total_gte]',"Cond. Basket Total GTE");

        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

        CLEAR(CurrRec);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_campaign_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[discount_amnt]',"Discount Amnt.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[discount_amnt_applies_to]',"Discount Amnt. applies to");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[discount_percent]',"Discount Percent");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[discount_percent_applies_to]',"Discount Percent applies to");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[discount_applies_to_qty_max]',"Discount applies to Qty. (max)");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[qty_free_item]',"Qty. Free Item");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[type_free_item]',"Type Free Item");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[alternative_shipping_option]',"Alternative Shipping Option");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[text_condition_items]',"Text Condition Items");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[text_action_items]',"Text Action Items");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[text_basket]',"Text Basket");

        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,gSendNow)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteCampaignElement@1000000196(ParamCampaignElement@1000000000 : Record 5447346);
    VAR
      WebshopCampaignElement@1000000001 : Record 5447346;
      CurrRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      WITH WebshopCampaignElement DO BEGIN
        GET(ParamCampaignElement."Link-to Header Code",ParamCampaignElement."Link Type",ParamCampaignElement.Type,
          ParamCampaignElement."Item No.",ParamCampaignElement."Item Variant Code",ParamCampaignElement."Category Line No.");

        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_campaign_element');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[link_to_header_code]',"Link-to Header Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[link_type]',"Link Type");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[type]',Type);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[item_no]',"Item No.");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[item_var_code]',"Item Variant Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[category_line_no]',"Category Line No.");

        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
        ELSE
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('instant_delete',CurrRec);

      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateCampaignByCode@1000000197(WebshopCampaignCode@1000000000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      WebshopCampaignHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447345;
      WebshopCampaignElement@1000000002 : Record 5447346;
    BEGIN
      IF WebshopCampaignHeader.GET(WebshopCampaignCode) THEN BEGIN
        UpdateCampaignHeader(WebshopCampaignHeader);
        WebshopCampaignElement.SETRANGE("Link-to Header Code",WebshopCampaignHeader.Code);
        IF WebshopCampaignElement.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          UpdateCampaignElement(WebshopCampaignElement);
        UNTIL WebshopCampaignElement.NEXT = 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteCampaignByCode@1000000198(WebshopCampaignCode@1000000000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      WebshopCampaignHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447345;
      WebshopCampaignElement@1000000002 : Record 5447346;
    BEGIN
      WebshopCampaignElement.SETRANGE("Link-to Header Code",WebshopCampaignCode);
      IF WebshopCampaignElement.FINDSET THEN
        WebshopCampaignElement.DELETEALL(TRUE);

      IF WebshopCampaignHeader.GET(WebshopCampaignCode) THEN
        DeleteCampaignHeader(WebshopCampaignHeader);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckCampaignImage@1000000199(CampaignHeader@1000000000 : Record 5447345;Field@1000000001 : FieldRef) Ok : Boolean;
    VAR
      ImageFieldName@1000000002 : Text[30];
      InitFieldValue@1000000003 : Boolean;
      CurrRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1000000006 : File;
      RecRef@1000000007 : RecordRef;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelne Webshop Kampagnen-Datei pr?fen

      InitFieldValue := Field.VALUE;
      RecRef.GETTABLE(CampaignHeader);
      Field := RecRef.FIELD(Field.NUMBER);

      CASE Field.NAME OF
        'Icon Condition Items':   ImageFieldName := 'icon_condition_items';
        'Banner Condition Items': ImageFieldName := 'banner_condition_items';
        'Icon Action Items':      ImageFieldName := 'icon_action_items';
        'Banner Action Items':    ImageFieldName := 'banner_action_items';
        'Banner Basket':          ImageFieldName := 'banner_basket';
      END;

      WITH CampaignHeader DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_campaign_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'field',ImageFieldName);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('check_field_content',CurrRec);
        IF GetRec = '' THEN
          Ok := FALSE
        ELSE IF GetRec = '100' THEN
          Ok := TRUE
        ELSE
          IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
            ERROR(GetRec)
          ELSE BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
              LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
               IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
              END;
              LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
              LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
              LogFile.CLOSE;
              IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                MESSAGE(GetRec);
            END ELSE
              ERROR('No Logfile specified');
          END;
        IF Ok <> InitFieldValue THEN BEGIN
          Field.VALUE(Ok);
          RecRef.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteCampaignImage@1000000200(CampaignHeader@1000000001 : Record 5447345;Field@1000000000 : FieldRef);
    VAR
      ImageFieldName@1000000007 : Text[30];
      InitFieldValue@1000000006 : Boolean;
      CurrRec@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1000000003 : File;
      RecRef@1000000002 : RecordRef;
      Success@1000000008 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // Einzelne Webshop Kampagnen-Datei l�schen

      InitFieldValue := Field.VALUE;
      RecRef.GETTABLE(CampaignHeader);
      Field := RecRef.FIELD(Field.NUMBER);

      CASE Field.NAME OF
        'Icon Condition Items':   ImageFieldName := 'icon_condition_items';
        'Banner Condition Items': ImageFieldName := 'banner_condition_items';
        'Icon Action Items':      ImageFieldName := 'icon_action_items';
        'Banner Action Items':    ImageFieldName := 'banner_action_items';
        'Banner Basket':          ImageFieldName := 'banner_basket';
      END;


      WITH CampaignHeader DO BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_campaign_header');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[company]',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'primary[code]',Code);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'data[' + ImageFieldName + ']','');

        WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);
        IF WebshopSetup."Use Update Buffer" THEN
          Success := WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('update',CurrRec,FALSE,TRUE)
        ELSE
          Success := WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',CurrRec);

        IF Success AND InitFieldValue THEN BEGIN
          Field.VALUE(FALSE);
          RecRef.MODIFY(TRUE);
        END;
      END;
    END;

    END;

    END;

OBJECT Codeunit 5447303 Webshop Update All Data
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=27.07.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    TableNo=472;
    OnRun=BEGIN
            // Webshop Gesamtakualisierung Aufruf f�r Projektwarteschlange
            EVALUATE(ParameterPriority,"Parameter String");
            IF ParameterPriority = 'reset' THEN
              ResetJobQueue
            ELSE
              UpdateAllData();
          END;

  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      WebshopSetup@1000000001 : Record 5447300;
      Text001@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Webshop Gesamtaktualisierung';
      Text002@1000000002 : TextConst 'DEU=Webshop kundenspezifische Gesamtaktualisierung';
      WebshopManagement@1000000003 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447301;
      CustWebshopUpdateManagement@1000000004 : Codeunit 5447302;
      WebshopMarketplaceUpdate@1000000011 : Codeunit 5447312;
      CountRecords@1000000007 : Boolean;
      i@1000000006 : Integer;
      WebshopUpdateBuffer@1000000008 : Record 5447314;
      JobQueueEntry@1000000010 : Record 472;
      ParameterPriority@1000000009 : Text[250];
      Text003@1000000012 : TextConst 'DEU=Bitte Verbindungsadresse eintragen;ENU=Please fill out the http address';

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllData@1000000049();
    BEGIN
      WebshopSetup.GET;
      IF WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector" <> '' THEN BEGIN

        WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('','',TRUE,FALSE);

        // Kundenspezifische Aktualisierung Gesamtdaten
        CustWebshopUpdateManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Text002);
        CountRecords := TRUE;
        FOR i := 1 TO 2 DO BEGIN
          CustUpdate;
          CountRecords := FALSE;
        END;
        CustWebshopUpdateManagement.StateIndicatorClose();


        // Allgemeine Aktualisierung Gesamtdaten
        WebshopUpdateManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Text001);
        CountRecords := TRUE;
        FOR i := 1 TO 2 DO BEGIN
          Update;
          CountRecords := FALSE;
        END;
        WebshopUpdateManagement.StateIndicatorClose();

        // Marketplace Aktualisierung Gesamtdaten
        IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace" THEN BEGIN
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.StateIndicatorOpen(Text001);
          CountRecords := TRUE;
          FOR i := 1 TO 2 DO BEGIN
            MarketplaceUpdate;
            CountRecords := FALSE;
          END;
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.StateIndicatorClose();
        END;

        // Datum Aktualisierung Bewegungsdaten in Webshop-Setup setzten
        WebshopSetup."Last Update All Data" := CURRENTDATETIME;
        IF NOT GUIALLOWED THEN
          WebshopSetup."Last Auto Update All Data" := CURRENTDATETIME;
        WebshopSetup.MODIFY;

        WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('','',TRUE,FALSE);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF GUIALLOWED THEN
          ERROR(Text003);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE Update@1000000002();
    BEGIN
      // Allgemeine Aktualisierung
      WebshopSetup.GET;
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllWebshopSetup(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllShop(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllLanguage(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllCountry(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllShippingOptions(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllPaymentOptions(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllVATPostingSetup(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Coupons" THEN BEGIN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.GetAllCouponLine(CountRecords);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllCouponHeader(CountRecords);
      END;
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Webshop Item" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllWebshopItem(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Customizations" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllItemCustomizations(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Webshop Category" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllCategory(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Webshop Category" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllWebshopItemCategory(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Active Customer" OR WebshopSetup."Update All Customer" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllCustomer(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Salesperson" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSalesperson(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Sales Line Discount" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSalesLineDiscount(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Invoice Discount" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllInvoiceDiscount(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Sales Order" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSalesOrder(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Sales Invoice" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSalesInvoiceHeader(CountRecords);
      IF (WebshopSetup."Update Sales Shipment") AND (NOT WebshopSetup."Complete Marketplace Orders") THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSalesShipmentHeader(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Cr.Memo Headers" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSalesCrMemoHeader(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Return Orders" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllReturnOrders(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Attributes" THEN BEGIN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllAttribute(CountRecords);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllAttributeOption(CountRecords);
      END;
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Subscription Orders" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.GetAllSubscriptionCustomerLink(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Subscription Headers" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSubscriptionHeader(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Subscr. Seq. Steps" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSubscSeqStep(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Subscr. Item Links" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSubscItemLink(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Subscr. Cust. Links" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdAllSubscCustLink(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Permissions" THEN BEGIN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllPermissionGroup(CountRecords);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllPermGroupLink(CountRecords);
      END;
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Segments to Newsletter" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.NewsletterSyncAllSegment(CountRecords);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CustUpdate@1000000003();
    BEGIN
      // Kundenspezifische Aktualisierung
    END;

    PROCEDURE MarketplaceUpdate@1000000001();
    BEGIN
      // Marketplace aktualisierung
      WebshopSetup.GET;
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace" THEN BEGIN
        IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace Products" THEN
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.UpdateMarketplaceItems(CountRecords);
        IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace Price" THEN
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.UpdateMarketplacePrice(CountRecords);
        IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace Inventory" THEN
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.UpdateMarketplaceInventory(CountRecords);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ResetJobQueue@1000000000();
    BEGIN
      JobQueueEntry.SETFILTER("Object ID to Run",'5447303');
      JobQueueEntry.SETFILTER(Status,'%1',JobQueueEntry.Status::Error);
      IF JobQueueEntry.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        JobQueueEntry.Status := JobQueueEntry.Status::Ready;
        JobQueueEntry.MODIFY(TRUE);
        COMMIT;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }

OBJECT Codeunit 5447304 Webshop Update Dynamic Data
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=27.07.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    TableNo=472;
    OnRun=BEGIN
            // Start der Aktualisierung �ber Projektwartschlange
            EVALUATE(ParameterPriority,"Parameter String");
            IF ParameterPriority = 'reset' THEN
              ResetJobQueue
            ELSE
              UpdateDynamicData;
          END;

  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      WebshopSetup@1000000000 : Record 5447300;
      WebshopManagement@1000000002 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447301;
      CustWebshopUpdateManagement@1000000005 : Codeunit 5447302;
      WebshopMarketplaceUpdate@1000000011 : Codeunit 5447312;
      CountRecords@1000000004 : Boolean;
      i@1000000003 : Integer;
      Text001@1000000007 : TextConst 'DEU=Webshop Aktualisierung Bewegungsdaten';
      Text002@1000000006 : TextConst 'DEU=Webshop kundenspezifische Aktualisierung Bewegungsdaten';
      WebshopUpdateBuffer@1000000008 : Record 5447314;
      JobQueueEntry@1000000009 : Record 472;
      ParameterPriority@1000000010 : Text[250];
      Text003@1000000012 : TextConst 'DEU=Bitte Verbindungsadresse eintragen;ENU=Please fill out the http address';

    PROCEDURE UpdateDynamicData@1000000049();
    BEGIN
      WebshopSetup.GET;

      IF WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector" <> '' THEN BEGIN

        WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('','',TRUE,FALSE);

        // Kundenspezifische Aktualisierung Bewegungsdaten
        CustWebshopUpdateManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Text002);
        CountRecords := TRUE;
        FOR i := 1 TO 2 DO BEGIN
          CustUpdate;
          CountRecords := FALSE;
        END;
        CustWebshopUpdateManagement.StateIndicatorClose();


        // Allgemeine Aktualisierung Bewegungsdaten
        WebshopUpdateManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Text001);
        CountRecords := TRUE;
        FOR i := 1 TO 2 DO BEGIN
          Update;
          CountRecords := FALSE;
        END;
        WebshopUpdateManagement.StateIndicatorClose();


        // Marketplace Aktualisierung Bewegungsdaten
        IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace" THEN BEGIN
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.StateIndicatorOpen(Text001);
          CountRecords := TRUE;
          FOR i := 1 TO 2 DO BEGIN
            MarketplaceUpdate;
            CountRecords := FALSE;
          END;
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.StateIndicatorClose();
        END;


        // Datum Aktualisierung Bewegungsdaten in Webshop-Setup setzten
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        WebshopSetup."Last Update Dynamic Data" := CURRENTDATETIME;
        IF NOT GUIALLOWED THEN
          WebshopSetup."Last Auto Update Dynamic Data" := CURRENTDATETIME;
        WebshopSetup.MODIFY;

        WebshopUpdateBuffer.SendRequest('','',TRUE,FALSE);

      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF GUIALLOWED THEN
          ERROR(Text003);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE Update@1000000002();
    BEGIN
      // Aktualisierung Bewegungsdaten

      IF WebshopSetup."Update Orders" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.GetAllSalesHeader(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Get Return Orders" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.GetAllReturnOrder(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.ProcessAllInvoiceOnlinePayment(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.ProcessAllCrMemoOnlinePayment(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.ClearAllInvoiceOnlinePayment(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.ClearAllCrMemoOnlinePayment(CountRecords);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.GetAllCouponLine(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Sales Order" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSalesOrder(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Item Inventory" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllWebshopItemInventory(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Std. Prices" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllWebshopItemStdPrices(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Item Inventory" AND WebshopSetup."Update Item Variant" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllItemVariantInventory(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Sales Invoice" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.GetAllInvoiceRequest(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Sales Shipment" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.GetAllShipmentRequest(CountRecords);
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Comments" THEN BEGIN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.GetAllItemComments(CountRecords);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllWebshopItemComment(CountRecords);
      END;

      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllReturnReason(CountRecords);

      IF WebshopSetup."Create Subscription Orders" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.ManageSubscriptions(CountRecords);

      IF NOT GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SendAllOrderMail2(CountRecords);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SendAllOrderMail3(CountRecords);
        WebshopUpdateManagement.SendAllOrderMail4(CountRecords);
      END;

      IF (WebshopSetup."Update Sales Shipment") AND (WebshopSetup."Complete Marketplace Orders") THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateAllSalesShipmentHeader(CountRecords);

      IF WebshopSetup."Update Newsletter Customers" THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.TransferNewsletter(CountRecords);
      IF NOT CountRecords THEN
        WebshopUpdateManagement.GetAndSaveLastSolrUpdateDT();
    END;

    PROCEDURE CustUpdate@1000000003();
    BEGIN
      // Kundenspezifische Aktualisierung Bewegungsdaten
    END;

    PROCEDURE MarketplaceUpdate@1000000001();
    BEGIN
      // Marketplace aktualisierung
      WebshopSetup.GET;
      IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace" THEN BEGIN
        IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace Price" THEN
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.UpdateMarketplacePrice(CountRecords);
        IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace Inventory" THEN
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.UpdateMarketplaceInventory(CountRecords);
        IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace Orders" THEN
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.UpdateMarketplaceOrders(CountRecords);
        IF WebshopSetup."Complete Marketplace Orders" THEN
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.UpdateMarketplaceOrderState(CountRecords);
        IF WebshopSetup."Update Marketplace Errors" THEN BEGIN
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.GetAllMarketplaceErrors(CountRecords);
          WebshopMarketplaceUpdate.GetAllMarketplaceItemErrors(CountRecords);
        END;
      END;
    END;

      IF JobQueueEntry.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        JobQueueEntry.Status := JobQueueEntry.Status::Ready;
        JobQueueEntry.MODIFY(TRUE);
        COMMIT;
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {

    PROCEDURE ShowWebform@1000000029();
    VAR
      Text001@1000000000 : TextConst 'DEU=Webshop Webform;ENU=Webshop Webform';
    BEGIN
      WebshopSetup.GET;
      RANDOMIZE();

      Request :=
        STRSUBSTNO('%1webforms/webform.php?legacy=1&pass=%2&company=%3',
          WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector",
          WebshopSetup."HTTP Password",
          WebshopManagement.URLEncode(COMPANYNAME));
      Request +=
        STRSUBSTNO('&rand=%1&nav_lang=%2&rtc=FALSE',
          RANDOM(2000),
          GLOBALLANGUAGE);

      //Textedit
      IF WebformType = WebformType::TextModul THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&code=%1&webform=TextModul&webformtype=Editor',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(RecordCode));
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::ItemDesc THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&item_no=%3&line_no=%4&webform=ItemDescription&webformtype=Editor',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ItemNo),
            LineNo);
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::CategoryDesc THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&line_no=%3&type=%4&webform=CategoryDesc&webformtype=Editor',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            LineNo,
            FORMAT(DescriptionType,0,'<Standard Format,2>'));
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::ShippingOption THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&line_no=%3&webform=ShippingOption&webformtype=Editor',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            LineNo);
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::PaymentOption THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&line_no=%3&webform=PaymentOption&webformtype=Editor',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            LineNo);
      END;

      //Marketplace

      IF WebformType = WebformType::Marketplace THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&webform=Marketplace&webformtype=Marketplace&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&marketplace_type=%3&parent_id=%4' +
                     '&root_only%5',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(FORMAT(MarketplaceType)),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(FORMAT(ParentId)),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(FORMAT(RootOnly)),
            FORMAT(AttachmentNo));
        SaveRequested := TRUE;
      END;

      //Upload
      IF WebformType = WebformType::TxtModAttac THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&text_module_code=%1&webform=TxtModAttac&webformtype=Upload&shop_code=%2&attachment_no=%3',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(RecordCode),
            WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code",
            FORMAT(AttachmentNo));
        SaveRequested := TRUE;
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::ItemFile THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&webform=ItemFile&webformtype=Upload&multiupload=1&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&item_no=%3&type=%4&line_no=%5',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            ItemNo,
            FORMAT(ItemFileType,0,'<Standard Format,2>'),
            LineNo);
        SaveRequested := TRUE;
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::AttributeIcon THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&webform=AttributeIcon&webformtype=Upload&code=%1&attribute_code=%2',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(RecordCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(AttributeCode));
        SaveRequested := TRUE;
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::CouponBackground THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&webform=CouponBackground&webformtype=Upload&shop_code=%1&code=%2&no=%3',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            BackgroundNo);
        SaveRequested := TRUE;
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::CategoryImage THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&webform=CategoryImage&webformtype=Upload&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&line_no=%3&type=%4',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            LineNo,
            FORMAT(AttachmentType,0,'<Standard Format,2>'));
        SaveRequested := TRUE;
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::Placeholder THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&webform=Placeholder&webformtype=Upload&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode));
        SaveRequested := TRUE;
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::CampaignImages THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&webform=CampaignImages&webformtype=Upload&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&code=%3&field=%4',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(RecordCode),
            WebFieldName);
        SaveRequested := TRUE;
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::Collection THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&webformtype=Collection&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode));
        SaveRequested := TRUE;
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::ShippingOptionIcon THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&line_no=%3&webform=ShippingOption&webformtype=Upload',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            LineNo);
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::PaymentOptionIcon THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&shop_code=%1&language_code=%2&line_no=%3&webform=PaymentOption&webformtype=Upload',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(ShopCode),
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(LanguageCode),
            LineNo);
      END;

      IF WebformType = WebformType::SalesPerson THEN BEGIN
        Request +=
          STRSUBSTNO('&sales_person_code=%1&webform=SalesPerson&webformtype=Upload',
            WebshopManagement.URLEncode(RecordCode));
      END;

      IF ISCLEAR(DynamicCommerceShopControl) THEN
        CREATE(DynamicCommerceShopControl);
        DynamicCommerceShopControl.setSize(800,560);
        DynamicCommerceShopControl.setTitle(Text001 + ' - ' + RecordCode);
        DynamicCommerceShopControl.setMaximizeable(FALSE);
        DynamicCommerceShopControl.LoadUrl(Request);
    END;
