    PROCEDURE IdentifyServer@1000000063();
    VAR
      URLString@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      Servername@1000000001 : Text[50];
      i@1000000002 : Integer;
      text001@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=Ihre lokalen Einstellungen haben sich ge�ndert oder Sie versuchen den Webshop von einem anderen System aus zu aktualisieren. Setzen Sie die Systemeigenschaften in der Webshop Einrichtung zur�ck oder kontaktieren Sie Ihren Systemadministrator.';
    BEGIN
      // Sicherheitsfunktion vor dem Verbinden

      IF NOT ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
        WebshopSetup.GET;
        URLString := CONTEXTURL;
        i := STRPOS(URLString, 'servername=');
        URLString := DELSTR(URLString,1,i+10);
        i := STRPOS(URLString,'&');
        Servername := COPYSTR(DELSTR(URLString,i),1,50);

        IF (WebshopSetup.Servername <> '') AND (WebshopSetup.Companyname <> '') THEN BEGIN
          IF (Servername <> WebshopSetup.Servername) OR (COMPANYNAME <> WebshopSetup.Companyname) THEN
            ERROR(text001);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopSetup.Servername := Servername;
          WebshopSetup.Companyname := COMPANYNAME;
          WebshopSetup.MODIFY;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE AddVar@1000000004(VAR Data@1000000000 : Text[1024];VarName@1000000001 : Text[100];VarValue@1000000002 : Variant);
    VAR
      VarValueInteger@1000000003 : Integer;
      VarValueDecimal@1000000004 : Decimal;
      VarValueText@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      VarValueDate@1000000006 : Date;
      VarValueDateTime@1000000007 : DateTime;
      VarValueTime@1000000008 : Time;
      VarValueBoolean@1000000009 : Boolean;
      TempStr@1000000010 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Variable einem zu sendenden Datensatz hinzuf�gen

      IF VarValue.ISBOOLEAN THEN BEGIN
        VarValueBoolean := VarValue;
        IF VarValueBoolean THEN
          VarValueText := '1'
        ELSE
          VarValueText := '0';
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + VarValueText + '&';
      END;

      IF VarValue.ISCODE OR VarValue.ISTEXT THEN BEGIN
        VarValueText := VarValue;
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + URLEncode(VarValueText) + '&';
      END;

      IF VarValue.ISINTEGER OR VarValue.ISOPTION THEN BEGIN
        VarValueInteger := VarValue;
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + FORMAT(VarValueInteger) + '&';
      END;

      IF VarValue.ISDECIMAL THEN BEGIN
        VarValueDecimal := VarValue;
        TempStr := CONVERTSTR(FORMAT(VarValueDecimal,0,'<Precision,2:2><Standard Format,1>'),',','.');
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + TempStr + '&';
      END;

      IF VarValue.ISDATE THEN BEGIN
        VarValueDate := VarValue;
        IF VarValueDate <> 0D THEN BEGIN
          VarValueText := FORMAT(VarValueDate,0,'<Year4>-<Month,2>-<Day,2>');
        END ELSE BEGIN
          VarValueText := 'NULL';
        END;
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + VarValueText + '&';
      END;

      IF VarValue.ISTIME THEN BEGIN
        VarValueTime := VarValue;
        Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=' + FORMAT(VarValueTime) + '&';
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE AddDTVar@1000000009(VAR Data@1000000000 : Text[1024];VarName@1000000001 : Text[100];VarValue@1000000002 : DateTime);
    VAR
      VarValueInteger@1000000003 : Integer;
      VarValueDecimal@1000000004 : Decimal;
      VarValueText@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      VarValueDate@1000000006 : Date;
      VarValueDateTime@1000000007 : DateTime;
      VarValueTime@1000000008 : Time;
      VarValueBoolean@1000000009 : Boolean;
      TempStr@1000000010 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Datetime Variable einem zu sendenden Datensatz hinzuf�gen

      Data := Data + ConvertText(VarName,TRUE) + '=';
      Data := Data + FORMAT(VarValue,0,'<Year4>-<Month,2>-<Day,2> <Hours24,2>:<Minutes,2>:<Seconds,2>') + '&';
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendRequest@1000000015(Action@1000000001 : Text[100];Data@1000000002 : Text[1024]) Response : Boolean;
    VAR
      Request@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      Error200@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Verbindung zum Webshop konnte nicht aufgebaut werden\Aktion: %1';
      Error300@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Keine Antwort vom Webshop erhalten\Aktion: %1';
      BigTextResponse@1000000008 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1000000009 : File;
      Error400@1000000013 : TextConst 'DEU=Fehlerhafte Antwort vom Webshop erhalten\Aktion: %1\Antwort: %2';
      Log001@1000000010 : TextConst 'DEU=### SendRequest: %1 - %2 ###';
      Log002@1000000011 : TextConst 'DEU="--> "';
      Log003@1000000012 : TextConst 'DEU="<-- "';
      LogResponse@1000000016 : Text[1024];
      i@1000000015 : Integer;
      ResponseText@1000000017 : Text[1024];
      HTTPRequest@1000 : DotNet "'Microsoft.MSXML, Version=8.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a'.MSXML.XMLHTTPRequestClass";
      Counter1@1001 : Integer;
      Counter2@1002 : Integer;
      ReqReadyState@1003 : Integer;
      SleepDuration@1004 : Duration;
      SleepUntilDateTime@1005 : DateTime;
      DataSent@1006 : Boolean;
      ResponseAsText@1007 : Text[1024];
      LogResponseText@1008 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      // Datensatz senden (Antwort TRUE/FALSE)

      WebshopSetup.GET;
      Request := WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector" + 'navconnect.php?action=' + Action;
      IF WebshopSetup."Log Active" THEN BEGIN
        LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
        LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
        IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
          LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File")
        ELSE
          LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File");
        LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
        LogFile.WRITE(STRSUBSTNO(Log001,Action,CURRENTDATETIME));
        IF Data <> '' THEN
          LogFile.WRITE(Log002 + COPYSTR(Data,1,1020));
        LogFile.CLOSE;
      END;
      Data := Data + 'pass=' + WebshopSetup."HTTP Password";
      //CREATE(HTTPRequest);
      //ResponseText := SendRequestHelp(Request,Data);
      HTTPRequest := HTTPRequest.XMLHTTPRequestClass;

      HTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,TRUE,'','');
      HTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
      HTTPRequest.ToString();
      HTTPRequest.send(Data);

      Counter1 := 0;
      SleepDuration := 25;   //25 Millisekunden Wartezeit
      WHILE (HTTPRequest.readyState<>4) AND (Counter1<41) DO BEGIN
        ReqReadyState := HTTPRequest.readyState;
        Counter2 := 0;
        IF (Counter1=40) AND (ReqReadyState<>4) THEN
          ERROR('Could not send Data. Request: '+Request+'; Data: '+Data+' Ready State'+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.readyState));
        IF (ReqReadyState=0) THEN BEGIN
          SleepUntilDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME + SleepDuration;
          WHILE (CURRENTDATETIME <= SleepUntilDateTime) AND (ReqReadyState<>4) DO BEGIN
            Counter2 += 1;
          END;
          SleepUntilDateTime := 0DT;
            HTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,TRUE,'','');
          HTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
          HTTPRequest.ToString();
          HTTPRequest.send(Data);
          ReqReadyState := HTTPRequest.readyState;
        END;
        SleepUntilDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME + SleepDuration;
        WHILE (CURRENTDATETIME <= SleepUntilDateTime) AND (ReqReadyState<>4) DO BEGIN
          Counter2 += 1;
        END;
        SleepUntilDateTime := 0DT;
        Counter1 += 1;
      END;
      Counter1 := 0;
      WHILE (ReqReadyState<>4) AND (Counter1<41) DO BEGIN

        Counter2 := 0;

          //Abbrechen beim 41. Durchlauf
        IF (Counter1 = 40) THEN BEGIN
          HTTPRequest.abort();
          ERROR('FALSE:WebRequest-Error. HTTP-Request konnte nicht abgeschlossen werden. ReadyState: '+FORMAT(ReqReadyState));
        END;

        IF (ReqReadyState=0) THEN BEGIN
          HTTPRequest.abort();

          SleepUntilDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME + SleepDuration;
          WHILE (CURRENTDATETIME <= SleepUntilDateTime) DO BEGIN
            Counter2 += 1;
          END;
          SleepUntilDateTime := 0DT;

          HTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,TRUE,'','');
          HTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
          HTTPRequest.ToString();
          HTTPRequest.send(Data);
          DataSent := TRUE;
          ReqReadyState := HTTPRequest.readyState;
        END;

      //Wenn die send-methode aufgerufen wurde, aber noch keine Informationen empfangen wurden und mindestens 5 bereits stattfanden
        IF (ReqReadyState=2) AND (Counter1>4) THEN BEGIN
          SleepUntilDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME + SleepDuration;
          WHILE (CURRENTDATETIME < SleepUntilDateTime) DO BEGIN
          END;
          SleepUntilDateTime := 0DT;
        END;

      //Wenn die send-methode aufgerufen wurde, aber noch keine Informationen empfangen wurden und mindestens 5 bereits stattfanden
        IF (ReqReadyState=3) THEN BEGIN
          SleepUntilDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME + SleepDuration;
          WHILE (CURRENTDATETIME < SleepUntilDateTime) DO BEGIN
          END;
          SleepUntilDateTime := 0DT;
        END;

        ReqReadyState := HTTPRequest.readyState;
        Counter1 += 1;
      END;



      Counter2 := 0;
      //Vollst�ndige Antwort
      IF (HTTPRequest.status <> 200) THEN BEGIN

          ERROR('FALSE:WebRequest-Error. HTTP-Status Not 200: '+FORMAT(HTTPRequest.status)+' Code: '+HTTPRequest.statusText);

      END;



      ResponseAsText := HTTPRequest.responseText;
        IF WebshopSetup."Log Active" THEN BEGIN
          LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
          LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
          IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
            LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File")
          ELSE
            LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File");
          LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
          i := 1;
          REPEAT
            LogResponseText := COPYSTR(ResponseAsText,i,1020);
            LogFile.WRITE(Log003 + LogResponseText);
            i += 1020;
          UNTIL i > STRLEN(ResponseAsText);

          LogFile.WRITE('');
          LogFile.CLOSE;
        END;

        IF ResponseAsText = '100' THEN BEGIN
          Response := TRUE;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF ResponseText <> '' THEN
            ERROR(Error400,Action,ResponseText)
          ELSE
             ERROR(Error300,Action);
        END;


      IF Response <> TRUE THEN BEGIN
        IF STRLEN(ResponseText) >0 THEN
          ERROR(COPYSTR(ResponseText,7))
      END ELSE
        Response := TRUE;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SendXMLRequest@1000000013(Action@1000000001 : Text[100];XMLFileName@1000000000 : Text[1024]) Response : Boolean;
    VAR
      StreamSupported@1000000009 : Boolean;
      Request@1000000008 : Text[1024];
      InS@1000000007 : InStream;
      Resp@1000000006 : Text[1024];
      NoofSign@1000000005 : Integer;
      ErrorFile@1000000004 : File;
      ErrorFileName@1000000003 : Text[500];
      ErrorText@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      Error200@1000000014 : TextConst 'DEU=Verbindung zum Webserver konnte nicht aufgebaut werden';
      Error300@1000000013 : TextConst 'DEU=Keine Antwort vom Webserver erhalten';
      XMLDoc@1000 : DotNet "'Microsoft.MSXML, Version=8.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a'.MSXML.DOMDocument";
      WinHTTPRequest@1001 : DotNet "'Microsoft.MSXML, Version=8.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a'.MSXML.XMLHTTPRequestClass";
      Node@1002 : DotNet "'Microsoft.MSXML, Version=8.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a'.MSXML.IXMLDOMNode";
    BEGIN
      // XML senden (Antwort TRUE/FALSE)
      {
      StreamSupported := TRUE;
      ErrorFileName := ENVIRON('temp') + '\NavConnectError.log';

      WebshopSetup.GET;
      Request := WebshopSetup."HTTP Adress Connector" + 'navconnect.php?action=' + Action;
      //CREATE(WinHTTPRequest);
      WinHTTPRequest := WinHTTPRequest.XMLHTTPRequestClass;
      WinHTTPRequest.open('POST',Request,FALSE,'','');
      WinHTTPRequest.setRequestHeader('Content-Type','application/x-www-form-urlencoded');

      //CLEAR(XMLDoc);
      //CREATE(XMLDoc);
      XMLDoc.async := FALSE;
      XMLDoc.load(XMLFileName);
      Node := XMLDoc.selectSingleNode('//file/pass');
      //IF NOT ISCLEAR(Node) THEN
        Node.text(WebshopSetup."HTTP Password");

      WinHTTPRequest.send(XMLDoc);
      //IF WinHTTPRequest.waitForResponse(10000) THEN BEGIN

        CLEAR(XMLDoc);

        IF (StreamSupported) THEN BEGIN
          InS := WinHTTPRequest.responseStream;
          NoofSign := InS.READ(Resp); //Read first 1024 Sign
        END ELSE BEGIN
          NoofSign := 0;
          Resp := WinHTTPRequest.responseText;
        END;

        IF Resp = '100' THEN BEGIN
          Response := TRUE
        END ELSE BEGIN
          Response := FALSE;

          IF Resp <> '' THEN BEGIN
            ErrorText := Resp;

            IF (StreamSupported) THEN BEGIN
              IF EXISTS(ErrorFileName) THEN
                ERASE(ErrorFileName);
              ErrorFile.CREATE(ErrorFileName);
              ErrorFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
              ErrorFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
              WHILE (NoofSign > 0) DO BEGIN
                ErrorFile.WRITE(Resp);
                NoofSign := InS.READ(Resp);
              END;
              ErrorFile.CLOSE;
            END;

            ERROR(ErrorText);
          END ELSE
            ERROR(Error300);
        END;
      //END ELSE BEGIN
      //  ERROR(Error200);
      //  Response := FALSE;
      //END;
      //CLEAR(WinHTTPRequest);
      }
    END;

      PrinterSelection@1000000013 : Record 78;
      Language@1000000009 : Record 5447302;
      NAVLanguage@1000000010 : Record 8;
      SalesHeader@1000000005 : Record 36;
      SalesInvoiceHeader@1000000002 : Record 112;
      SalesShipmentHeader@1000000008 : Record 110;
      NotDeletePrinterSelection@1000000015 : Boolean;
      i@1000000006 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // PDF-Datei aus Beleg (Verkaufsauftrag, Verkaufsrechnung, Verkaufslieferung) erstellen (PDF Writer - bioPDF erforderlich)
      {
      IF Language.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode) AND (DocumentNo <> '') THEN BEGIN

        Language.TESTFIELD("PDF Temp File Path");

        // Systemsprache f�r mehrsprachige Belege umstellen
        NAVLanguage.GET(LanguageCode);
        IF NAVLanguage."Windows Language ID" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          CurrLanguageId := GLOBALLANGUAGE();
          GLOBALLANGUAGE(NAVLanguage."Windows Language ID");
        END;

        // Beleg ausw�hlen und Dateinamen festlegen
        CASE Type OF
          Type::SalesOrder: BEGIN
            Reportid := Language."Report PDF Order Confirmation";
            IF SalesHeader.GET(SalesHeader."Document Type"::Order,DocumentNo) THEN
              FileName := FORMAT(SalesHeader."Document Type") + ' ' + DocumentNo + '.pdf';
          END;
          Type::SalesInvoice: BEGIN
            Reportid := Language."Report PDF Invoice";
            IF SalesInvoiceHeader.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              FileName := SalesInvoiceHeader.TABLECAPTION + ' ' + DocumentNo + '.pdf';
          END;
          Type::SalesShipment: BEGIN
            Reportid := Language."Report PDF Shipment";
            IF SalesShipmentHeader.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              FileName := SalesShipmentHeader.TABLECAPTION + ' ' + DocumentNo + '.pdf';
          END;
        END;

        // PDF Einstellungen festlegen
        IF FileName <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF EXISTS(Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName) THEN
            ERASE(Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName);
          IF ISCLEAR(bioPDF) THEN
            CREATE(bioPDF);
          bioPDF.Init;
          bioPDF.SetValue('Output',Language."PDF Temp File Path" + FileName);
          bioPDF.SetValue('ConfirmOverwrite','no');
          bioPDF.SetValue('ShowSaveAS','never');
          bioPDF.SetValue('ShowSettings','never');
          bioPDF.SetValue('ShowPDF','no');
          bioPDF.SetValue('ShowProgress','no');
          bioPDF.SetValue('ShowProgressFinished','no');
          bioPDF.SetValue('DisableOptionDialog','yes');
          bioPDF.SetValue('SuppressErrors','yes');
          bioPDF.SetValue('Title',FileName);
          bioPDF.SetValue('Subject',FileName);
          IF (Language."PDF Background File" <> '') AND EXISTS(Language."PDF Background File") THEN
            bioPDF.SetValue('Superimpose',Language."PDF Background File");
          bioPDF.WriteSettings();

          // Standard-Drucker festlegen
          IF PrinterSelection.GET(USERID,Reportid) THEN BEGIN
            NotDeletePrinterSelection := TRUE;
            IF PrinterSelection."Printer Name" <> 'PDF Writer - bioPDF' THEN
              CurrPrinterName := PrinterSelection."Printer Name";
          END ELSE BEGIN
            PrinterSelection.INIT;
            PrinterSelection."User ID" := USERID;
            PrinterSelection."Report ID" := Reportid;
            PrinterSelection.INSERT;
          END;
          PrinterSelection."Printer Name" := 'PDF Writer - bioPDF';
          PrinterSelection.MODIFY;

          // PDF drucken
          CASE Type OF
            Type::SalesOrder: BEGIN
              SalesHeader.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
              SalesHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesHeader."No.");
              REPORT.RUN(Reportid,FALSE,FALSE,SalesHeader);
            END;
            Type::SalesInvoice: BEGIN
              SalesInvoiceHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesInvoiceHeader."No.");
              REPORT.RUN(Reportid,FALSE,FALSE,SalesInvoiceHeader);
            END;
            Type::SalesShipment: BEGIN
              SalesShipmentHeader.SETRANGE("No.",SalesShipmentHeader."No.");
              REPORT.RUN(Reportid,FALSE,FALSE,SalesShipmentHeader);
            END;
          END;

          // PDF auf Webserver laden
          i := 1;
          WHILE i < 100 DO BEGIN
            IF EXISTS(Language."PDF Temp File Path"+FileName) THEN BEGIN
              UploadAttachment(Language."PDF Temp File Path"+FileName,FileName);
              i := 100;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              SLEEP(500);
              i += 1;
            END;
          END;

          // Lokale PDF-Datei l�schen
          IF EXISTS(Language."PDF Temp File Path"+FileName) THEN
            ERASE(Language."PDF Temp File Path"+FileName);

          // Standard-Drucker zur�cksetzten
          IF NotDeletePrinterSelection THEN BEGIN
            IF CurrPrinterName <> '' THEN BEGIN
              PrinterSelection."Printer Name" := CurrPrinterName;
              PrinterSelection.MODIFY;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            PrinterSelection.DELETE;
          END;

          // Sprache zur�cksetzten
          IF CurrLanguageId <> 0 THEN
            GLOBALLANGUAGE(CurrLanguageId);

        END;
      END;
      }
    END;

    PROCEDURE UploadAttachment@1000000067(Filename@1000000008 : Text[200];ServerFileName@1000000010 : Text[200]);
    VAR
      InputFile@1000000000 : File;
      ConvertedFile@1000000001 : File;
      InputFileInStr@1000000002 : InStream;
      ConvertedFileOutStr@1000000004 : OutStream;
      ConvertedFileInStr@1000000003 : InStream;
      Data@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      i@1000000006 : Integer;
      TempData@1000000007 : Text[600];
      FileRecord@1000000009 : Text[1024];
      XMLHeader@1000000012 : Text[1024];
      TempFilePath@1000000011 : Text[200];
    BEGIN
      // Datei in tempor�res Webverzeichnis laden
      {
      InputFile.OPEN(Filename);
      InputFile.CREATEINSTREAM(InputFileInStr);
      ConvertedFile.CREATETEMPFILE;
      ConvertedFile.CREATEOUTSTREAM(ConvertedFileOutStr);
      Base64EncodeStream(InputFileInStr,ConvertedFileOutStr);
      ConvertedFile.CREATEINSTREAM(ConvertedFileInStr);

      CLEAR(FileRecord);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'filename',ServerFileName);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'action','create');
      SendRequest('upload_attachment',FileRecord);

      WHILE NOT (ConvertedFileInStr.EOS) DO BEGIN
        CLEAR(FileRecord);
        CLEAR(Data);
        FOR i := 1 TO 8 DO BEGIN
          ConvertedFileInStr.READTEXT(TempData);
          Data += TempData;
        END;
        AddVar(FileRecord,'filename',ServerFileName);
        AddVar(FileRecord,'action','write');
        AddVar(FileRecord,'filedata',Data);
        SendRequest('upload_attachment',FileRecord);
      END;

      CLEAR(FileRecord);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'filename',ServerFileName);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'action','convert');
      SendRequest('upload_attachment',FileRecord);
      }
      {
      // Neue Funktion von TH implementiert: FK 19.06.2012

      InputFile.OPEN(Filename);
      InputFile.CREATEINSTREAM(InputFileInStr);

      TempFilePath := ENVIRON('temp') + '\' + 'nav_attachment_' + FORMAT(RANDOM(10000000)) + '.xml';
      ConvertedFile.CREATE(TempFilePath);

      ConvertedFile.CREATEOUTSTREAM(ConvertedFileOutStr);

      ConvertedFileOutStr.WRITETEXT('<?xml version="1.0" ?><file><pass></pass>');
      ConvertedFileOutStr.WRITETEXT('<action>write</action>');
      ConvertedFileOutStr.WRITETEXT('<filename>'+ServerFileName+'</filename>' + '<filedata>');

      Base64EncodeStream(InputFileInStr,ConvertedFileOutStr);

      ConvertedFileOutStr.WRITETEXT('</filedata></file>');
      ConvertedFile.CREATEINSTREAM(ConvertedFileInStr);
      ConvertedFile.CLOSE;

      CLEAR(FileRecord);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'filename',ServerFileName);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'action','create');
      SendRequest('upload_attachment',FileRecord);


      SendXMLRequest('upload_attachment',TempFilePath);

      CLEAR(FileRecord);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'filename',ServerFileName);
      AddVar(FileRecord,'action','convert');
      SendRequest('upload_attachment',FileRecord);

      IF EXISTS(TempFilePath) THEN
        ERASE(TempFilePath);
      }
    END;

    PROCEDURE "--- Allgemein ---"@1000000008();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetItemLink@1000000074(WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304) Link : Text[250];
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      Shop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000004 : Record 5447302;
      Customer@1000000003 : Record 18;
      CustomerNo@1000000005 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      // Link auf einen Webshopartikel erzeugen

      WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
        Shop.GET("Shop Code");
        Language.GET("Shop Code","Language Code");
        Shop.TESTFIELD("Frontend Link");
        IF (Shop."Shop Typ" = Shop."Shop Typ"::b2b) OR (Shop."Shop Typ" = Shop."Shop Typ"::salesperson) THEN BEGIN
          Shop.TESTFIELD("Base Login Customer");
          Customer.GET(Shop."Base Login Customer");
          Customer.TESTFIELD("Webshop Active");
          CustomerNo := Customer."No.";
        END;
        WebshopItem.TESTFIELD("Last Date Updated");
        WebshopItem.TESTFIELD(Active);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',"Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',"Language Code");
        IF CustomerNo <> '' THEN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'customer_no',CustomerNo);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'item_no',"Item No.");
        Link := Shop."Frontend Link" + GetRequest('get_item_link',CurrRec);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertSalesLine@1000000041(SalesHeader@1000000001 : Record 36;VAR SalesLine@1000000009 : Record 37;Typ@1000000003 : 'Text,Item,G/L Account,Resource';No@1000000005 : Code[20];VariantCode@1000000007 : Code[10];Quantity@1000000006 : Decimal;UnitPrice@1000000008 : Decimal;Description@1000000002 : Text[50];IsCouponItem@1000000010 : Boolean;LineDiscount@1000000011 : Decimal;UnitOfMeasure@1000000012 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      LastSalesLine@1000000000 : Record 37;
      NewLineNo@1000000004 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // Einf�gen einer Verkaufszeile

      LastSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
      LastSalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesHeader."No.");
      IF LastSalesLine.FINDLAST THEN
        NewLineNo := LastSalesLine."Line No." + 10000
      ELSE
        NewLineNo := 10000;

      SalesLine.INIT;
      SalesLine.VALIDATE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
      SalesLine.VALIDATE("Document No.",SalesHeader."No.");
      SalesLine.VALIDATE("Line No.",NewLineNo);
      SalesLine.INSERT(TRUE);

      CASE Typ OF
        Typ::Text: BEGIN
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Type,SalesLine.Type::" ");
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("No.",No);
          IF Description <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,Description);
        END;
        Typ::Item: BEGIN
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Type,SalesLine.Type::Item);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("No.",No);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit of Measure",UnitOfMeasure);
          IF Description <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,Description);
          IF VariantCode <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Variant Code",VariantCode);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Quantity,Quantity);
          IF UnitPrice <> 0 THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit Price",UnitPrice);
          IF IsCouponItem=TRUE THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Line Discount %",100)
          ELSE IF LineDiscount > 0 THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Line Discount %",LineDiscount);
        END;
        Typ::"G/L Account": BEGIN
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Type,SalesLine.Type::"G/L Account");
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("No.",No);
          IF Description <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,Description);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Quantity,Quantity);
          IF UnitPrice <> 0 THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit Price",UnitPrice);
        END;
        Typ::Resource: BEGIN
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Type,SalesLine.Type::Resource);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE("No.",No);
          IF Description <> '' THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,Description);
          SalesLine.VALIDATE(Quantity,Quantity);
          IF UnitPrice <> 0 THEN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit Price",UnitPrice);
        END;
      END;

      SalesLine.MODIFY(TRUE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CalculateOnlineDiscount@1000000052(SalesHeader@1000000000 : Record 36);
    VAR
      Shop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      ShopLanguage@1000000006 : Record 5447302;
      SalesLine@1000000001 : Record 37;
      StandardText@1000000003 : Record 7;
      OnlineDiscountAmount@1000000004 : Decimal;
      OrderAmount@1000000005 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      // Online-Rabatt berechnen
      {
      OnlineDiscountAmount := 0;
      OrderAmount := 0;
      WebshopSetup.GET;
      IF Shop.GET(SalesHeader."Webshop Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
        IF ShopLanguage.GET(SalesHeader."Webshop Shop Code",SalesHeader."Webshop Language Code") THEN BEGIN
          SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",SalesHeader."Document Type");
          SalesLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",SalesHeader."No.");
          SalesLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLine.Type::Item);
          IF SalesLine.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            IF SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice" <> 0 THEN
              OrderAmount += SalesLine."Line Amount" / SalesLine.Quantity * SalesLine."Qty. to Invoice";
          UNTIL SalesLine.NEXT = 0;
          SalesLine.SETRANGE(Type,SalesLine.Type::"G/L Account");
          SalesLine.SETRANGE("No.",Shop."Online Discount");
          SalesLine.SETFILTER("Outstanding Quantity",'<>0');
          StandardText.GET(ShopLanguage."Online Discount Text");
          OnlineDiscountAmount := -ROUND(OrderAmount/100*Shop."Weborder Typ");
          IF SalesLine.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Description,STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,Shop."Weborder Typ"));
            SalesLine.VALIDATE(Quantity,1);
            SalesLine.VALIDATE("Unit Price",OnlineDiscountAmount);
            SalesLine.MODIFY(TRUE);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF Shop."Weborder Typ" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
              InsertSalesLine(SalesHeader,SalesLine,2,Shop."Online Discount",1,OnlineDiscountAmount,
                              STRSUBSTNO(StandardText.Description,Shop."Weborder Typ"));
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      }
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetWebshopItemModified@1000000061(ItemNo@1000000000 : Code[20];UpdateAllWebshopItem@1000000006 : Boolean;UpdateParentItem@1000000004 : Boolean;UpdateVariants@1000000005 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000001 : Record 5447304;
      WebshopItemLink@1000000002 : Record 5447308;
      WebshopItem2@1000000003 : Record 5447304;
      WebshopItem3@1000000007 : Record 5447304;
    BEGIN
      // �nderungsdatum f�r Artikelaktualisierung setzten

      WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Item No.",ItemNo);

      IF NOT UpdateAllWebshopItem THEN BEGIN
        IF WebshopItem.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Shop Code",WebshopItem."Shop Code");
          WebshopItem.SETRANGE("Language Code",WebshopItem."Language Code");
        END;
      END;

      IF WebshopItem.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN REPEAT
        WebshopItem3.GET(WebshopItem."Shop Code",WebshopItem."Language Code",WebshopItem."Item No.");
        WebshopItem3."Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME();
        WebshopItem3."Last User Modified" := USERID;
        WebshopItem3.MODIFY;

        // Zugeh�rige Varianten Aktualisieren
        IF UpdateVariants THEN BEGIN
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.",WebshopItem."Item No.");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",WebshopItem."Shop Code");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,WebshopItemLink.Typ::Variant);
          IF WebshopItemLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            WebshopItem2.SETRANGE("Shop Code",WebshopItemLink."Shop Code");
            WebshopItem2.SETRANGE("Item No.",WebshopItemLink."Linked Item No.");
            IF WebshopItem2.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
              WebshopItem2."Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME();
              WebshopItem2."Last User Modified" := USERID;
              WebshopItem2.MODIFY;
            UNTIL WebshopItem2.NEXT = 0;
          UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;
        END;

        // Zugeh�rigen Mutterartikel aktualisieren
        IF UpdateParentItem THEN BEGIN
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Item No.");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE(Typ,WebshopItemLink.Typ::Variant);
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Shop Code",WebshopItem."Shop Code");
          WebshopItemLink.SETRANGE("Linked Item No.",WebshopItem."Item No.");
          IF WebshopItemLink.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            WebshopItem2.SETRANGE("Shop Code",WebshopItemLink."Shop Code");
            WebshopItem2.SETRANGE("Item No.",WebshopItemLink."Item No.");
            IF WebshopItem2.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
              WebshopItem2."Last Date Modified" := CURRENTDATETIME();
              WebshopItem2."Last User Modified" := USERID;
              WebshopItem2.MODIFY;
            UNTIL WebshopItem2.NEXT = 0;
          UNTIL WebshopItemLink.NEXT = 0;
        END;

      UNTIL WebshopItem.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorOpen@1000000014(Title@1000 : Text[250]);
    VAR
      MaxTextLength@1001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(MainTableCount);
        NoOfMainTable := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord2 := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord3 := 0;
        NoOfRecords := 0;
        RecordNo := 0;
        NewProgress := 0;
        OldProgress := 0;
        NewProgress2 := 0;
        OldProgress2 := 0;
        NextRecordNo := 0;
        Title := DELCHR(Title,'>',' .');
        Title := DELCHR(Title,'<',' ');
        IF Title = '' THEN
          Title := Text55000;
        MaxTextLength := STRLEN(Text55001);
        IF STRLEN(Text55002) > MaxTextLength THEN
          MaxTextLength := STRLEN(Text55002);
        IF STRLEN(Text55003) > MaxTextLength THEN
          MaxTextLength := STRLEN(Text55003);
        IF STRLEN(Text55004) > MaxTextLength THEN
          MaxTextLength := STRLEN(Text55004);
        Window.OPEN(
          Title +'...\\' +
          PADSTR(Text55001,MaxTextLength,' ') + '  @1@@@@@@@@@@@@@\' +
          PADSTR(Text55002,MaxTextLength,' ') + '  #2#######################\' +
          PADSTR(Text55003,MaxTextLength,' ') + '  #3######\' +
          PADSTR(Text55004,MaxTextLength,' ') + '  @4@@@@@@@@@@@@@');
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorNewTable@75(TableCaption@1000 : Text[250];RecordCount@1001 : Integer);
    VAR
      i@1002 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        IF MainRecord THEN BEGIN
          NoOfMainRecord2 := NoOfMainRecord2 + NoOfMainRecord3;
          MainRecord := FALSE;
        END;
        Window.UPDATE(2,TableCaption);
        Window.UPDATE(3,RecordCount);
        Window.UPDATE(4,0);
        OldProgress := 0;
        RecordNo := 0;
        NoOfRecords := RecordCount;
        NextRecordNo := 0;
        IF (NoOfMainRecord <> 0) AND (RecordCount <> 0) THEN
          FOR i := 1 TO NoOfMainTable DO
            IF MainTableCaption[i] = TableCaption THEN BEGIN
              MainRecord := TRUE;
              NoOfMainRecord3 := MainTableCount[i];
              MainTableCaption[i] := '';
              EXIT;
            END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorUpdate@76();
    BEGIN
      IF GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        IF RecordNo >= NoOfRecords THEN
          EXIT;
        RecordNo := RecordNo + 1;
        IF RecordNo < NextRecordNo THEN
          EXIT;
        NextRecordNo := NextRecordNo + ROUND(NoOfRecords / 100,1);
        NewProgress := ROUND(RecordNo / NoOfRecords * 100,1);
        IF NewProgress <> OldProgress THEN BEGIN
          OldProgress := NewProgress;
          Window.UPDATE(4,NewProgress * 100);
          IF MainRecord THEN BEGIN
            NewProgress2 := ROUND((NoOfMainRecord2 + RecordNo) / NoOfMainRecord * 100,1);
            IF (NewProgress2 > OldProgress2) AND (NewProgress2 <= 100) THEN BEGIN
              OldProgress2 := NewProgress2;
              Window.UPDATE(1,NewProgress2 * 100);
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorRegisterTable@77(TableCaption@1000 : Text[250];RecordCount@1001 : Integer);
    BEGIN
      IF GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        NoOfMainTable := NoOfMainTable + 1;
        MainTableCaption[NoOfMainTable] := TableCaption;
        MainTableCount[NoOfMainTable] := RecordCount;
        NoOfMainRecord := NoOfMainRecord + RecordCount;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorClose@1();
    BEGIN
      IF GUIALLOWED THEN BEGIN
        Window.CLOSE;
        CLEAR(MainTableCount);
        NoOfMainTable := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord2 := 0;
        NoOfMainRecord3 := 0;
        NoOfRecords := 0;
        RecordNo := 0;
        NewProgress := 0;
        OldProgress := 0;
        NewProgress2 := 0;
        OldProgress2 := 0;
        NextRecordNo := 0;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE "--- Stingkonvertierung ---"@1000000005();

    END;

    PROCEDURE Explode@1000000042(VAR TextRecord@1000000000 : Text[1024];Delimiter@1000000003 : Text[30]) TextField : Text[250];
    VAR
      Pos@1000000001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      Pos := STRPOS(TextRecord,Delimiter);
      IF Pos <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        TextField := (COPYSTR(TextRecord,1,Pos-1));
        TextRecord := (COPYSTR(TextRecord,Pos+STRLEN(Delimiter)));
      END ELSE BEGIN
        TextField := TextRecord;
        TextRecord := '';
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE URLEncode@1000000003(ASCIIText@1000000000 : Text[1024]) HTTPText : Text[1024];
    VAR
      Char@1000000001 : Char;
      CharInt@1000000002 : Integer;
      i@1000000003 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      CLEAR(HTTPText);
      //ASCIIText := ConvertText(ASCIIText,TRUE);
      FOR i := 1 TO STRLEN(ASCIIText) DO BEGIN
        Char := ASCIIText[i];
        CharInt := Char;
        HTTPText := HTTPText + URLEncodeCharset(CharInt);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE InitCharAscii@7(VAR Text@1002 : Text[191]);
    VAR
      Counter@1000 : Integer;
      Char@1001 : Char;
    BEGIN
      Text := '';

      FOR Counter := 1 TO 191 DO BEGIN
        Char := Counter + 64;
        Text[Counter] := Char;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ConvertText@12(Text@1001 : Text[1024];DosToWindows@1004 : Boolean) ConvertedText : Text[1024];
    VAR
      Ascii@1003 : Text[191];
      Ansi@1000 : Text[191];
    BEGIN
      InitCharAscii(Ascii);
      InitCharAnsi(Ansi);

      IF DosToWindows THEN
        ConvertedText := CONVERTSTR(Text,Ascii,Ansi)
      ELSE
        ConvertedText := CONVERTSTR(Text,Ansi,Ascii);
    END;

    PROCEDURE Base64EncodeStream@1112400001(VAR inStr@1112400000 : InStream;VAR outStr@1112400001 : OutStream);
    VAR
      b@1112400002 : Binary[3];
      nRead@1112400003 : Integer;
      c@1112400004 : ARRAY [4] OF Char;
      nWritten@1112400005 : Integer;
      txt64Chars@1112400006 : Text[64];
    BEGIN
      {
      txt64Chars := 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ';
      txt64Chars += 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
      txt64Chars += '0123456789+/';

      WHILE NOT inStr.EOS DO BEGIN
        // read up to 3 bytes...
        nRead := inStr.READ(b);

        // zero out bytes not read...
        IF nRead < 3 THEN
          b[3] := 0;
        IF nRead < 2 THEN
          b[2] := 0;

        // c[1] is high 6 bits of b[1]...
        c[1] := b[1] DIV 4;

        // c[2] is low 2 bits of b[1] and high 4 bits of b[2]...
        c[2] := ((b[1] MOD 4) * 16) + (b[2] DIV 16);

        // c[3] is low 4 bits of b[2] and high 2 bits of b[3]...
        c[3] := ((b[2] MOD 16) * 4) + (b[3] DIV 64);

        // c[4] is low 6 bits of b[3]...
        c[4] := b[3] MOD 64;

        // convert c[1..4] to the Base64Encoded char...
        //c[1] := txt64Chars[c[1] + 1];
        //c[2] := txt64Chars[c[2] + 1];
        //IF nRead > 1 THEN
        //  c[3] := txt64Chars[c[3] + 1]
        //ELSE
        //  c[3] := '=';
        //IF nRead > 2 THEN
        //  c[4] := txt64Chars[c[4] + 1]
        //ELSE
        //  c[4] := '=';

        // write 'em out...
        outStr.WRITE(c[1]);
        outStr.WRITE(c[2]);
        outStr.WRITE(c[3]);
        outStr.WRITE(c[4]);
        nWritten += 1;

        // write a cr/lf every 72 chars...
        IF nWritten = 18 THEN BEGIN
          c[1] := 13;
          outStr.WRITE(c[1]);
          c[1] := 10;
          outStr.WRITE(c[1]);
          nWritten := 0;
        END;
      END;

      // write the trailing cr/lf...
      IF nWritten <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        c[1] := 13;
        outStr.WRITE(c[1]);
        c[1] := 10;
        outStr.WRITE(c[1]);
      END;
      }
    END;

    PROCEDURE Base64DecodeStream@1112400002(VAR inStr@1112400001 : InStream;VAR outStr@1112400000 : OutStream);
    VAR
      b@1112400002 : Binary[3];
      c@1112400003 : ARRAY [4] OF Char;
      idxChar@1112400004 : Integer;
      inPos@1112400005 : Integer;
      nEqualSigns@1112400006 : Integer;
      txt64Chars@1112400007 : Text[64];
      Text000@1112400008 : TextConst 'DEU=Ung�ltiges Zeichen "%1" an Position %2 festgestellt.;ENU=Invalid character ''''%1'''' encountered at position %2.';
      Text001@1112400009 : TextConst 'DEU=Unerwarteten End-Of-Stream an Position %1 festgestellt.;ENU=Unexpected end-of-stream encountered at position %1.';
    BEGIN
      {
      txt64Chars := 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ';
      txt64Chars += 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
      txt64Chars += '0123456789+/';

      inPos := 0;
      WHILE NOT inStr.EOS DO BEGIN
        // read 1st char (ignore leading white space)...
        inStr.READ(c[1],1);
        inPos += 1;
        WHILE (c[1] IN [9, 10, 13, 32]) AND (NOT inStr.EOS) DO BEGIN
          inStr.READ(c[1],1);
          inPos += 1;
        END;

        IF NOT inStr.EOS THEN BEGIN
          // check 1st char...
          idxChar := STRPOS(txt64Chars, FORMAT(c[1]));
          IF idxChar = 0 THEN
            ERROR(Text000, FORMAT(c[1]), inPos);
          c[1] := idxChar - 1;

          // get/check 2nd char...
          inStr.READ(c[2],1);
          inPos += 1;
          idxChar := STRPOS(txt64Chars, FORMAT(c[2]));
          IF idxChar = 0 THEN
            ERROR(Text000, FORMAT(c[2]), inPos);
          c[2] := idxChar - 1;

          // get/check 3rd char...
          IF inStr.EOS THEN
            ERROR(Text001, inPos);
          inStr.READ(c[3],1);
          inPos += 1;
          nEqualSigns := 0;
          IF c[3] = '=' THEN BEGIN
            c[3] := 0;
            nEqualSigns += 1;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            idxChar := STRPOS(txt64Chars, FORMAT(c[3]));
            IF idxChar = 0 THEN
              ERROR(Text000, FORMAT(c[3]), inPos);
            c[3] := idxChar - 1;
          END;

          // get/check 4th char...
          IF inStr.EOS THEN
            ERROR(Text001, inPos);
          inStr.READ(c[4],1);
          inPos += 1;
          IF (nEqualSigns > 0) AND (c[4] <> '=') THEN
            ERROR(Text000, FORMAT(c[4]), inPos);
          IF c[4] = '=' THEN BEGIN
            c[4] := 0;
            nEqualSigns += 1;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            idxChar := STRPOS(txt64Chars, FORMAT(c[4]));
            IF idxChar = 0 THEN
              ERROR(Text000, FORMAT(c[4]), inPos);
            c[4] := idxChar - 1;
          END;

          // b[1] is c[1] and bits(5,6) of c[2]...
          b[1] := (c[1] * 4) + (c[2] DIV 16);
          outStr.WRITE(b[1]);

          // b[2] is low 4 bits of c[2] and bits(3..6) of c[3]...
          IF nEqualSigns < 2 THEN BEGIN
            b[2] := ((c[2] MOD 16) * 16) + (c[3] DIV 4);
            outStr.WRITE(b[2]);
          END;

          // b[3] is low 2 bits of c[3] and the 6 bits in c[4]...
          IF nEqualSigns = 0 THEN BEGIN
            b[3] := ((c[3] MOD 4) * 64) + c[4];
            outStr.WRITE(b[3]);
          END;
        END;
      END;
      }
    END;

    PROCEDURE EncodeFF@1108800000(Text@1108800000 : Text[1024]) : Text[1024];
    VAR
      I@1108800001 : Integer;
      J@1108800003 : Integer;
      NewText@1108800002 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      FOR I := 1 TO STRLEN(Text) DO
        IF I MOD 2 <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          J += 1;
          NewText[J] := 255 - Text[I];
        END;
      FOR I := STRLEN(Text) DOWNTO 2 DO
        IF I MOD 2 = 0 THEN BEGIN
          J += 1;
          NewText[J] := 255 - Text[I];
        END;
      EXIT(NewText);
    END;

    PROCEDURE DecodeFF@1108800001(Text@1108800000 : Text[1024]) : Text[1024];
    VAR
      I@1108800001 : Integer;
      J@1108800003 : Integer;
      NewText@1108800002 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      FOR I := 1 TO STRLEN(Text) DO
        IF I MOD 2 <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          J += 1;
          NewText[I] := 255 - Text[J];
        END;
      FOR I := STRLEN(Text) DOWNTO 2 DO
        IF I MOD 2 = 0 THEN BEGIN
          J += 1;
          NewText[I] := 255 - Text[J];
        END;
      EXIT(NewText);
    END;

    PROCEDURE CodeHasSpecialChars@1000000048(Text@1000000000 : Code[20]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      SpecialChars@1000000001 : ARRAY [25] OF Code[1];
      i@1000000002 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      SpecialChars[1] := '�';
      SpecialChars[2] := '�';
      SpecialChars[3] := '�';
      SpecialChars[4] := '�';
      SpecialChars[5] := '�';
      SpecialChars[6] := '\';
      SpecialChars[7] := '/';
      SpecialChars[8] := '$';
      SpecialChars[9] := '�';
      SpecialChars[10] := '!';
      SpecialChars[11] := '(';
      SpecialChars[12] := ')';
      SpecialChars[13] := '%';
      SpecialChars[14] := '<';
      SpecialChars[15] := '>';
      SpecialChars[16] := '|';
      SpecialChars[17] := '.';
      SpecialChars[18] := ',';
      SpecialChars[19] := ';';
      SpecialChars[20] := '#';
      SpecialChars[21] := '?';
      SpecialChars[22] := '&';

      IF STRPOS(Text,' ') <> 0 THEN
        EXIT(TRUE);

      FOR i := 1 TO ARRAYLEN(SpecialChars) DO BEGIN
        IF STRPOS(Text,SpecialChars[i]) <> 0 THEN
          EXIT(TRUE);
      END;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }

OBJECT Codeunit 5447308 Webshop Frontend Link
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=30.06.17;
    Time=16:00:00;
    Version List=MYSW14.00;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    OnRun=VAR
            Customer@1000000000 : Record 18;
            Salesperson@1000000001 : Record 13;
          BEGIN
            // �ffnet den Webshop mit dem in der Einrichtung hinterlegten Debitor

            WebshopSetup.GET;

            IF STRLEN(gShopCode)>0 THEN
              Shop.GET(gShopCode)
            ELSE BEGIN
              DefaultNumber := 1;

              IF Shop.COUNT > 1 THEN BEGIN
                i := 1;
                IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                  IF i <> 1 THEN
                    OptionString += ',';
                  OptionString += Shop.Description;
                  IF Shop.Code = WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code" THEN
                    DefaultNumber := i;
                  i += 1;
                UNTIL Shop.NEXT = 0;
                SelectedOption := DIALOG.STRMENU(OptionString,DefaultNumber,Text001);
              END ELSE BEGIN
                SelectedOption := 1;
              END;
              IF SelectedOption = 1 THEN
                Shop.FINDFIRST
              ELSE IF SelectedOption > 1 THEN BEGIN
                Shop.RESET;
                i := 1;
                IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                  i += 1;
                UNTIL (SelectedOption=i) OR (Shop.NEXT = 0);
              END ELSE
                EXIT;
            END;


            Shop.TESTFIELD("Frontend Link");
            CASE Shop."Shop Typ" OF
              Shop."Shop Typ"::b2b: BEGIN
                Shop.TESTFIELD("Base Login Customer");
                Customer.GET(Shop."Base Login Customer");
                WebshopUpdateManagement.OpenCustomerLogin(Customer);
              END;
              Shop."Shop Typ"::b2c: BEGIN
                HYPERLINK(Shop."Frontend Link");
              END;
              Shop."Shop Typ"::salesperson: BEGIN
                Shop.TESTFIELD("Default Salesperson Code");
                Salesperson.GET(Shop."Default Salesperson Code");
                WebshopUpdateManagement.OpenSalespersonLogin(Salesperson);
              END;
              Shop."Shop Typ"::catalog: BEGIN
                HYPERLINK(Shop."Frontend Link");
              END;
            END;
          END;

  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      WebshopManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000008 : Codeunit 5447301;
      WebshopSetup@1000000001 : Record 5447300;
      Shop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      OptionString@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      Text001@1000000004 : TextConst 'DEU=Welchen Webshop m�chten Sie �ffnen?';
      DefaultNumber@1000000005 : Integer;
      SelectedOption@1000000006 : Integer;
      i@1000000007 : Integer;
      gShopCode@1000 : Code[10];

    PROCEDURE SetShopCode@1(ShopCode@1000 : Code[10]);
    BEGIN
      gShopCode := ShopCode;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce - dynamic commerce GmbH - www.dc-solution.de ---
    }
    END.
  }
}

    VAR
      WebshopManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopSetup@1000000001 : Record 5447300;

      Text002@1000000003 : TextConst 'DEU=Ebay_;ENU=Ebay_';

    PROCEDURE "--- WebshopManagement ---"@1000000040();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE AddVar@1000000041(VAR Data@1000000002 : Text[1024];VarName@1000000001 : Text[100];VarValue@1000000000 : Variant);
    BEGIN
      WebshopManagement.AddVar(Data,VarName,VarValue);
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorOpen@1000000014(Title@1000 : Text[250]);
    BEGIN
      WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorOpen(Title);
    END;

    PROCEDURE StateIndicatorClose@1();
    BEGIN
      WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorClose();
    END;

    PROCEDURE "--- Webshop Marketplace ---"@1000000011();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateMarketplace@1000000001(WebshopShopCode@1000000003 : Code[10];WebshopLanguageCode@1000000000 : Code[10];Operation@1000000004 : Text[20];ParamKeys@1000000005 : ARRAY [20] OF Text[20];ParamValues@1000000001 : ARRAY [20] OF Text[20]);
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      I@1000000006 : Integer;
      WebshopShop@1000000007 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopLanguage@1000000008 : Record 5447302;
    BEGIN

      IF (WebshopShop.GET(WebshopShopCode)) AND (WebshopLanguage.GET(WebshopShopCode,WebshopLanguageCode)) THEN BEGIN
        AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',WebshopLanguage."Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',WebshopLanguage.Code);
        IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'marketplace_type','1');
        IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'marketplace_type','2');

        AddVar(CurrRec,'operation',Operation);

        FOR I := 1 TO ARRAYLEN(ParamKeys) DO BEGIN
          IF (ParamKeys[I] <> '') THEN BEGIN
            AddVar(CurrRec,'parameter[' + ParamKeys[I] + ']',ParamValues[I]);
          END;
        END;

        WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update_marketplace_queue',CurrRec);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateMarketplaceItems@1000000006(CountRecords@1000000003 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WebshopShop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopLanguage@1000000001 : Record 5447302;
      CurrRec@1000000000 : Text[1024];
      ParamKeys@1000000004 : ARRAY [20] OF Text[20];
      ParamValues@1000000005 : ARRAY [20] OF Text[20];
    BEGIN
      IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
        WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(WebshopShop.TABLECAPTION,WebshopShop.COUNT)
      END ELSE BEGIN
        WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(WebshopShop.TABLECAPTION,WebshopShop.COUNT);

        WebshopShop.SETFILTER(WebshopShop."Shop Typ",'%1',WebshopShop."Shop Typ"::marketplace);
        IF WebshopShop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
          IF (WebshopShop."AWS Access Key ID" <> '') OR (WebshopShop."Ebay App Name" <> '') THEN BEGIN
            WebshopLanguage.SETFILTER(WebshopLanguage."Shop Code",WebshopShop.Code);
            IF WebshopLanguage.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
              UpdateMarketplace(WebshopShop.Code,WebshopLanguage.Code,'send_products',ParamKeys,ParamValues);
            UNTIL WebshopLanguage.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        UNTIL WebshopShop.NEXT = 0;

        WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateMarketplaceItem@1000000003(ParamWebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304);
    VAR
      WebshopItem@1000000001 : Record 5447304;
      WebshopShop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopLanguage@1000000003 : Record 5447302;
      ParamKeys@1000000005 : ARRAY [20] OF Text[20];
      ParamValues@1000000004 : ARRAY [20] OF Text[20];
    BEGIN
      WebshopItem.GET(ParamWebshopItem."Shop Code",ParamWebshopItem."Language Code",ParamWebshopItem."Item No.");

      WebshopShop.SETFILTER(WebshopShop."Shop Typ",'%1',WebshopShop."Shop Typ"::marketplace);
      IF WebshopShop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
        IF (WebshopShop.Code = WebshopItem."Shop Code") OR (WebshopShop."Use Items from Shop Code" = WebshopItem."Shop Code") THEN BEGIN
          ParamKeys[1] := 'item_no';
          EVALUATE(ParamValues[1],WebshopItem."Item No.");
          ParamKeys[2] := 'function';

          ParamValues[2] := 'update_products';
          UpdateMarketplace(WebshopShop.Code,WebshopItem."Language Code",'send_products',ParamKeys,ParamValues);

          ParamValues[2] := 'update_inventory';
          UpdateMarketplace(WebshopShop.Code,WebshopItem."Language Code",'send_products',ParamKeys,ParamValues);

          ParamValues[2] := 'update_price';
          UpdateMarketplace(WebshopShop.Code,WebshopItem."Language Code",'send_products',ParamKeys,ParamValues);

          IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN BEGIN
            ParamValues[2] := 'update_images';
            UpdateMarketplace(WebshopShop.Code,WebshopItem."Language Code",'send_products',ParamKeys,ParamValues);

            ParamValues[2] := 'update_relationship';
            UpdateMarketplace(WebshopShop.Code,WebshopItem."Language Code",'send_products',ParamKeys,ParamValues);
          END;
        END;
      UNTIL WebshopShop.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetItemLink@1000000074(WebshopItem@1000000000 : Record 5447304) Link : Text[250];
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      Shop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000004 : Record 5447302;
      Customer@1000000003 : Record 18;
      CustomerNo@1000000005 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      // Link auf einen Webshopartikel erzeugen

      WITH WebshopItem DO BEGIN
        Shop.GET("Shop Code");
        Language.GET("Shop Code","Language Code");
        Shop.TESTFIELD("Frontend Link");
        WebshopItem.TESTFIELD("Last Date Updated");
        WebshopItem.TESTFIELD(Active);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',"Shop Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',"Language Code");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'item_no',"Item No.");
        Link := Shop."Frontend Link" + WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_item_link_ebay',CurrRec);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckMarketplaceErrors@1000000005(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Marktplatz Fehler pr�fen;ENU=Check Marketplace Errors';
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      ErrorCount@1000000002 : Integer;
      GetResponseText@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      ErrorCount2@1000000005 : Integer;
      I@1000000006 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      ErrorCount := 0;

      AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_errors');
      AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
      GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
      EVALUATE(ErrorCount,GetResponseText);

      CLEAR(CurrRec);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_item');
      AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[marketplace_error]','NOTEMPTY');
      GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
      EVALUATE(ErrorCount2,GetResponseText);

      ErrorCount := ErrorCount + ErrorCount2;


      IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
        WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,ErrorCount)
      END ELSE BEGIN
        WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,ErrorCount);
        CLEAR(CurrRec);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
        WebshopManagement.SendRequest('send_marketplace_errors',CurrRec);
        FOR I := 1 TO ErrorCount DO
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DelMarketplaceSubCategories@1000000204(ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[30];ShopLanguageCode@1000000001 : Code[10];MarketplaceType@1000000002 : Option;ParentCode@1000000003 : Text[45]) Success : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',ShopCode);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',ShopLanguageCode);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'marketplace_type',MarketplaceType);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'parent_id',ParentCode);
      Success := WebshopManagement.SendRequest('clearMarketplaceCategorySubTreePost',CurrRec);
    END;

    PROCEDURE QueueMktplSubCategoriesFetch@1000000205(ShopCode@1000000003 : Code[30];ShopLanguageCode@1000000002 : Code[10];MarketplaceType@1000000001 : Option;ParentCode@1000000000 : Code[45]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      CurrRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      ResponseText@1000000006 : Text[1024];
      QueueEntryID@1000000005 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      AddVar(CurrRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'shop_code',ShopCode);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'language_code',ShopLanguageCode);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'marketplace_type',MarketplaceType);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'parent_id',ParentCode);
      ResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('queueSubCategoryFetchPost',CurrRec);
      IF EVALUATE(QueueEntryID,ResponseText) THEN BEGIN
        EXIT(QueueEntryID > 0);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckMktplSubCategoriesFetch@1000000206(ShopCode@1000000003 : Code[30];ShopLanguageCode@1000000002 : Code[10];MarketplaceType@1000000001 : Option;ParentCode@1000000000 : Text[45]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      GetRec@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      ResponseText@1000000005 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      AddVar(GetRec,'company',COMPANYNAME);
      AddVar(GetRec,'shop_code',ShopCode);
      AddVar(GetRec,'language_code',ShopLanguageCode);
      AddVar(GetRec,'marketplace_type',MarketplaceType);
      AddVar(GetRec,'parent_id',ParentCode);
      ResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('checkMarketplaceSubcategoriesFetchedPost',GetRec);
      EXIT(ResponseText = '1');
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetAllFetchedMktplCategories@1000000207(ShopCode@1000000000 : Code[30];ShopLanguageCode@1000000007 : Code[10];CountRecords@1000000008 : Boolean;AboveLineNo@1000000011 : Integer);
    VAR
      NoOfRec@1000000006 : Integer;
      GetRec@1000000001 : Text[1024];
      ResponseText@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      i@1000000003 : Integer;
      CategoryFetched@1000000004 : Boolean;
      Error001@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Ein Datensatz f�r eine Marketplace-Kategorie konnte nicht synchronisiert werden.;ENU=A Record for a Marketplace-Category could not be synchronized.';
      Text001@1000000009 : TextConst 'DEU=Ermittelte Marktplatz-Kategorien;ENU=Fetched Marketplace Categories';
      LineNoInterval@1000000010 : Integer;
      Category@1000000012 : Record 5447303;
    BEGIN
      AddVar(GetRec,'table','shop_marketplace_categories');
      IF AboveLineNo > 0 THEN BEGIN
        Category.GET(ShopCode,ShopLanguageCode,AboveLineNo);
        AddVar(GetRec,'filter[parent_category_code]',Category."Search Code");
      END ELSE BEGIN
        AddVar(GetRec,'filter[parent_category_code]','');
      END;
      AddVar(GetRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);

      CLEAR(ResponseText);
      ResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',GetRec);

      IF EVALUATE(NoOfRec,WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',GetRec)) THEN BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfRec)
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //Verschiebe nachfolgende Kategorien
          LineNoInterval := 10000;
          MoveAllCategoriesBelowLineNo(ShopCode,ShopLanguageCode,AboveLineNo,(NoOfRec * LineNoInterval));

          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfRec);
          i := 1;
          WHILE (i <= NoOfRec) DO BEGIN
            CategoryFetched := GetNextFetchedMktplCategory(ShopCode,ShopLanguageCode,AboveLineNo);
            IF CategoryFetched THEN BEGIN
              i += 1;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              ERROR(Error001);
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetNextFetchedMktplCategory@1000000208(ShopCode@1000000013 : Code[30];ShopLanguageCode@1000000014 : Code[10];AboveLineNo@1000000018 : Integer) Success : Boolean;
    VAR
      Delimiter@1000000001 : Text[5];
      SendRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000007 : Text[1024];
      GetResponseText@1000000005 : Text[1024];
      Category@1000000000 : Record 5447303;
      LogFile@1000000004 : File;
      AboveCategoryCode@1000000003 : Text[40];
      RecID@1000000006 : Integer;
      RecMarketplaceType@1000000008 : Option;
      RecCategoryCode@1000000009 : Text[45];
      RecName@1000000012 : Text[45];
      RecLevel@1000000010 : Integer;
      RecParentCode@1000000011 : Text[45];
      RecRootCode@1000000019 : Text[45];
      CategoryInsert@1000000015 : Boolean;
      SetRec@1000000016 : Text[1024];
      NewLineNo@1000000017 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      WITH Category DO BEGIN
        Delimiter := '-;-';
        CLEAR(GetRec);
        CLEAR(SendRec);
        AddVar(SendRec,'table','shop_marketplace_categories');
        AddVar(SendRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(SendRec,'fields','id,marketplace_type,category_code,category_name,' +
               'category_level,parent_category_code,root_category_code');
        IF AboveLineNo > 0 THEN BEGIN
          Category.RESET;
          Category.GET(ShopCode,ShopLanguageCode,AboveLineNo);
          AddVar(SendRec,'filter[parent_category_code]',Category."Search Code");
        END ELSE BEGIN
          AddVar(SendRec,'filter[parent_category_code]','');
        END;

        AddVar(SendRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',SendRec);
        IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          EVALUATE(RecID,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(RecMarketplaceType,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(RecCategoryCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(RecName,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(RecLevel,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(RecParentCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(RecRootCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));

          AboveCategoryCode := Category.GetAboveCodeForParentCode(ShopCode,ShopLanguageCode,RecParentCode);
          Category.RESET;
          NewLineNo := Category.GetNextCategoryLineNo(ShopCode,ShopLanguageCode,AboveCategoryCode);
          Category.INIT;
          VALIDATE(Category."Shop Code",ShopCode);
          VALIDATE(Category."Language Code",ShopLanguageCode);
          VALIDATE(Category.Name,RecName);
          VALIDATE(Category.Level,RecLevel);
          VALIDATE(Category."Search Code",RecCategoryCode);
          VALIDATE(Category."Line No.",NewLineNo);
          IF RecRootCode <> '' THEN
            VALIDATE(Category."Marketplace Root Category Code",RecRootCode)
          ELSE
            VALIDATE(Category."Marketplace Root Category Code",RecCategoryCode);
          CategoryInsert := Category.INSERT;
          IF CategoryInsert THEN BEGIN
              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_marketplace_categories');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',RecID);
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

              //FetchCategoryAttributes(Category,TRUE);

          END;
          COMMIT;
          EXIT(CategoryInsert);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                    ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE MoveAllCategoriesBelowLineNo@1000000129(ShopCode@1000000003 : Code[30];LanguageCode@1000000004 : Code[10];AboveLineNo@1000000000 : Integer;MoveByInterval@1000000001 : Integer);
    VAR
      Category@1000000002 : Record 5447303;
      NewLineNo@1000000005 : Integer;
      LineNoInterval@1000000006 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      Category.SETRANGE("Shop Code",ShopCode);
      Category.SETRANGE("Language Code",LanguageCode);
      Category.SETFILTER("Line No.",'>%1',AboveLineNo);
      Category.ASCENDING(FALSE);
      IF Category.FINDFIRST THEN REPEAT
        NewLineNo := Category."Line No." + MoveByInterval;
        Category.RENAME(Category."Shop Code",Category."Language Code",NewLineNo);
      UNTIL Category.NEXT = 0;
      COMMIT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FetchCategoryAttributes@1000000124(ParamCategory@1000000000 : Record 5447303;CountRecords@1000000015 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Category@1000000001 : Record 5447303;
      CurrRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      Delimiter@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      IsVariation@1000000005 : Boolean;
      Description@1000000006 : Text[250];
      WebshopAttribute@1000000007 : Record 5447320;
      WebshopAttributeLink@1000000008 : Record 5447321;
      WebshopShop@1000000009 : Record 5447301;
      NextWebshopAttribute@1000000010 : Boolean;
      IsRequired@1000000011 : Boolean;
      SetRec@1000000012 : Text[1024];
      AttributeId@1000000013 : Integer;
      AttributeType@1000000014 : Integer;
      Prefix@1000000016 : Text[1024];
      DescriptionCode@1000000017 : Text[30];
      NoOfRec@1000000018 : Integer;
      ResponseText@1000000019 : Text[1024];
    BEGIN
      WebshopShop.GET(ParamCategory."Shop Code");
      CLEAR(SetRec);
      AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_marketplace_variant_attributes');
      IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
        AddVar(SetRec,'filter[category_code]',ParamCategory."Marketplace Root Category Code")
      ELSE IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
        AddVar(SetRec,'filter[category_code]',ParamCategory."Search Code");
      AddVar(SetRec,'filter[marketplace_type]',WebshopShop."Marketplace Type");
      AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',TRUE);
      WebshopManagement.SendRequest('bulk_update',SetRec);

      CLEAR(GetRec);
      AddVar(GetRec,'table','shop_marketplace_variant_attributes');
      IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
        AddVar(GetRec,'filter[category_code]',ParamCategory."Marketplace Root Category Code")
      ELSE IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
        AddVar(GetRec,'filter[category_code]',ParamCategory."Search Code");
      AddVar(GetRec,'filter[marketplace_type]',WebshopShop."Marketplace Type");
      AddVar(GetRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);

      ResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',GetRec);
      EVALUATE(NoOfRec,ResponseText);

      IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
        WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(WebshopAttribute.TABLECAPTION,NoOfRec);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(WebshopAttribute.TABLECAPTION,NoOfRec);
        WITH Category DO REPEAT
          NextWebshopAttribute := FALSE;

          IF Category.GET(ParamCategory."Shop Code",ParamCategory."Language Code",ParamCategory."Line No.") THEN BEGIN
            Delimiter := '-;-';

            CLEAR(CurrRec);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_variant_attributes');
            AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
            AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','id,description,is_variation,is_required');

            IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[category_code]',ParamCategory."Marketplace Root Category Code")
            ELSE IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
              AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[category_code]',ParamCategory."Search Code");

            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[marketplace_type]',WebshopShop."Marketplace Type");
            AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
            GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
            IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
              EVALUATE(AttributeId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(Description,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(IsVariation,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              EVALUATE(IsRequired,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
              CLEAR(WebshopAttribute);
              CLEAR(WebshopAttributeLink);
              WebshopAttribute.RESET;
              IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN BEGIN
                Prefix := Text001;
                AttributeType := 1;
              END ELSE IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN BEGIN
                Prefix := Text002;
                AttributeType := 2;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                Prefix := '';
                AttributeType := 0;
              END;

              DescriptionCode := COPYSTR(Description,1,30-STRLEN(Prefix));
              DescriptionCode := Prefix + DescriptionCode;

              WebshopAttribute.SETRANGE(WebshopAttribute.Code,DescriptionCode);
              IF NOT WebshopAttribute.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                WebshopAttribute.INIT;
                WebshopAttribute.Code := DescriptionCode;
                WebshopAttribute.Description := Description;
                WebshopAttribute."Attribute Type" := AttributeType;
                WebshopAttribute.INSERT;
              END;

              FetchCategoryAttributesValues(Category,WebshopAttribute);

              WebshopAttributeLink.RESET;
              WebshopAttributeLink.SETRANGE(WebshopAttributeLink.Type,WebshopAttributeLink.Type::Category);
              WebshopAttributeLink.SETRANGE(WebshopAttributeLink."Shop Code",Category."Shop Code");
              WebshopAttributeLink.SETRANGE(WebshopAttributeLink."Language Code",Category."Language Code");
              WebshopAttributeLink.SETRANGE(WebshopAttributeLink."Category Line No.",Category."Line No.");
              WebshopAttributeLink.SETRANGE(WebshopAttributeLink."Attribute Code",DescriptionCode);
              IF NOT WebshopAttributeLink.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                WebshopAttributeLink.INIT;
                WebshopAttributeLink.Type := WebshopAttributeLink.Type::Category;
                WebshopAttributeLink."Shop Code" := Category."Shop Code";
                WebshopAttributeLink."Language Code" := Category."Language Code";
                WebshopAttributeLink."Category Line No." := Category."Line No.";
                WebshopAttributeLink."Attribute Code" := DescriptionCode;
                WebshopAttributeLink."Is Variation" := IsVariation;
                WebshopAttributeLink."Is Required" := IsRequired;
                WebshopAttributeLink.INSERT;
              END;
              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_marketplace_variant_attributes');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',AttributeId);
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

              NextWebshopAttribute := TRUE;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL NextWebshopAttribute = FALSE;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FetchCategoryAttributesValues@1000000004(WebshopCategory@1000000015 : Record 5447303;WebshopAttribute@1000000000 : Record 5447320);
    VAR
      WebshopShop@1000000001 : Record 5447301;
      WebshopAttributeOption@1000000010 : Record 5447322;
      CurrRec@1000000002 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      Delimiter@1000000004 : Text[1024];
      IsVariation@1000000005 : Boolean;
      Description@1000000006 : Text[250];
      SetRec@1000000012 : Text[1024];
      NextAttributeValue@1000000007 : Boolean;
      AttributeValueId@1000000008 : Integer;
      AttributeValue@1000000009 : Text[100];
      AttributeDescription@1000000011 : Text[100];
      Prefix@1000000013 : Text[30];
      AttributeDescriptionCode@1000000014 : Text[30];
      AttributeValueCode@1000000016 : Text[30];
    BEGIN

      WebshopShop.GET(WebshopCategory."Shop Code");

      Delimiter := '~|~';

      WITH WebshopAttributeOption DO REPEAT
        NextAttributeValue := FALSE;

        CLEAR(CurrRec);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_variant_attribute_values');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','id,description,value');

        IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[category_code]',WebshopCategory."Marketplace Root Category Code")
        ELSE IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
          AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[category_code]',WebshopCategory."Search Code");

        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[marketplace_type]',WebshopShop."Marketplace Type");
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[description]',WebshopAttribute.Description);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
        IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN

          EVALUATE(AttributeValueId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(AttributeDescription,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(AttributeValue,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));

          IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::Amazon THEN
            Prefix := Text001
          ELSE IF WebshopShop."Marketplace Type" = WebshopShop."Marketplace Type"::eBay THEN
            Prefix := Text002
          ELSE
            Prefix := '';

          //AttributeDescriptionCode := COPYSTR(AttributeDescription,1,30-STRLEN(Prefix));
          //AttributeDescriptionCode := Prefix + AttributeDescriptionCode;

          AttributeValueCode := COPYSTR(AttributeValue,1,30);

          IF NOT GET(WebshopAttribute.Code,AttributeValueCode) THEN BEGIN
            INIT;
            "Attribute Code" := WebshopAttribute.Code;
            Code := AttributeValueCode;
            Description := AttributeDescription;
            Sorting := 0;
            INSERT(TRUE);
          END;

          CLEAR(SetRec);
          AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_marketplace_variant_attribute_values');
          AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',AttributeValueId);
          AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
          WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);

          NextAttributeValue := TRUE;

        END;
      UNTIL NOT NextAttributeValue;
    END;
