    PROCEDURE GetAllMarketplaceErrors@1000000016(CountRecords@1000000000 : Boolean);
    VAR
      Text001@1000000001 : TextConst 'DEU=Marktplatz Fehlermeldungen abrufen';
      NoOfError@1000000002 : Integer;
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      GetResponseText@1000 : Text[1024];
      LogFile@1001 : File;
    BEGIN
      //Alle neuen Marktplatz Fehlermeldungen abrufen

      WebshopSetup.GET;
      AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_errors');
      AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
      AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[errormsg]','NOTEMPTY');
      GetResponseText := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_no_of_records',CurrRec);
      IF EVALUATE(NoOfError,GetResponseText) THEN BEGIN
        IF CountRecords THEN BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorRegisterTable(Text001,NoOfError);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorNewTable(Text001,NoOfError);
          GetMarketplaceError;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
          ERROR(GetResponseText)
        ELSE BEGIN
          IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
            LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
            LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
            IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
             IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
              ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
            END;
            LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
            LogFile.WRITE(GetResponseText);
            LogFile.CLOSE;
            IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
              MESSAGE(GetResponseText);
          END ELSE
            ERROR('No Logfile specified');
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetMarketplaceError@1000000013();
    VAR
      Shop@1000000012 : Record 5447301;
      Language@1000000011 : Record 5447302;
      CurrRec@1000000003 : Text[1024];
      GetRec@1000000020 : Text[1024];
      SetRec@1000000030 : Text[1024];
      Delimiter@1000000006 : Text[1024];
      i@1000000027 : Integer;
      Pos@1000000005 : Integer;
      NextError@1000000001 : Boolean;
      ErrorId@1000000009 : Integer;
      ShopCode@1000000032 : Code[20];
      LanguageCode@1000000033 : Code[10];
      ResetRec@1000000096 : Text[1024];
      Counter@1000000108 : Integer;
      WebshopMarketplaceErrors@1000000000 : Record 5447318;
      MarketplaceType@1000000002 : Integer;
      GOperation@1000000004 : Text[250];
      GParameter@1000000007 : Text[250];
      ErrorMsg@1000000008 : Text[250];
      LogFile@1000000010 : File;
      WebshopMarketplaceErrors2@1000000013 : Record 5447318;
      PrimaryID@1000000014 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //Alle neuen Marktplatz Fehlermeldungen abrufen
      Delimiter := '-;-';

      Counter := 0;
      WITH WebshopMarketplaceErrors DO REPEAT
        NextError := FALSE;

        CLEAR(CurrRec);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'table','shop_marketplace_errors');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'delimiter',Delimiter);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'fields','id,marketplace_type,operation,parameter,errormsg');
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[update_insert]',TRUE);
        AddVar(CurrRec,'filter[errormsg]','NOTEMPTY');
        GetRec := WebshopManagement.GetRequest('get_content',CurrRec);
        IF STRPOS(GetRec,Delimiter) <> 0 THEN BEGIN

          EVALUATE(ErrorId,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(MarketplaceType,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(GOperation,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(GParameter,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ErrorMsg,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));

          RESET;
          IF ErrorId > 0 THEN BEGIN
            INIT;
            WebshopMarketplaceErrors2.RESET;
            IF WebshopMarketplaceErrors2.FINDLAST THEN
              PrimaryID := WebshopMarketplaceErrors2.PRIMARY +1 ELSE
              PrimaryID := 1;
            VALIDATE(PRIMARY,PrimaryID);

            VALIDATE("Error Type","Error Type"::General);
            VALIDATE("Marketplace Type",MarketplaceType);
            VALIDATE(Operation,GOperation);
            VALIDATE(Parameter,GParameter);
            VALIDATE(Errortext,COPYSTR(ErrorMsg,1,250));
            VALIDATE(WebId,ErrorId);
            INSERT(TRUE);

            // Error als �bertragen markieren
            CLEAR(SetRec);
            AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_marketplace_errors');
            AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',ErrorId);
            AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
            WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);
            NextError := TRUE;
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            COMMIT;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              // Error als �bertragen markieren
              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_marketplace_errors');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',ErrorId);
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);
              NextError := TRUE;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT WebshopSetup."Catch & Log WebRequest Errors" THEN
              ERROR(GetRec)
            ELSE BEGIN
              IF STRLEN(WebshopSetup."Log File")>0 THEN BEGIN
                LogFile.TEXTMODE(TRUE);
                LogFile.WRITEMODE(TRUE);
                IF NOT FILE.EXISTS(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN BEGIN
                 IF NOT LogFile.CREATE(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                  ERROR('Could not create logfile at specified location');
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF NOT LogFile.OPEN(WebshopSetup."Log File") THEN
                    ERROR('Could not open existing logfile');
                END;
                LogFile.SEEK(LogFile.LEN);
                LogFile.WRITE(GetRec);
                LogFile.CLOSE;
                IF GUIALLOWED AND WebshopSetup."Show Errors as Messages" THEN
                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END;
        END;
      UNTIL NOT NextError;
    END;

          EVALUATE(GErrorCode,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));
          EVALUATE(ErrorMsg,WebshopManagement.Explode(GetRec,Delimiter));

          RESET;
          IF ErrorId > 0 THEN BEGIN
            // Einrichtung ermitteln
            WebshopSetup.GET;
            Shop.GET(ShopCode);
            Language.GET(ShopCode,LanguageCode);
            INIT;
            WebshopMarketplaceErrors2.RESET;
            IF WebshopMarketplaceErrors2.FINDLAST THEN
              PrimaryId := WebshopMarketplaceErrors2.PRIMARY +1 ELSE
              PrimaryId := 1;
            VALIDATE(PRIMARY,PrimaryId);
            VALIDATE("Error Type","Error Type"::Item);

            VALIDATE("Shop Code",Shop.Code);
            VALIDATE("Language Code",Language.Code);
            VALIDATE("Item No.",ItemNo);
            VALIDATE(Operation,GOperation);
            VALIDATE(Errorcode,GErrorCode);
            VALIDATE(Errortext,COPYSTR(ErrorMsg,1,250));
            VALIDATE(WebId,ErrorId);
            INSERT(TRUE);

            // Error als �bertragen markieren

            AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_marketplace_item_errors');

            AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);

            NextError := TRUE;
            WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            COMMIT;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              // Error als �bertragen markieren
              CLEAR(SetRec);
              AddVar(SetRec,'table','shop_marketplace_item_errors');
              AddVar(SetRec,'primary[id]',ErrorId);
              AddVar(SetRec,'data[update_insert]',FALSE);
              WebshopManagement.SendRequest('update',SetRec);
              NextError := TRUE;
              WebshopManagement.StateIndicatorUpdate;
            END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF GetRec<>'0' THEN BEGIN

                  MESSAGE(GetRec);
              END ELSE
                ERROR('No Logfile specified');
            END;
          END;
        END;
      UNTIL NOT NextError;
    END;
